# 01 — Round 1 原始论断(longevity-evidence)

## L6 — The measurement and endpoint layer: how would we know if any of this worked?

### L6-01 **[承重]**

A 2025 scoping review that screened 3,780 publications found no established regulatory framework anywhere for drugs targeting aging, and states plainly that FDA, EMA and Health Canada do not classify aging as a disease. Caliber: scoping review of published literature and regulatory documents, not a survey of regulators; funded by Canadian Frailty Network; authors declare no competing interests.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Muscedere J, Shorey CL, Duque G, Kim P, Lorbergs AL, McGlory C, Merchant RA, Newman JC, Rolland Y, Vellas B. Advancing geroscience research – A scoping review of regulatory environments for gerotherapeutics. J Nutr Health Aging. 2025;29(9):100637.
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12310402/
- 逐字引:> Regulatory bodies such as the FDA, the EMA and Health Canada do not classify aging as a disease, which complicates the approval process for therapies targeting aging.
- 利益相关:Funded by the Canadian Frailty Network; authors declare no competing financial interests.

### L6-02 **[承重]**

TAME (Targeting Aging with Metformin) has no ClinicalTrials.gov registration as of 2026-07-28. A ClinicalTrials.gov API v2 query for the term "Targeting Aging with Metformin" returns totalCount = 1, and the single record is NCT05651620, The CALERIE Legacy Study (Tufts University, n=216, start 2023-04-06) — a false positive. A second formulation ("TAME metformin aging") returns the same single record. No Albert Einstein College of Medicine / Barzilai TAME record exists. This is the flagship trial of the geroscience field, announced in 2015-2016 and routinely described in press as FDA-approved and underway.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:ClinicalTrials.gov API v2, query.term="Targeting Aging with Metformin", countTotal=true, run 2026-07-28.
- URL:https://clinicaltrials.gov/api/v2/studies?query.term=%22Targeting%20Aging%20with%20Metformin%22&countTotal=true&pageSize=10&fields=NCTId%7CBriefTitle%7COverallStatus%7CEnrollmentCount%7CStartDate%7CLeadSponsorName
- 逐字引:> totalCount: 1 — NCT05651620, "The CALERIE™ Legacy Study", Tufts University, enrollment 216, start 2023-04-06.

### L6-03 **[承重]**

AFAR's own TAME page, fetched 2026-07-28, describes the trial prospectively and unfunded: design prepared, participants and donors still listed under "what's needed", and FDA acceptance of an aging indication described as a hope rather than an achievement. Design as stated: 3,000 individuals aged 65-79, six years.

- 来源类型:vendor-selfreport
- 出处:American Federation for Aging Research (AFAR), TAME Trial page, afar.org, accessed 2026-07-28.
- URL:https://www.afar.org/tame-trial
- 逐字引:> We hope the FDA will approve aging as an indication, to signify that aging can be 'treated.'
- 利益相关:AFAR is the fundraising sponsor for TAME; the page is a donation solicitation.

### L6-04 **[承重]**

The foundational TAME paper by Barzilai and colleagues does not mention the FDA in its abstract, and I could locate no primary FDA document (letter, guidance, Federal Register notice, advisory committee record) accepting a composite morbidity endpoint for an aging indication. The widely repeated claim that "the FDA agreed to TAME's composite endpoint in 2015" traces only to secondary retellings — AFAR pages, podcasts, trade press — not to a regulator-issued document.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Barzilai N, Crandall JP, Kritchevsky SB, Espeland MA. Metformin as a tool to target aging. Cell Metab. 2016;23(6):1060-1065. PMID 27304507.
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/27304507/
- 逐字引:> Aging has been targeted by genetic and dietary manipulation and by drugs in order to increase lifespan and health span in numerous models. Metformin, which has demonstrated protective effects against several age-related diseases in humans, will be tested in the TAME (Targeting Aging with Metformin) trial, as the initial step in the development of increasingly effective next-generation drugs.
- 利益相关:Barzilai is TAME's principal investigator and the campaign's public face.

### L6-05 **[承重]**

Even sympathetic insiders phrase TAME's regulatory significance in the conditional. A 2023 Nature Communications consensus review by geroscience leaders says TAME 'may serve as a proof of concept that proves to the medical agencies that aging can be a therapeutic indication in itself' — i.e. the indication does not exist and TAME is the hoped-for vehicle, not an accomplished precedent.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Rolland Y, Sierra F, Ferrucci L, et al. Challenges in developing Geroscience trials. Nat Commun. 2023;14:5038.
- URL:https://www.nature.com/articles/s41467-023-39786-7
- 逐字引:> The TAME trial may serve as a proof of concept that proves to the medical agencies that aging can be a therapeutic indication in itself.

### L6-06 **[承重]**

The Loyal LOY-002 milestone is an endpoint precedent only in a narrow, veterinary sense. On 2025-02-26 Loyal announced FDA Center for Veterinary Medicine acceptance of the 'reasonable expectation of effectiveness' (RXE) section of a conditional-approval application, for dogs aged 10+ weighing 14 lb or more, aimed at extending healthy lifespan. RXE is an explicitly lower evidentiary bar than substantial evidence of effectiveness and is granted before the confirmatory effectiveness study reads out; the company itself states two of three sections (manufacturing, safety) remained outstanding. Species: dog. Center: CVM, not CDER. Not a human-drug precedent and not evidence the drug works.

- 来源类型:vendor-selfreport
- 出处:Loyal. 'Not just for big dogs! Our senior dog program receives preliminary FDA efficacy acceptance (RXE).' loyal.com, 26 February 2025.
- URL:https://loyal.com/posts/loy-002-receives-rxe-from-the-fda
- 逐字引:> LOY-002, our program bringing longevity to dogs of almost all sizes, has also earned its formal FDA acceptance for reasonable expectation of efficacy (RXE). ... The other two primary FDA requirements needed for conditional approval (and our market launch) are manufacturing and safety.
- 利益相关:Loyal is a venture-funded company selling the drug; this is a company blog post, not an FDA document.

### L6-07 **[承重]**

Technical noise alone — the same DNA sample assayed twice — produces deviations of up to 9 years between replicates for six prominent epigenetic clocks. This is measurement error on identical material, not biological variation between visits, and it is the number most often mis-quoted at the wrong caliber. The paper's PC-retrained versions bring most replicate pairs within 1.5 years. Reliability analyses used 36 technical replicate pairs (GSE55763) plus an independent set of 37 pairs supplied by Elysium Health.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Higgins-Chen AT, Thrush KL, Wang Y, et al. (Levine ME, senior author). A computational solution for bolstering reliability of epigenetic clocks: implications for clinical trials and longitudinal tracking. Nature Aging. 2022;2:644-661. PMID 36277076.
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/36277076/
- 逐字引:> Epigenetic clocks are widely used aging biomarkers calculated from DNA methylation data, but this data can be surprisingly unreliable. Here we show technical noise produces deviations up to 9 years between replicates for six prominent epigenetic clocks, limiting their utility.
- 利益相关:See L6-08 — the authors' fix is licensed to Elysium Health, which also supplied the validation data.

### L6-08 **[承重]**

The paper that both documented first-generation clock unreliability and sold the remedy was written by authors whose remedy is licensed to a consumer biological-age testing company, using that company's data. Verbatim competing-interests statement discloses Elysium Health licensing through Yale, a prior scientific advisory role and consulting fees, a co-author who was a former Elysium employee, and consulting fees from FOXO Technologies.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Higgins-Chen AT, et al. Nature Aging. 2022;2:644-661 — Competing interests statement.
- URL:https://www.nature.com/articles/s43587-022-00248-2
- 逐字引:> M.E.L. and A.T.H.-C. have built epigenetic aging metrics involving the technology described in the present paper, and these metrics are licensed by Elysium Health through Yale University. Elysium provided paired blood and saliva replicate datasets reported in this study, but otherwise did not fund the study and did not play a role in conceptualization, design, decision to publish or preparation of the manuscript. M.E.L. previously acted as a Scientific Advisor for, and received consulting fees from, Elysium Health. T.H.S. was previously an employee of Elysium Health. A.T.H.-C. received consulting fees from FOXO Technologies for work unrelated to the present manuscript.
- 利益相关:Elysium Health (licensee, data provider, former employer of a co-author, former advisory relationship); FOXO Technologies; Yale University holds the license.

### L6-09 **[承重]**

DunedinPACE's headline test-retest reliability of ICC 0.96 (n=36 replicate pairs, GSE55763) and 0.97 (n=28, EPIC-EPIC) falls to 0.87 (95% CI 0.82-0.90) across array generations (450k-to-EPIC, n=350 pairs). The high figure was also achieved partly by construction: candidate CpG probes were pre-screened for test-retest reliability. Reliability by design is not evidence that the construct tracks anything causal, and vendors quote the same-platform figure.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Belsky DW, Caspi A, Corcoran DL, et al. DunedinPACE, a DNA methylation biomarker of the pace of aging. eLife. 2022;11:e73420.
- URL:https://elifesciences.org/articles/73420
- 逐字引:> For DunedinPACE, reliability was excellent (ICC = 0.96 [0.93–0.98] ... DunedinPACE ICCs were 0.97 [0.94–0.98] ... 0.87 [0.82–0.90]
- 利益相关:Duke University holds intellectual property on the Pace-of-Aging measures and DunedinPACE is licensed commercially (TruDiagnostic); I did not verify the competing-interests block from the primary render in this session — verify before publication.

### L6-10 **[承重]**

The same person can be assigned biological ages ~20-40 years apart depending purely on which tissue is sampled — which is exactly what separates blood-based from saliva/cheek-based direct-to-consumer vendors. In 83 individuals (47 adults 19-70y, 36 children 9-14y; 284 samples), within-person buccal-versus-blood differences were 35.78 +/- 0.68 years for GrimAge2 (buccal vs dried blood spot), 40.25 +/- 0.79 years (buccal vs PBMC), 26.18 +/- 0.78 years for Hannum, and 20.96 +/- 1.00 years for PhenoAge. Blood-versus-blood comparisons (dried blood spot vs buffy coat) showed no significant differences for any clock.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Apsley AT, Ye Q, Caspi A, Chiaro C, Etzel L, Hastings WJ, Heim CM, Kozlosky J, Noll JG, Schreier HMC, Shenk CE, Sugden K, Shalev I. Cross-tissue comparison of epigenetic aging clocks in humans. Aging Cell. 2025;24(4):e14451.
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC11984668/
- 逐字引:> Tissue type plays an important role in the estimation of biological age and should be carefully considered when using epigenetic clocks.
- 利益相关:NIH-funded (U01ES030949, P50HD089922, U24AG066528 and others) plus Penn State; conflict of interest statement: none declared.

### L6-11 **[承重]**

The criterion that would make a clock a regulatory surrogate endpoint is stated explicitly in the geroscience literature and has not been met by any clock: it is not enough that the marker predicts outcomes observationally, the treatment-induced change in the marker must reliably predict the magnitude of change in the clinical outcome, and the marker must reflect the mechanism by which the treatment acts.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Cummings SR, Kritchevsky SB. Endpoints for geroscience clinical trials: health outcomes, biomarkers, and biologic age. GeroScience. 2022;44(6):2925-2931. PMID 36260264.
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/36260264/
- 逐字引:> Using biomarkers instead of clinical outcomes would substantially reduce the size and the duration of clinical trials. This requires validation of surrogate markers showing that treatment induced change in the marker reliably predicts the magnitude of change in the clinical outcome. The surrogate marker must also reflect the biological mechanism for the effect of treatment on the clinical outcome. "Biological age" is a superficially attractive marker for such trials. However, it is essential to establish that treatment induced change in biological age reliably predict the magnitude of benefits in the clinical outcome.

### L6-12 **[承重]**

As of a 2023 consensus review by the field's own leadership, there are no consensually accepted biomarkers of aging at all — a stronger statement than 'not yet validated as surrogates'.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Rolland Y, Sierra F, Ferrucci L, et al. Challenges in developing Geroscience trials. Nat Commun. 2023;14:5038.
- URL:https://www.nature.com/articles/s41467-023-39786-7
- 逐字引:> Currently there are no consensually accepted biomarkers of aging.

### L6-13 **[承重]**

The Biomarkers of Aging Consortium's own 2024 Nature Medicine paper — whose author list includes essentially everyone with a commercial or academic stake in clocks becoming surrogate endpoints — concedes that not only is no biomarker validated, there is not even agreement on how validation should be done. Note the tense throughout: biomarkers 'could' serve as surrogate endpoints.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Moqri M, Herzog C, Poganik JR, Ying K, Justice JN, Belsky DW, ... Ferrucci L. Validation of biomarkers of aging. Nat Med. 2024;30(2):360-372. PMID 38355974.
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/38355974/
- 逐字引:> Such biomarkers could predict aging-related outcomes and could serve as surrogate endpoints for the evaluation of interventions promoting healthy aging and longevity. However, no consensus exists on how biomarkers of aging should be validated before their translation to the clinic.
- 利益相关:Author list spans academic labs and longevity-industry actors; several authors have licensing or advisory relationships with biological-age testing firms.

### L6-14 **[承重]**

The Biomarkers of Aging Consortium states in print the circularity at the centre of the whole field: no geroprotector has been validated in humans, so biomarkers get built from the changes putative interventions produce, and then those biomarkers are used to 'validate' the interventions. Caliber: this appears in Table 1 as an argument against adopting 'responds to putative geroprotectors' as a biomarker criterion; the paper's evidence base is an expert questionnaire with n=29 respondents of 34 invited, described by the authors as predominantly preclinical academic scientists.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Biomarkers of Aging Consortium; Herzog CMS, Goeminne LJE, Poganik JR, Barzilai N, Belsky DW, ... Moqri M, Gladyshev VN. Challenges and recommendations for the translation of biomarkers of aging. Nature Aging. 2024;4 (published 16 September 2024). doi:10.1038/s43587-024-00683-3
- URL:https://www.agingconsortium.org/s/Challenges-and-recommendations-for-the-translation-of-biomarkers-of-aging.pdf
- 逐字引:> No existing geroprotectors have been validated in humans. This may lead to a circularity argument, in which biomarkers are developed using changes induced by putative interventions and thus 'validate' these interventions but do not necessarily truly capture a geroprotective effect.
- 利益相关:Consortium includes founders and advisors of longevity biotech and biological-age testing companies (e.g. Barzilai, Horvath, Verdin, Zhavoronkov, Fedichev, Fortney, Sebastiano, Betts-LaCroix among the coauthors).

### L6-15 **[承重]**

The same Consortium paper supplies the field's own cautionary precedent for surrogate failure: a good predictor can be a bad surrogate when its predictive and response mechanisms differ. Their worked example is a sarcopenia RCT in which increases in lean body and appendicular mass — the assumed surrogate — did not translate into improvements in leg press strength, gait speed, or other health endpoints.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Biomarkers of Aging Consortium; Herzog CMS, et al. Challenges and recommendations for the translation of biomarkers of aging. Nature Aging. 2024. doi:10.1038/s43587-024-00683-3
- URL:https://www.agingconsortium.org/s/Challenges-and-recommendations-for-the-translation-of-biomarkers-of-aging.pdf
- 逐字引:> some very good predictors may still be poor surrogate endpoints, in particular when predictive and response properties have different mechanisms. For instance, increases in lean body and appendicular mass (assumed surrogate endpoints for functional measures) were not associated with improvements in leg press strength, gait speed or other health endpoints in a randomized controlled trial of a sarcopenia treatment; these and other examples of failed surrogate endpoints underscore the need to ultimately understand causal mechanisms.
- 利益相关:As L6-14.

### L6-16 **[承重]**

The largest standardized head-to-head evaluation of aging biomarkers to date — 39 biomarkers across more than 20,000 individuals from diverse cohorts — found that a clock's ability to predict chronological age is essentially uncorrelated with its ability to predict mortality (R = 0.12, P = 0.67). The Horvath skin-and-blood clock had the best chronological accuracy (R-squared = 0.88); GrimAge2 had the strongest mortality association (HR = 2.57) and healthspan prediction (HR = 2.00). Caliber: these are observational hazard ratios in cohorts, per unit of biomarker, not intervention effects, and they establish prediction, not surrogacy.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Ying K, Paulson S, Eames A, Tyshkovskiy A, ... Poganik JR, Moqri M, Gladyshev VN. A unified framework for systematic curation and evaluation of aging biomarkers. Nature Aging. 2025 (published 4 November 2025). doi:10.1038/s43587-025-00987-y
- URL:https://www.nature.com/articles/s43587-025-00987-y
- 逐字引:> Here we show that the ability of traditional aging clocks to predict chronological age does not correlate with mortality prediction capacity (R = 0.12, P = 0.67), suggesting that these metrics capture distinct biological processes.
- 利益相关:Biomarkers of Aging Consortium authorship; Gladyshev and Moqri hold longevity-biotech affiliations.

### L6-17 

As of 3 July 2026 — three weeks before this research run — the Consortium's Nature Medicine paper on immune aging biomarkers still opens by conceding there is no consensus on how to quantify immune aging for clinical trials. The 'no consensus' position is current, not a historical artifact of 2022-2024.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Cipriano A, Justice J, Poganik JR, Moqri M, Abudayyeh OO, Gootenberg J, ... Furman D, Herzog CMS. Immune aging biomarkers for clinical trials. Nat Med. 2026;32:2368-2382 (published 3 July 2026).
- URL:https://www.nature.com/articles/s41591-026-04493-5
- 逐字引:> Despite this central role, there is no consensus on how to quantify immune aging, especially in clinical trials.
- 利益相关:Author list includes industry figures (e.g. Joan Mannick, resTORbio/Tornado Therapeutics lineage; Yotam Levin; Daniel Martin-Herranz, Chronomics/Hurdle).

### L6-18 **[承重]**

Registry audit of the geroscience trial landscape, ClinicalTrials.gov API v2, run 2026-07-28, condition='Aging', interventional studies. Total 3,076. Of these: 2,198 (71.5%) enroll 100 or fewer participants; 1,373 (44.6%) enroll 50 or fewer; 355 (11.5%) have an industry lead sponsor; 90 (2.9%) are Phase 3; 15 (0.49%) name mortality, death or survival in a primary outcome measure; 25 (0.81%) name 'biological age', 'epigenetic age' or 'DNA methylation age' in a primary outcome measure. Surrogate biological-age primary endpoints thus outnumber mortality primary endpoints by 1.7 to 1. Caveats: query.cond is a text/MeSH-expanded search that over-captures studies merely tagging 'aging' and under-captures geroprotector trials registered under a specific disease; enrollment figures include anticipated as well as actual.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:ClinicalTrials.gov API v2 aggregate counts, query.cond=Aging with filter.advanced on StudyType, EnrollmentCount, LeadSponsorClass, Phase and PrimaryOutcomeMeasure; run 2026-07-28.
- URL:https://clinicaltrials.gov/api/v2/studies?query.cond=Aging&filter.advanced=AREA%5BStudyType%5DINTERVENTIONAL&countTotal=true&pageSize=1&fields=NCTId
- 逐字引:> totalCount: 3076 (interventional) / 2198 (enrollment <=100) / 1373 (enrollment <=50) / 355 (industry lead sponsor) / 90 (Phase 3) / 15 (mortality|death|survival in primary outcome) / 25 (biological age|epigenetic age|DNA methylation age in primary outcome)

### L6-19 **[承重]**

The published state of the art on lifespan-RCT feasibility is qualitative, not quantitative: the field asserts that a trial with lifespan as its sole objective would take decades and be very expensive, and moves on to surrogates. Even TAME, the flagship, is powered on a six-year composite morbidity endpoint in roughly 3,000 people aged 65-80 across 14 US sites, not on lifespan.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Rolland Y, Sierra F, Ferrucci L, et al. Challenges in developing Geroscience trials. Nat Commun. 2023;14:5038.
- URL:https://www.nature.com/articles/s41467-023-39786-7
- 逐字引:> Mortality and longevity are outcomes that are difficult to apply in randomized controlled trials because they require long-term follow-up and large sample sizes. A study with the sole objective of lifespan extension would take decades and would be very expensive.

### L6-20 **[承重]**

The hallmarks-of-aging framework has been criticized in the peer-reviewed literature as a pseudo-paradigm: a list dressed as a theory, lacking the explanatory power of the hallmarks-of-cancer account it imitates, and actively obstructing the search for a genuine causal paradigm. The title metaphor is a hoverfly mimicking a wasp's warning colours without the sting.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Gems D, de Magalhães JP. The hoverfly and the wasp: A critique of the hallmarks of aging as a paradigm. Ageing Research Reviews. 2021;70:101407.
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC7611451/
- 逐字引:> while the hallmarks of cancer provide a paradigmatic account of the causes of cancer with profound explanatory power, the hallmarks of aging do not. ... The hallmarks of aging, as a pseudo-paradigm, risks acting as a conceptual obstacle to the development of a genuine paradigm with real explanatory power. ... The aging hallmarks account does not really provide an explanatory framework in terms of primary causes of aging.

### L6-21 

The hallmarks framework grew from nine hallmarks (López-Otín et al., Cell 2013) to twelve (López-Otín et al., Cell 2023, adding disabled macroautophagy, chronic inflammation and dysbiosis) without any of the original nine being retired. A framework that accommodates new candidates by accretion and never sheds one is a taxonomy, not a theory that could have been refuted — which matters because 'targets a hallmark of aging' is the standard rhetorical bridge used to promote a mechanism claim into an implied clinical benefit claim.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:López-Otín C, Blasco MA, Partridge L, Serrano M, Kroemer G. The Hallmarks of Aging. Cell. 2013;153(6):1194-1217; and López-Otín C, Blasco MA, Partridge L, Serrano M, Kroemer G. Hallmarks of aging: An expanding universe. Cell. 2023;186(2):243-278.
- URL:https://www.cell.com/cell/fulltext/S0092-8674(22)01377-0

### L6-22 **[承重]**

Foundational methodological critique of clocks predates the current boom: there is no gold standard of biological aging, clocks predict all-cause mortality at the population but not the individual level, elastic-net-derived clocks offer limited mechanistic insight because of their black-box nature, and standard arrays assay only about 3% of CpG sites in the human genome. The paper makes no recommendation supporting clinical-trial or direct-to-consumer use.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Bell CG, Lowe R, Adams PD, et al. DNA methylation aging clocks: challenges and recommendations. Genome Biology. 2019;20:249.
- URL:https://genomebiology.biomedcentral.com/articles/10.1186/s13059-019-1824-y
- 逐字引:> there is not one measure or 'gold standard' of biological aging ... both can predict all-cause mortality at a population, but not individual level ... limited mechanistic insight due to their black box nature

### L6-23 

WHO's proposed ICD-11 code MG2A 'old age' was withdrawn after formal consultation and replaced, around 8 July 2022, with 'Ageing associated decline in intrinsic capacity' under General Symptoms. Longevity advocacy sites present this as 'aging is now a medical target in ICD-11 with 2 codes'; the actual decision moved away from coding chronological age as a diagnosis. I could not reach the Lancet Healthy Longevity comment describing the withdrawal (HTTP 403), so this entry is second-hand and needs verbatim verification before use.

- 来源类型:media-secondhand
- 出处:Sholl J. et al. (comment). How 'old age' was withdrawn as a diagnosis from ICD-11. The Lancet Healthy Longevity. 2022. PIIS2666-7568(22)00102-7. — primary text not reached in this session.
- URL:https://www.thelancet.com/journals/lanhl/article/PIIS2666-7568(22)00102-7/fulltext
- 利益相关:The competing framing is promoted by longevityforall.org and longevityalliance.org, advocacy organizations campaigning for aging to be classified as a disease.

**交叉口径问题:**
- "The FDA approved the TAME trial" / "FDA accepted aging as an indication": FDA does not approve trials, it clears INDs and gives non-binding advice at pre-IND meetings. The 2015 'FDA agreement on the composite endpoint' has no primary FDA document behind it that I could find. Even the peer-reviewed Muscedere 2025 scoping review uses the wrong verb: 'Only one study—the Targeting Aging with Metformin (TAME) trial—has been approved by the FDA...'. Treat any 'FDA-approved aging trial' phrasing as an unverified secondary claim.
- The Higgins-Chen '9 years' figure is deviation between TECHNICAL REPLICATES — the same DNA sample assayed twice. It is routinely re-quoted as if it were biological variability between visits or the error bar on a person's reported biological age across time. Different caliber; the technical-replicate reading is far more damaging and is the correct one.
- DunedinPACE ICC: the same eLife paper reports 0.96-0.97 within a single array platform (n=36 and n=28 replicate pairs) but 0.87 (0.82-0.90) across 450k-to-EPIC array generations (n=350 pairs). Vendors and reviews quote 0.96. Also, DunedinPACE's probes were pre-screened for reliability, so the high ICC is partly by construction, not an independent property of the biology.
- GrimAge2 'hazard ratio 2.57 for mortality' (Ying 2025) is an observational HR per unit of the biomarker in cohort data. It is regularly deployed as if it licensed the interventional inference 'lower your GrimAge, lower your mortality risk'. No trial supports that step.
- Consumer biological-age results are not comparable across vendors because of sampling tissue alone: Apsley 2025 found within-person GrimAge2 differences of 35.78 years between buccal and dried blood spot in the same individuals. Blood-based and saliva/cheek-based vendors are therefore not measuring the same quantity, yet both report a single number in years.
- Search indexes returned mutually contradictory per-clock ICC values ('GrimAge ICC > 0.9' versus 'most clocks only moderate-to-good, ICC 0.4-0.7, no clock excellent'). I could not reach the primary Higgins-Chen ICC table (Nature paywall, PMC reCAPTCHA, bioRxiv 403). Do not publish any per-clock ICC number that has not been read from a primary table.
- Advocacy framing of ICD-11 ('aging is a medical target in ICD-11 with 2 codes') inverts what happened: WHO withdrew 'old age' as a diagnosis and replaced it with 'ageing associated decline in intrinsic capacity'.
- Multiple pages surfaced by search (imedic.health, geroevidence.com, longevity.direct, mullosaint.com, research.mental-momentum.ai) assert that TAME enrolled ~3,000 participants and will report in 2028-2029. These read as AI-generated content-farm output and are contradicted by the registry (no TAME record) and by AFAR's own page (still seeking donors and participants). Any 'TAME results' claim should be treated as fabricated until a registry entry exists.

**证据缺口(搜过但不存在):**
- No primary FDA document — letter, guidance, Federal Register notice, or advisory-committee record — accepting a composite morbidity endpoint for an 'aging' indication. Searched FDA.gov, NIA's FDA-relay page, and the TAME primary literature. The claim exists only in secondary retellings.
- TAME has no ClinicalTrials.gov registration as of 2026-07-28 (verified with two independent query formulations against the API). A trial announced in 2015-2016, described in press for a decade as FDA-blessed and underway, has never been registered — and AFAR's own page still lists participants and donors as outstanding needs.
- I could find no aging, biological-age, healthspan, frailty or multimorbidity biomarker among FDA's formally qualified biomarkers or its Table of Surrogate Endpoints. This is a partial verification only: FDA's qualified-biomarker list URL returned 404 and the surrogate-endpoint table is a JavaScript-rendered database I could not enumerate. State as 'I could not find one', not 'there is none'.
- No published explicit power or cost calculation for a human all-cause-mortality / lifespan RCT of a geroprotector — no required N, no follow-up duration, no dollar figure. The field asserts infeasibility qualitatively (Rolland 2023: 'would take decades and would be very expensive') and pivots to surrogates. The absence of the number is itself the finding; do not invent one.
- No trial anywhere demonstrating that an intervention-induced change in any epigenetic clock predicts the magnitude of change in a clinical outcome — the Cummings & Kritchevsky criterion for surrogacy. This is the single missing experiment on which the entire biomarker-as-endpoint edifice rests.
- No peer-reviewed head-to-head study that splits one biological sample across multiple commercial direct-to-consumer biological-age vendors and reports the discordance. Apsley 2025 (cross-tissue, within-person) is the nearest published proxy. Everything else that looks like a vendor comparison is affiliate-marketing content.
- The per-clock intraclass correlation table from Higgins-Chen 2022 (original vs PC versions of Horvath1, Horvath2, Hannum, PhenoAge, GrimAge, DNAmTL) could not be retrieved through any open route in this session. Needs institutional library access before any ICC-by-clock claim is published.
- No consensus definition of 'healthspan' as a measurable trial endpoint — the Biomarkers of Aging Consortium notes that prediction of age-related disease incidence ranked above healthspan among expert criteria 'possibly because there is not yet a universally agreed upon definition of healthspan'.
- No regulatory framework for gerotherapeutics exists in any jurisdiction surveyed (FDA, EMA, Health Canada), per a scoping review that screened 3,780 publications and found none.

## L1 — The NIA Interventions Testing Program (ITP) ledger: design, full results, sex asymmetry, industry failures, and limits

### L1-01 **[承重]**

ITP DESIGN — genetic background. Every ITP mouse is a UM-HET3: a four-way cross made by breeding CByB6F1 dams (BALB/cByJ x C57BL/6J) to C3D2F1 sires (C3H/HeJ x DBA/2J). No mouse is inbred and each is genetically unique, which removes the single-strain idiosyncrasy that invalidates most published mouse lifespan claims. This is a design feature, not a result.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Nadon NL, Strong R, Miller RA, Nelson J, Javors M, Sharp ZD, Peralba JM, Harrison DE. Design of aging intervention studies: the NIA interventions testing program. Age (Dordr). 2008;30(4):187-199. doi:10.1007/s11357-008-9048-1
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2585647/
- 逐字引:> The specific stock of HET mice used in the ITP is a cross of four inbred strains, generated by breeding two F1 hybrids, CB6F1 X C3D2F1.
- 利益相关:NIA intramural/extramural program; authors are the ITP principal investigators. No commercial interest.

### L1-02 **[承重]**

ITP DESIGN — power and sample size. Each intervention arm is 36 females and 44 males PER SITE, i.e. 108 females and 132 males total across the three sites, sized a priori to detect a 10% change in lifespan in either sex at 80% power. This is the pre-specified effect size floor: the ITP is not built to resolve effects smaller than ~10%.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Nadon NL et al. Design of aging intervention studies: the NIA interventions testing program. Age (Dordr). 2008;30(4):187-199
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2585647/
- 逐字引:> Each experimental group includes 36 females and 44 males per site, for a total of 108 females and 132 males for each intervention tested. ... Population sizes are large enough that the protocol will detect a 10% change in mean lifespan, in either sex, with 80% power, pooling data from as few as two sites.

### L1-03 **[承重]**

ITP DESIGN — three parallel sites, identical protocol, community-proposed compounds vetted by two-tier peer review. Sites: The Jackson Laboratory (Bar Harbor), University of Michigan, UT Health Science Center San Antonio. Mice are fed sterilized NIH-31 4%-fat chow ad libitum with the test compound mixed in; most protocols start dosing at 4 months of age, some later. Primary analysis is log-rank stratified by site.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Nadon NL et al. Age (Dordr). 2008;30(4):187-199; and Nadon NL, Strong R, Miller RA, Harrison DE. NIA Interventions Testing Program. eBioMedicine. 2017;21:3-4
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5514387/
- 逐字引:> Lifespan tests are done in parallel, using the same protocol, at three independent sites to increase robustness of the findings. ... The ITP uses a four-way cross genetically heterogeneous mouse model (UM-HET3) to reduce the impact of strain-specific characteristics on outcomes.

### L1-04 **[承重]**

BASELINE FOR ALL PERCENTAGES. Across 3,690 ITP control mice from six studies run 2004-2013, control female median lifespan was 887 days (95% CI 879-898) and control male median was 803 days (95% CI 791-815). Any ITP percentage must be read against these denominators; a '20% male extension' is roughly +160 days on an 803-day base.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Cheng CJ, Gelfond JAL, Strong R, Nelson JF. Genetically heterogeneous mice exhibit a female survival advantage that is age- and site-specific: Results from a large multi-site study. Aging Cell. 2019;18(3):e12905
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6516160/
- 逐字引:> Analysis of age-specific survival of 3,690 control ITP mice ... Females: 887 days, 95% CI: 879-898 ... Males: 803 days, 95% CI: 791-815

### L1-05 **[承重]**

CROSS-CALIBER TRAP — the famous rapamycin number. Harrison 2009 Nature's headline '14% for females and 9% for males' is the increase in AGE AT 90% MORTALITY (a maximum-lifespan measure) in mice started on 14 ppm encapsulated rapamycin at 600 DAYS of age. It is not a median-lifespan figure, and it is one of the SMALLER rapamycin effects in the ITP's own ledger. Median increases in that same cohort were about 9% (males) and 13% (females) pooled.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Harrison DE, Strong R, Sharp ZD, Nelson JF, Astle CM, Flurkey K, et al. Rapamycin fed late in life extends lifespan in genetically heterogeneous mice. Nature. 2009;460(7253):392-395. doi:10.1038/nature08221
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/19587680/
- 逐字引:> Here we report that rapamycin, an inhibitor of the mTOR pathway, extends median and maximal lifespan of both male and female mice when fed beginning at 600 days of age. On the basis of age at 90% mortality, rapamycin led to an increase of 14% for females and 9% for males.
- 利益相关:NIA-funded ITP. No commercial sponsor disclosed.

### L1-06 **[承重]**

RAPAMYCIN — the authors' own hedge, in the original Nature paper, that lifespan extension does not establish slowed aging. They explicitly allowed that the whole effect could be cancer postponement.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Harrison DE et al. Nature. 2009;460(7253):392-395
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2786175/
- 逐字引:> Rapamycin may extend lifespan by postponing death from cancer, by retarding mechanisms of ageing, or both.

### L1-07 **[承重]**

RAPAMYCIN — dose-response, start at 9 months. Median lifespan increase by dose and sex: 4.7 ppm = +3% males (p=0.19, NOT significant) and +16% females (p<0.0001); 14 ppm = +13% males (p=0.0015) and +21% females (p<0.0001); 42 ppm = +23% males (p<0.0001) and +26% females (p<0.0001). At 42 ppm the 90th-percentile increase was +8% males (p=0.001) and +11% females (p<0.001). Rapamycin is the ITP's main FEMALE-favouring hit, the opposite of the program's overall male bias.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Miller RA, Harrison DE, Astle CM, Fernandez E, Flurkey K, Han M, et al. Rapamycin-mediated lifespan increase in mice is dose and sex dependent and metabolically distinct from dietary restriction. Aging Cell. 2014;13(3):468-477. doi:10.1111/acel.12194
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4032600/
- 逐字引:> Rapamycin leads to a larger percentage increase in female longevity than in males, even though female controls live longer than male controls at TJL and UT. It seems likely that this differential effect, seen now in three consecutive independent cohorts, reflects higher levels of rapamycin in blood of female UM-HET3 mice compared with males.

### L1-08 **[承重]**

RAPAMYCIN — earlier start, 14 ppm from 9 months of age: median survival extended an average of 10% in males and 18% in females, significant at all three sites, with maximum lifespan also increased. In the SAME paper and same cohorts, resveratrol (300 and 1200 ppm) and simvastatin (12 and 120 ppm) had no significant effect on survival.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Miller RA, Harrison DE, Astle CM, Baur JA, Boyd AR, de Cabo R, et al. Rapamycin, but not resveratrol or simvastatin, extends life span of genetically heterogeneous mice. J Gerontol A Biol Sci Med Sci. 2011;66(2):191-201. doi:10.1093/gerona/glq178
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/20974732/
- 逐字引:> Rapamycin ... produced significant increases in life span, including maximum life span, at each of three test sites. Median survival was extended by an average of 10% in males and 18% in females. ... Resveratrol (at 300 and 1200 ppm food) and simvastatin (12 and 120 ppm) did not have significant effects on survival.

### L1-09 **[承重]**

RAPAMYCIN — late-life and intermittent regimens, all 42 ppm starting at 20 months. Continuous: +11% median males (p=0.0007), +15% females (p<0.0001). Alternating month-on/month-off: +9% males (p=0.002), +8% females (p<0.0001). A single 3-month pulse (20-23 months only): +11% males (p=0.024) but only +4% in females (p=0.15, not significant). Four other agents in the same cohort were null in both sexes: 17-DMAG 30 ppm, minocycline 300 ppm, beta-guanidinopropionic acid 3300 ppm, MitoQ 100 ppm.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Strong R, Miller RA, Bogue M, Astle CM, Chang J, Christy ER, et al. Rapamycin-mediated mouse lifespan extension: Late-life dosage regimes with sex-specific effects. Aging Cell. 2020;19(11):e13269. doi:10.1111/acel.13269
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC7681050/
- 逐字引:> developing clinical strategies for optimal benefit in long-term Rapa treatment ... may require careful stepwise refinement and adjustment for sex-specific effects.

### L1-10 **[承重]**

METFORMIN ALONE FAILED THE ITP. Metformin at 0.1% of diet (1000 ppm) from 9 months of age, both sexes, three sites: a non-significant +7% median in males pooled across sites (P=0.35) and no effect at all in females. This is the only ITP metformin lifespan test published — one dose, one starting age — and it is the load-bearing animal null under the entire 'metformin as a geroprotector' edifice.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Strong R, Miller RA, Antebi A, Astle CM, Bogue M, Denzel MS, et al. Longer lifespan in male mice treated with a weakly estrogenic agonist, an antioxidant, an alpha-glucosidase inhibitor or a Nrf2-inducer. Aging Cell. 2016;15(5):872-884. doi:10.1111/acel.12496
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5013015/
- 逐字引:> Met alone, at a dose of 0.1% in the diet, did not significantly extend lifespan.
- 利益相关:NIA-funded ITP; no commercial sponsor. Metformin is generic, so no vendor had an incentive to fund a larger test.

### L1-11 **[承重]**

METFORMIN + RAPAMYCIN combination (metformin 1000 ppm + rapamycin 14 ppm from 9 months) gave a 23% increase in median survival in BOTH sexes (P<0.001). Caliber caution: the claim that the combination beats rapamycin alone rests on comparison to a DIFFERENT cohort run in a different year (rapamycin 14 ppm alone gave +13% M / +21% F in Miller 2014), not on a concurrent rapamycin-only arm. In females the combination is indistinguishable from rapamycin alone.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Strong R et al. Aging Cell. 2016;15(5):872-884
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5013015/
- 逐字引:> Met (1000 ppm) and Rapa (14 ppm) ... 23% increase in survival compared with controls in both sexes

### L1-12 **[承重]**

METFORMIN — the counter-evidence. An ITP-affiliated group at UT Health San Antonio reanalysed all ITP data 2004-2022 with the Gehan test (more sensitive to early-life mortality differences) and reported that metformin, enalapril, 17-DMAG, CAPE and green tea extract cross significance when the log-rank test had missed them; metformin, enalapril and 17-DMAG in males only, CAPE and GTE in females only. Metformin males were listed as +8% median, log-rank p=0.2200, Gehan p=0.0460 (with the proportional-hazards assumption violated). The authors themselves flag inflated false-positive risk.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Jiang N, Gelfond J, Liu Q, Strong R, Nelson JF. The Gehan test identifies life-extending compounds overlooked by the log-rank test in the NIA Interventions Testing Program. GeroScience. 2024;46(5):4533-4541. doi:10.1007/s11357-024-01161-9
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC11335987/
- 逐字引:> The Gehan test identified 5 additional compounds, metformin, enalapril, 17-dimethylaminoethylamino-17-demethoxygeldanamycin hydrochloride (17-DMAG), caffeic acid phenethyl ester (CAPE), and green tea extract (GTE), which significantly increased survival but were previously missed by the log-rank test. ... We recognize that secondary analyses increase the risk of type 1 error (i.e. increased false positives).
- 利益相关:Authors are UT Health San Antonio ITP-site investigators (Strong and Nelson are ITP PIs), funded by an ITP grant (5U01AG022307). This is an in-house post-hoc reanalysis by the program itself, not independent adversarial replication. 'The authors declare no competing interests.'

### L1-13 **[承重]**

ACARBOSE — the largest single-agent male effect, replicated across doses. At 400, 1000 and 2500 ppm in genetically heterogeneous mice at three sites, every dose was significant in both sexes with no significant difference between doses; the two higher doses gave 16-17% increases in male median longevity but only 4-5% in females. Age at the 90th percentile rose 8-11% in males at every dose but only 3% in females, and only at 1000 ppm.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Harrison DE, Strong R, Alavez S, Astle CM, DiGiovanni J, Fernandez E, et al. Acarbose improves health and lifespan in aging HET3 mice. Aging Cell. 2019;18(2):e12898. doi:10.1111/acel.12898
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/30688027/
- 逐字引:> The two higher doses produced 16% or 17% increases in median longevity of males, but only 4% or 5% increases in females. Age at the 90th percentile was increased significantly (8%-11%) in males at each dose, but was significantly increased (3%) in females only at 1,000 ppm.

### L1-14 **[承重]**

ACARBOSE (original report) — 1000 ppm from 4 months: male median +22% (P<0.0001), female median +5% (P=0.01); 90th percentile +11% males (P<0.001), +9% females (P=0.001). In the same paper 17-alpha-estradiol 4.8 ppm from 10 months gave +12% male median (P=0.002) and did not alter female lifespan; NDGA at 800/2500/5000 ppm gave +8-10% male median (P 0.04 to 0.005) with no female benefit.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Harrison DE, Strong R, Allison DB, Ames BN, Astle CM, Atamna H, et al. Acarbose, 17-alpha-estradiol, and nordihydroguaiaretic acid extend mouse lifespan preferentially in males. Aging Cell. 2014;13(2):273-282. doi:10.1111/acel.12170
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC3954939/
- 逐字引:> the lack of effect in females is not due simply to altered pharmacodynamics

### L1-15 **[承重]**

LARGEST ITP EFFECT EVER PUBLISHED is a combination: rapamycin 14.7 ppm + acarbose 1000 ppm from 9 months gave +34% male and +28% female median lifespan (both p<0.0001). Started at 16 months instead, the same combination gave +13% in both sexes. Captopril 180 ppm from 5 months gave +14% males (p=0.001) and +6% females (p=0.002). Four other agents in that cohort (L-leucine, PB125, sulindac, syringaresinol) were null.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Strong R, Miller RA, Cheng CJ, Nelson JF, Gelfond J, Allani SK, et al. Lifespan benefits for the combination of rapamycin plus acarbose and for captopril in genetically heterogeneous mice. Aging Cell. 2022;21(12):e13724. doi:10.1111/acel.13724
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC9741502/
- 逐字引:> Each of these comparisons leads to the conclusion that the combination of Rapa and Aca leads to a greater extension of male lifespan than the same dose of Rapa by itself, although caution is always warranted when comparing among data sets produced in different years.

### L1-16 **[承重]**

NICOTINAMIDE RIBOSIDE FAILED THE ITP. NR at 1000 ppm from 8 months of age had no significant effect on lifespan in either sex pooled across the three sites: males p=0.252 (median -3%), females p=0.612 (median 0%). This is the NAD+-precursor supplement category's only gold-standard mouse lifespan test, and it is a clean null. In the same cohort candesartan cilexetil (30 ppm), geranylgeranylacetone (600 ppm) and MIF098 (240 ppm) were also null, while 17-alpha-estradiol 14.4 ppm raised male median lifespan 19% when started at 16 months (p<0.0001) and 11% when started at 20 months (p=0.007), with no female effect.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Harrison DE, Strong R, Reifsnyder P, Kumar N, Fernandez E, Flurkey K, et al. 17-a-estradiol late in life extends lifespan in aging UM-HET3 male mice; nicotinamide riboside and three other drugs do not affect lifespan in either sex. Aging Cell. 2021;20(5):e13328. doi:10.1111/acel.13328
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC8135004/
- 逐字引:> Lifespans for pooled data (across the three sites) were not significantly affected in UM-HET3 males or females fed diets with 1,000 ppm nicotinamide riboside
- 利益相关:NIA-funded ITP. Note: NR is sold as Tru Niagen/Niagen by ChromaDex, which did not fund this test. A 2022 corrigendum to this paper (Aging Cell, PMC9649595) was an authorship correction only and changed no data.

### L1-17 **[承重]**

FISETIN FAILED THE ITP. Fisetin 600 ppm from 20 months of age did not significantly increase lifespan in either sex. In the same report astaxanthin (1840 +/- 520 ppm from 12 months) extended male median lifespan 12% (p=0.003) and meclizine (544 +/- 48 ppm from 12 months) extended it 8% (p=0.03), neither with a significant 90th-percentile gain by the Wang-Allison test; SG1002, dimethyl fumarate, mycophenolic acid and 4-phenylbutyrate were all null.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Harrison DE, Strong R, Reifsnyder P, Rosenthal N, Korstanje R, Fernandez E, et al. Astaxanthin and meclizine extend lifespan in UM-HET3 male mice; fisetin, SG1002 (hydrogen sulfide donor), dimethyl fumarate, mycophenolic acid, and 4-phenylbutyrate do not significantly affect lifespan in either sex at the doses and schedules used. GeroScience. 2024;46(1):795-816. doi:10.1007/s11357-023-01011-0
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10828146/
- 逐字引:> Five other new agents were also tested as follows: fisetin, SG1002 (hydrogen sulfide donor), dimethyl fumarate, mycophenolic acid, and 4-phenylbutyrate. None of these increased lifespan significantly at the dose and method of administration tested in either sex.

### L1-18 **[承重]**

2026 — THE WITHIN-ITP NON-REPLICATION. The ITP tested eleven compounds/protocols and none significantly increased lifespan in either sex. Critically, astaxanthin, mitoglitazone and meclizine — all three previously reported BY THE ITP ITSELF as extending male lifespan — showed no benefit at different doses or later starting ages. In females, astaxanthin, late-start mitoglitazone and pioglitazone were associated with significantly REDUCED lifespan in the pooled data. Alpha-ketoglutarate (sold as Rejuvant) failed for the second independent time.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Korstanje R, Strong R, Salmon AB, Bogue MA, Curran SP, Diaz V, Fernandez E, Ginsburg B, Han M, Harrison DE, Kaczorowski C, Kaeberlein M, Kennedy BK, Kumar N, LaCroix-Fralish M, Leiser SF, Nelson JF, Polymenis M, Reifsnyder PC, Rosenthal NA, Silva E, Tower J, Miller RA. Astaxanthin, meclizine, mitoglitazone, pioglitazone, alpha-ketoglutarate, mifepristone, methotrexate, and atorvastatin-telmisartan do not increase lifespan in UM-HET3 mice. GeroScience. 2026;48(3):3821-3830. doi:10.1007/s11357-026-02201-2. Epub 2026-03-17. PMID 41843349
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41843349/
- 逐字引:> none of the tested compounds significantly increased lifespan in male or female mice ... Notably, astaxanthin, mitoglitazone, and meclizine—previously associated with lifespan extension in the ITP—showed no benefit when administered at different doses or starting at later ages.
- 利益相关:'The authors declare no competing interests.' Funded by NIA grants AG022308, AG022307, AG022303, AG013319, U24 AG066346.

### L1-19 **[承重]**

THE ITP CAN SHOW HARM, AND IT DOES — female-specific. 16-alpha-hydroxyestriol raised male median lifespan 15% (p=0.0001) but caused a significant 7% DECLINE in female lifespan. Canagliflozin started at 16 months raised male median lifespan 14% (p=0.004) and caused a significant 6% decline in females (p=0.03), whereas the same drug from 6 months was male-benefit/female-neutral — the ITP's own reading is female-specific late-life harm. Blood canagliflozin was roughly 20-fold higher in aged females than in young males. Alpha-ketoglutarate 20,000 ppm from 18 months, 2,4-dinitrophenol, hydralazine, nebivolol and sodium thiosulfate were all null in this cohort.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Miller RA, Harrison DE, et al. Lifespan effects in male UM-HET3 mice treated with sodium thiosulfate, 16-hydroxyestriol, and late-start canagliflozin. GeroScience. 2024;46(5):4657-4670. doi:10.1007/s11357-024-01176-2. PMID 38753230
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/38753230/
- 逐字引:> Cana given to mice at 6 months led, as in their previous study, to an increase in male lifespan without any change in female lifespan, suggesting that this agent may lead to female-specific late-life harm.
- 利益相关:This paper carries TWO published corrections (GeroScience 2025;47(4):6093-4, doi:10.1007/s11357-024-01283-0, PMID 39004654; and GeroScience 2025;47:6663-4, doi:10.1007/s11357-025-01752-0) resolving whether the tested molecule was 16-hydroxyestriol or 16-hydroxyestradiol — five places in the original text used the wrong name.

### L1-20 

NO PATHOLOGY EXPLANATION FOR THE FEMALE HARM. A dedicated end-of-life necropsy study of the 16-alpha-hydroxyestradiol and late-start canagliflozin cohorts, covering sex-specific lesions including pituitary and adrenal neoplasia, could not identify why these drugs shorten female life while extending male life. This is a boundary of the ITP method: it detects the mortality signal but cannot always explain it.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Snyder JM, et al. End-of-life pathology in UM-HET3 mice treated with 16 alpha-hydroxyestradiol or late-start canagliflozin. GeroScience. 2025;48(2):1787-1797. doi:10.1007/s11357-025-01741-3
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12972294/
- 逐字引:> The analysis did not, however, reveal a clear pattern that would explain the selective sex-specific effects of either agent on lifespan.

### L1-21 

CANAGLIFLOZIN — the primary male result. 180 ppm from 7 months of age raised male median survival 14% (p<0.001) and 90th-percentile survival 9% (p<0.001), with no effect in females (median +1%, not significant). Roughly 50 treated mice per sex per site against ~100 controls per sex per site. The authors attribute the benefit to blunting postprandial glucose peaks rather than average glycemia, noting no HbA1c change.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Miller RA, Harrison DE, Allison DB, Bogue M, Debarba L, Diaz V, et al. Canagliflozin extends life span in genetically heterogeneous male but not female mice. JCI Insight. 2020;5(21):e140019
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC7710304
- 逐字引:> The authors have declared that no conflict of interest exists.
- 利益相关:No commercial COI declared; canagliflozin is Janssen's Invokana but Janssen did not fund. Funding was NIH/ADA.

### L1-22 **[承重]**

GLYCINE is a genuine both-sex ITP hit but the effect is small: 8% dietary glycine from 9 months gave a 4-6% median lifespan increase, significant in males (p=0.002) and females (p<0.001), with the direction consistent at all three sites (p=0.01, 0.053, 0.03). Treated mice were less likely to die of pulmonary adenocarcinoma (p=0.03). Supplement marketing routinely cites glycine as an 'ITP-validated' longevity compound without this 4-6% caliber.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Miller RA, Harrison DE, Astle CM, Bogue MA, Brind J, Fernandez E, et al. Glycine supplementation extends lifespan of male and female mice. Aging Cell. 2019;18(3):e12953. doi:10.1111/acel.12953
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6516426/
- 逐字引:> Elevated glycine led to a small (4%-6%) but statistically significant lifespan increase, as well as an increase in maximum lifespan, in both males (p = 0.002) and females (p < 0.001).
- 利益相关:Co-author J. Brind has published extensively promoting glycine supplementation; the paper is NIA-funded.

### L1-23 **[承重]**

SEX ASYMMETRY — the ITP's own statement, 2025. Epicatechin (~+5%, p=0.037), halofuginone (~+9%, p=0.002) and mitoglitazone (~+9%, p=0.015) all raised male median lifespan when started at 7 months, with no benefit in females; epicatechin and halofuginone also raised 90% survival. The investigators state plainly that the male bias remains unexplained and is the field's dominant pattern.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Interventions Testing Program. Extension of lifespan by epicatechin, halofuginone and mitoglitazone in male but not female genetically heterogeneous mice. GeroScience. 2025. doi:10.1007/s11357-025-01881-6. Published online 19 Sep 2025
- URL:https://link.springer.com/article/10.1007/s11357-025-01881-6
- 逐字引:> This report continues the strong male bias in the efficacy of life-extending drugs identified so far.

### L1-24 **[承重]**

SEX ASYMMETRY — the mechanistic experiment. Castrating male mice at 3 months abolished the male-specific metabolic response to both acarbose and 17-alpha-estradiol: castrated males showed no glucose-clearance improvement at 22 months and lost the 17aE2-driven increase in mTORC2 substrate phosphorylation, behaving like intact females. The ITP group's conclusion is that testicular hormones are required for these drugs to work.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Garratt M, Bower B, Garcia GG, Miller RA. Sex differences in lifespan extension with acarbose and 17-alpha estradiol: gonadal hormones underlie male-specific improvements in glucose tolerance and mTORC2 signaling. Aging Cell. 2017;16(6):1256-1266
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5676051/
- 逐字引:> In contrast to intact males, castrated males showed no significant improvement in their ability to clear glucose after ACA or 17aE2 treatment when tested at 22 months of age. ... male gonads, probably via testosterone production, contribute to these sexually dimorphic metabolic responses, with castrated males showing the lack of drug response typical of intact females.
- 利益相关:Funded by the Glenn Foundation for Medical Research and NIH AG024824, AG022303.

### L1-25 **[承重]**

EXTERNAL CRITIQUE — the '900-day rule'. Short-lived controls inflate the apparent efficacy of longevity interventions, partly through regression to the mean, and independent replication of mouse lifespan results is rare. The proposed bar: trust an intervention only when control median lifespan is near 900 days or the treated group ends up considerably above 900 days. Applied to the ITP: control females (887 d) sit essentially at the threshold, control males (803 d) roughly 100 days below it — so ITP male-only hits rest on a control baseline that this critique would call short.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Pabis K, Barardo D, Gruber J, Sirbu O, Malavolta M, Selvarajoo K, Kaeberlein M, Kennedy BK. The impact of short-lived controls on the interpretation of lifespan experiments and progress in geroscience - Through the lens of the '900-day rule'. Ageing Res Rev. 2024;101:102512. doi:10.1016/j.arr.2024.102512
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/39332712/
- 逐字引:> Our reanalysis of lifespan studies from multiple sources suggests that short lifespans in the control group exaggerate the relative efficacy of putative longevity interventions. Results may be exaggerated due to statistical effects (e.g. regression to the mean) or other factors. ... a putative mouse longevity intervention should only be considered with high confidence when control median lifespans are close to 900 days or if the final lifespan of the treated group is considerably above 900 days.
- 利益相关:No COI declared. Note Kaeberlein and Kennedy are also co-authors on the 2026 ITP paper — this is an inside-the-field critique, not an outside attack.

### L1-26 **[承重]**

THE ITP'S OWN ADMITTED LIMIT — irreducible site heterogeneity. Despite identical SOPs, chow preparations and husbandry, control male mice at one of the three sites consistently weigh less and live longer than control males at the other two. Cheng 2019 makes this sharper: the male/female survival difference that is robust at UT and TJL does not appear in the pooled median at Michigan. Site is a real, unexplained effect variable inside the gold standard.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Nadon NL, Strong R, Miller RA, Harrison DE. NIA Interventions Testing Program: Investigating Putative Aging Intervention Agents in a Genetically Heterogeneous Mouse Model. eBioMedicine. 2017;21:3-4
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5514387/
- 逐字引:> the ITP has consistently found that control male mice at one site weigh less and live longer than the control males at the other two sites, even though each site uses the same food preparations and standardized husbandry ... site-to-site variation is unavoidable even with every effort made to minimize differences between sites.

### L1-27 **[承重]**

COUNTING THE LEDGER. As of the ITP's own 2024 tally of data collected 2004-2022, the program had identified 12 compounds that extend lifespan by the pre-specified log-rank test. An earlier program self-report covering the first 11 years counted 53 lifespan experiments on 30 test agents, with significant longevity effects published for 6. Use these program-stated counts rather than any aggregator's tally.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Jiang N, Gelfond J, Liu Q, Strong R, Nelson JF. GeroScience. 2024;46(5):4533-4541; and Nadon NL et al. 'The NIA Interventions Testing Program: An Update', Innovation in Aging 2017
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC11335987/
- 逐字引:> The National Institute on Aging Interventions Testing Program (ITP) has so far identified 12 compounds that extend the lifespan of genetically heterogeneous mice using the log-rank test.

### L1-28 **[承重]**

A NAMING TRAP THAT WILL CAUSE MISREPORTING. The ITP data portal lists a compound abbreviated 'UA' (2000 ppm, from 10 months, 2013 cohort, null). That is URSOLIC ACID, not urolithin A. The Harrison 2019 paper names it explicitly. Urolithin A (sold as Mitopure) has never been tested by the ITP for lifespan; anyone citing 'UA failed the ITP' as evidence about urolithin A is wrong.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Harrison DE et al. Acarbose improves health and lifespan in aging HET3 mice. Aging Cell. 2019;18(2):e12898
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/30688027/
- 逐字引:> Three other interventions were also tested: ursolic acid, 2-(2-hydroxyphenyl) benzothiazole (HBX), and INT-767; none of these affected lifespan at the doses tested.

### L1-29 

NAVIGATIONAL INDEX, NOT A CITABLE SOURCE. The Jackson Laboratory Mouse Phenome Database ITP portal lists every ITP cohort with compound, dose in ppm, starting age and per-sex p-values (e.g. metformin C2011 1000 ppm from 9 mo null; NR C2016 1000 ppm from 8 mo null; fisetin C2018 600 ppm from 20 mo null; canagliflozin C2016 180 ppm from 7 mo males p<0.0001). Use it to locate cohorts, then cite the paper — the portal's abbreviations are ambiguous (see the ursolic acid trap) and it does not carry the authors' qualifiers.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:Mouse Phenome Database (The Jackson Laboratory), ITP data portal, project ITP1. Accessed 2026-07-28
- URL:https://phenome.jax.org/projects/ITP1
- 利益相关:Hosted by The Jackson Laboratory, which is also one of the three ITP test sites.

**交叉口径问题:**
- THE BIGGEST ONE — 'rapamycin extends lifespan 14%'. The Harrison 2009 Nature headline '14% for females and 9% for males' is the increase in AGE AT 90% MORTALITY after starting at 600 days, not a median-lifespan figure. Median in that cohort was ~13% F / ~9% M. Meanwhile the ITP's own largest rapamycin median effects are +26% (F) and +23% (M) at 42 ppm from 9 months. Popular coverage mixes the 90th-percentile number, the median number, and the 42 ppm number interchangeably. Always state: which percentile, which dose in ppm, which starting age, which sex.
- METFORMIN'S p-VALUE HAS TWO INCOMPATIBLE PUBLISHED VERSIONS. Strong 2016 (the primary report) says +7% male median, P=0.35, pooled across sites. Jiang 2024's reanalysis table lists the same experiment as +8% median, log-rank p=0.2200, Gehan p=0.0460. These differ because of pooling/stratification and censoring date. Do not quote a metformin ITP p-value without naming the analysis. NOTE ALSO: my two extraction passes over Jiang 2024's Table 1 disagreed about whether metformin's Gehan p is 0.0460 or 0.0495 (the paper's Results text quotes 0.0495 for metformin males while the table row reads 0.0460); verify against the PDF before printing a number.
- 'THE ITP VALIDATED METFORMIN' is a promotion from a failed pre-specified test to a passed post-hoc test, run by the ITP's own site investigators on the same data across 132 comparisons, with the proportional-hazards assumption violated for the metformin curve. The honest statement is: metformin failed the ITP's pre-specified log-rank test, and a later post-hoc test by the same group found a marginal early-life signal in males only.
- 'UA' IN THE ITP DATA PORTAL IS URSOLIC ACID, NOT UROLITHIN A. Urolithin A (Mitopure) has no ITP lifespan test at all. Expect this substitution error in secondary write-ups.
- ASTAXANTHIN, MECLIZINE AND MITOGLITAZONE ARE NOW BOTH ITP HITS AND ITP NULLS. Each was reported as extending male lifespan in 2024/2025 and then failed at other doses or later start ages in the 2026 report — and astaxanthin and late-start mitoglitazone were associated with SHORTER female lifespan. Any claim citing only the 2024/2025 papers is now stale. This is the ITP contradicting itself, which is more informative than either result alone.
- COMBINATION CLAIMS REST ON HISTORICAL, NOT CONCURRENT, CONTROLS. Both 'metformin adds to rapamycin' (2016) and 'rapamycin+acarbose beats rapamycin alone' (2022) compare against rapamycin-only arms run in DIFFERENT YEARS. The ITP itself writes that 'caution is always warranted when comparing among data sets produced in different years.' In females the metformin+rapamycin result (+23%) is not better than rapamycin 14 ppm alone (+21%).
- MEDIAN vs 90th PERCENTILE routinely diverge in the ITP and the smaller number is usually the maximum-lifespan one. Acarbose: +16-17% male median but only +8-11% male 90th percentile. Astaxanthin/meclizine: significant male median gains but 90th-percentile gains that failed the Wang-Allison test. A compound that moves median but not maximum is a better candidate for 'delays disease' than 'slows aging'.
- 'THE ITP TESTED X AND IT WORKED' NEEDS A DOSE AND A START AGE. Rapamycin at 4.7 ppm did essentially nothing in males (+3%, p=0.19) while 42 ppm gave +23%. Acarbose worked at 400-2500 ppm; canagliflozin helped males at 7 months and HARMED females at 16 months. Dose and timing are not footnotes in this ledger, they are the result.

**证据缺口(搜过但不存在):**
- NO EXPLICIT ITP POLICY STATEMENT COMMITTING TO PUBLICATION REGARDLESS OF OUTCOME. I searched the design papers (Nadon 2008, Nadon 2017, the ITP update, the JAX program page) and found no sentence saying the program publishes null results as a matter of policy. The claim is strongly supported by BEHAVIOUR — paper titles like '...do not increase lifespan in UM-HET3 mice' (2026) and 'Rapamycin, but NOT resveratrol or simvastatin...' (2011) — but it should be written as an inference from the publication record, not quoted as a stated commitment. The NIA ITP web pages return HTTP 405 to automated fetch and would need manual retrieval to close this.
- NO SECOND METFORMIN TEST. The ITP has published exactly one metformin lifespan experiment: 1000 ppm, start at 9 months, one cohort. There is no higher-dose test, no earlier or later start, no different formulation, and no metformin plasma-level measurement reported in Strong 2016. Given that metformin is the single most-hyped human geroprotector, the absence of a dose-ranging ITP metformin study is itself a striking hole in the evidence base.
- UROLITHIN A HAS NEVER BEEN TESTED BY THE ITP. Neither has NMN (only nicotinamide riboside), spermidine, taurine, berberine, CoQ10 (only MitoQ), or dasatinib+quercetin as a lifespan protocol. Several of these are sold on 'lifespan extension' framing with no gold-standard mouse lifespan test at all — an absence of evidence that marketing presents as unremarkable.
- NO ITP HEALTHSPAN LEDGER. The ITP's primary endpoint is lifespan. Function/healthspan measures (rotarod, grip, glucose tolerance, necropsy pathology) appear inconsistently across cohorts and are not reported for most compounds, so there is no way to build a healthspan ledger parallel to the lifespan ledger. For most ITP hits we do not know whether the extra months were healthy ones.
- NO SECOND-SPECIES CONFIRMATION WITHIN THE ITP. No compound has been carried by the ITP from mouse to a second mammal. The NIA runs a separate Caenorhabditis Intervention Testing Program, but it does not test the same compound list, so there is no cross-species replication arm.
- NO PUBLISHED ITP DISCUSSION OF THE BARRIER-FACILITY / AD-LIBITUM-FEEDING LIMIT THAT I COULD REACH. The design papers describe standardized sterile NIH-31 chow fed ad libitum and controlled housing, but I found no ITP paper discussing what it means that every hit is a hit in a pathogen-controlled, predator-free, ad-libitum-overfed environment — especially for the several hits (acarbose, canagliflozin, rapamycin, metformin) that act on glucose handling and could be partly correcting ad-lib overfeeding rather than slowing aging. The external critique of this comes from outside the program (Pabis 2024).
- THE FULL ELEVEN-ARM LIST OF THE 2026 GeroScience PAPER IS NOT IN THE ABSTRACT. Eight compounds are named in the title; the remaining three protocols (and all doses and start ages) require the full text, which is paywalled at Springer and not yet in PMC as of 2026-07-28.
- NO INDEPENDENT (NON-ITP) REPLICATION OF ANY ITP RESULT AT ITP SCALE. The Pabis 2024 critique makes the general point that mouse lifespan results are rarely independently replicated; I found no outside group that has run a three-site, both-sex, ~240-mouse lifespan test of acarbose, canagliflozin, 17-alpha-estradiol or glycine. The ITP's internal three-site design is the only replication these compounds have.
