The impact of short-lived controls on the interpretation of lifespan experiments and progress in geroscience — Through the lens of the '900-day rule'. Ageing Res Rev. 2024;101:102512 — https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/39332712/ — peer-reviewed (external critique)
- Mouse Phenome Database, ITP data portal (project ITP1), The Jackson Laboratory — https://phenome.jax.org/projects/ITP1 — official-primary (navigational index of all cohorts, doses, start ages, per-sex p-values)
- JAX Aging Center, Interventions Testing Program page — https://www.jax.org/research-and-faculty/research-centers/aging-center/research/itp — official-primary (program description)
- NIA, Interventions Testing Program (ITP) program pages — https://www.nia.nih.gov/research/dab/interventions-testing-program-itp — official-primary (COULD NOT be machine-fetched; returns HTTP 405)

## L5 — Caloric restriction, fasting, and time-restricted eating: the human and primate evidence

### L5-01 **[承重]**

CALERIE-2's registered primary outcome measures were change in core body temperature and change in resting metabolic rate (corrected for body composition) at 12 and 24 months — mechanistic 'rate of living' surrogates. Neither mortality nor incident disease appears as a primary or secondary endpoint anywhere in the registry record. Sponsor Duke University; actual enrollment 238; May 2007 – Nov 2012; eligibility age 21-50 (men) / 21-47 (women), BMI 22-27.9, with CVD and diabetes excluded.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:ClinicalTrials.gov, NCT00427193, 'Comprehensive Assessment of Long-Term Effects of Reducing Intake of Energy (CALERIE)', Duke University; retrieved via ClinicalTrials.gov API v2, 2026-07-28
- URL:https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT00427193
- 逐字引:> Primary Outcome Measures: Change in Core Body Temperature, Baseline to 12 Months; Change in Core Temperature, Baseline to 24 Months; Change in Resting Metabolic Rate (RMR) Corrected for Changes in Body Composition, Baseline to 12 Months; Change in Resting Metabolic Rate (RMR) Corrected for Changes in Body Composition [Baseline, 24 months]

### L5-02 **[承重]**

CALERIE-2 FAILED BOTH of its registered primary endpoints at 24 months. RMR residual decreased more in the CR arm at 12 months (p=0.04) but not at 24 months, and core temperature 'differed little between groups' — i.e. null. Human, N=218 (143 CR : 75 ad libitum), non-obese adults BMI 21.9-28.0, 2 years. This is almost never mentioned in coverage of CALERIE; every famous CALERIE result is a downstream secondary, exploratory, or post-hoc analysis.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Ravussin E, Redman LM, Rochon J, et al. 'A 2-Year Randomized Controlled Trial of Human Caloric Restriction: Feasibility and Effects on Predictors of Health Span and Longevity.' J Gerontol A Biol Sci Med Sci. 2015;70(9):1097-1104. PMID 26187233
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/26187233/
- 逐字引:> The CR group achieved 11.7±0.7 %CR (mean ± standard error) and maintained 10.4±0.4% weight loss. Weight change in AL was negligible. RMR residual decreased significantly more in CR than AL at 12 months (p = .04) but not 24 months (M24). Core temperature change differed little between groups.

### L5-03 **[承重]**

CALERIE-2 prescribed 25% caloric restriction but achieved 11.7±0.7% (Ravussin 2015) / 11.9±0.7% (Kraus 2019). The delivered dose was under half the target. Any statement of the form '25% caloric restriction in humans produced X' is false; CALERIE tested roughly 12% CR. Daily intake fell from 2,467 to 2,170 kcal/day.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Kraus WE, Bhapkar M, Huffman KM, et al. '2 years of calorie restriction and cardiometabolic risk (CALERIE): exploratory outcomes of a multicentre, phase 2, randomised controlled trial.' Lancet Diabetes Endocrinol. 2019;7(9):673-683. PMID 31303390
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/31303390/
- 逐字引:> 218 randomized (143 calorie restriction, 75 control); achieved CR 11.9% calorie reduction (from 2,467 to 2,170 kcal/day) over 2 years.

### L5-04 **[承重]**

The CALERIE cardiometabolic results that are universally cited as the trial's payoff are labelled 'exploratory outcomes' in the paper's own title. N=218, 2:1 randomization, 2 years: LDL-C p<0.0001; total-cholesterol:HDL p<0.0001; SBP p<0.0011; DBP p<0.0001; CRP p=0.012 at 2y; insulin sensitivity index p<0.0001; metabolic syndrome score p<0.0001. Weight -7.5 kg CR vs -0.1 kg AL, 71% of loss being fat mass. Caliber caveat: the abstract reports p-values without effect sizes and confidence intervals.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Kraus WE, et al. Lancet Diabetes Endocrinol. 2019;7(9):673-683. PMID 31303390
- URL:https://www.thelancet.com/journals/landia/article/PIIS2213-8587(19)30151-2/abstract
- 逐字引:> 2 years of calorie restriction and cardiometabolic risk (CALERIE): exploratory outcomes of a multicentre, phase 2, randomised controlled trial

### L5-05 **[承重]**

In the 2023 Nature Aging CALERIE methylation analysis (a post-hoc analysis of banked specimens, N=197 with DNAm data of 220 randomized), exactly ONE of roughly five aging clocks moved. DunedinPACE: d=-0.29 (95% CI -0.45,-0.13) at 12mo and d=-0.25 (95% CI -0.41,-0.09) at 24mo, both P<0.003, a '2-3%' slowing. PhenoAge and GrimAge were flat and at 24 months both had point estimates in the WRONG direction: PhenoAge 24mo d=+0.05 (95% CI -0.11,0.20), P>0.50; GrimAge 24mo d=+0.05 (95% CI -0.07,0.17), P>0.40. First-generation Horvath and Hannum clocks were likewise non-significant.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Waziry R, Ryan CP, Corcoran DL, et al. 'Effect of long-term caloric restriction on DNA methylation measures of biological aging in healthy adults from the CALERIE trial.' Nature Aging. 2023;3:248-257. PMC10148951
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10148951/
- 逐字引:> DunedinPACE 12-month: d = −0.29 (95% CI −0.45, −0.13), P < 0.003; 24-month: d = −0.25 (95% CI −0.41, −0.09), P < 0.003. PhenoAge 12-month: d = −0.03 (95% CI −0.19, 0.12), P > 0.50; 24-month: d = 0.05 (95% CI −0.11, 0.20), P > 0.50. GrimAge 12-month: d = −0.04 (95% CI −0.16, 0.07), P > 0.40; 24-month: d = 0.05 (95% CI −0.07, 0.17), P > 0.40
- 利益相关:D.L. Corcoran and D.W. Belsky are named inventors on the DunedinPACE invention (Duke University / University of Otago), licensed to a commercial entity. The single clock that moved is the one two authors invented and commercially licensed.

### L5-06 **[承重]**

The CALERIE DunedinPACE authors themselves state that the treatment and control DunedinPACE trajectories overlap by close to 90%, that no gold-standard measure of biological aging exists, and — critically — that whether any slowing translated to clinical benefit is unknown. These caveats are in the paper and essentially never travel with the headline.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Waziry R, et al. Nature Aging. 2023;3:248-257. PMC10148951
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10148951/
- 逐字引:> Moreover, changes in DunedinPACE over follow-up showed substantial overlap between the CR treatment group and the AL control group; effect-size estimates imply close to 90% overlap of DunedinPACE trajectories between the two groups. ... CALERIE follow-up is, so far, limited to the end of the intervention period. Whether treatment and any slowing in biological aging that resulted from it translated to long-term clinical benefit is currently unknown.
- 利益相关:See L5-05. Funding NIA R01AG061378 to D.W.B.

### L5-07 

The Author Correction attached to the Waziry CALERIE Nature Aging paper is administrative, not scientific: it corrects Belsky's first listed affiliation. Worth stating explicitly so that the existence of a 'correction' is not over-read as a substantive retraction of the finding.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Author Correction: 'Effect of long-term caloric restriction on DNA methylation measures of biological aging in healthy adults from the CALERIE trial.' Nature Aging. 2023. PMC10275750
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10275750/
- 逐字引:> the first affiliation listed for D. W. Belsky was incorrect ... should have been listed as Butler Columbia Aging Center, Columbia University Mailman School of Public Health, New York, NY, USA

### L5-08 **[承重]**

CALERIE's own investigators reported that 2 years of ~12% CR caused significant bone mineral density LOSS at all three clinically important fracture sites, in healthy non-obese adults aged 37.9±7.2. CR vs AL at 24 months: lumbar spine -0.013±0.003 vs +0.007±0.004 g/cm2 (p<0.001); total hip -0.017±0.002 vs +0.001±0.003 (p<0.001); femoral neck -0.015±0.003 vs -0.005±0.004 (p=0.03). Bone resorption markers rose. This is the underreported cost side, stated by the trialists themselves.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Villareal DT, Fontana L, Das SK, et al. 'Effect of Two-Year Caloric Restriction on Bone Metabolism and Bone Mineral Density in Non-Obese Younger Adults: A Randomized Clinical Trial.' J Bone Miner Res. 2016;31(1):40-51. PMID 26332798
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/26332798/
- 逐字引:> Therefore, bone loss at clinically important sites of osteoporotic fractures represents a potential limitation of prolonged CR for extending life span.

### L5-09 

CALERIE participants lost a meaningful minority of lean tissue: 71% of weight lost was fat (Kraus 2019), i.e. ~29% was not; the MRI ancillary study (n=43; 28 CR, 15 AL) found ~13% CR over 24 months produced 78% of tissue loss as fat and 17.5% as lean tissue. Human, healthy non-obese, 2 years.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Kraus WE, et al. Lancet Diabetes Endocrinol. 2019;7(9):673-683; and CALERIE MRI ancillary, 'Effect of 2-year caloric restriction on organ and tissue size in nonobese 21- to 50-year-old adults in a randomized clinical trial: the CALERIE study.' Am J Clin Nutr. 2021. PMID 34159359
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/34159359/
- 逐字引:> approximately 13% caloric restriction over 24 months led to a preferential loss of fat (78% of total tissue loss) over lean (17.5% of total tissue loss) tissue

### L5-10 **[承重]**

The CALERIE Legacy follow-up study (11-16 years post-trial) ALSO avoids hard clinical endpoints: its registered primary outcomes are 'Biological age - Klemera-Doubal Method' and 'Healthspan'. Status active-not-recruiting, estimated enrollment 216, started 2023-04-06, primary completion estimated 2027-01-31, completion 2028-01-31. So even the long-term follow-up of the only long-term human CR RCT will not report mortality or incident disease as a primary result, and reports nothing before 2027.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:ClinicalTrials.gov, NCT05651620, 'Legacy Effects of CALERIE(TM), a 2-year Calorie Restriction Intervention, on Hallmarks of Healthspan and Aging'; retrieved via API v2, 2026-07-28
- URL:https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT05651620
- 逐字引:> Primary Outcome Measures: Biological age - Klemera-Doubal Method [11-16 years post CALERIE trial]; Healthspan [11-16 years post CALERIE trial]. Overall Status: ACTIVE_NOT_RECRUITING. Primary Completion Date (Estimated): 2027-01-31

### L5-11 **[承重]**

The famous Wisconsin monkey result (2009) rested on a chosen endpoint: 37% (14/38) of controls vs 13% (5/38) of CR animals died of AGE-RELATED causes (p=0.03) — but in the same paper all-cause mortality was NOT significant (p=0.16). Crucially, MORE CR animals (9) than controls (7) died of 'non-age-related' causes and were excluded from the headline analysis. Rhesus macaques, N=76, all Indian-origin, adult at onset (7-14 years), ~30% restriction.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Colman RJ, Anderson RM, Johnson SC, et al. 'Caloric restriction delays disease onset and mortality in rhesus monkeys.' Science. 2009;325(5937):201-204. PMC2812811
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2812811/
- 逐字引:> The effect of CR on overall mortality is in the predicted direction, but is currently not statistically significant (p=0.16). ... Seven control and 9 animals subjected to CR died of non-age-related causes, which included complications of anesthesia, gastric bloat, endometriosis, and injury. ... All animals were adults (7 to 14 years old) when introduced into the study.

### L5-12 **[承重]**

With five more years of accrued deaths, the Wisconsin all-cause result did firm up: age-related mortality HR 2.89 (95% CI 1.34-6.25), P=0.007; all-cause mortality HR 1.78 (95% CI 1.04-3.04), P=0.037. The honest grading is that the 2009 all-cause claim was unsupported at the time and became supported by 2014, at a modest hazard ratio whose confidence interval nearly touches 1. Rhesus macaques, ~30% CR from young adulthood.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Colman RJ, Beasley TM, Kemnitz JW, Johnson SC, Weindruch R, Anderson RM. 'Caloric restriction reduces age-related and all-cause mortality in rhesus monkeys.' Nat Commun. 2014;5:3557. PMID 24691430
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24691430/
- 逐字引:> Here we show that CR significantly improves age-related and all-cause survival in monkeys on a long-term ~30% restricted diet since young adulthood. ... Age-related mortality: HR 2.89 (95% CI: 1.34–6.25), P=0.007; all-cause mortality: HR 1.78 (95% CI: 1.04–3.04), P=0.037

### L5-13 **[承重]**

The NIA rhesus CR study found NO survival benefit at either onset age. Young/adolescent-onset: 81 monkeys (40 control, 41 CR); old-onset: 34 monkeys (16 control, 18 CR). Some metabolic improvements (lower triglycerides in older CR, lower cholesterol in older CR males, lower glucose in CR males) but no survival improvement and no significant difference in age-related disease incidence.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Mattison JA, Roth GS, Beasley TM, et al. 'Impact of caloric restriction on health and survival in rhesus monkeys from the NIA study.' Nature. 2012;489(7415):318-321. PMID 22932268
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/22932268/
- 逐字引:> We report here that a CR regimen implemented in young and older age rhesus monkeys at the National Institute on Aging (NIA) has not improved survival outcomes.

### L5-14 **[承重]**

The 2017 joint reconciliation identified the diet fed to CONTROLS as a principal explanation. Wisconsin used a semi-purified diet in which sucrose was 45% of total carbohydrates and fat 10.6%; NIA used a naturally sourced diet with sucrose under 7% of carbohydrates and fat 5.0%. Wisconsin therefore compared CR against ad libitum feeding of a high-sucrose Western-style diet, while NIA compared CR against a healthier diet. 'Does CR work?' is not separable from 'CR relative to what?'

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Mattison JA, Colman RJ, Beasley TM, et al. 'Caloric restriction improves health and survival of rhesus monkeys.' Nat Commun. 2017;8:14063. PMC5247583
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5247583/
- 逐字引:> A naturally sourced diet was employed at the NIA facility [while] a semi-purified diet was employed at UW to ensure that intake could be fully defined. ... sucrose comprised less than 7% of total carbohydrates [NIA] versus 45% of total carbohydrates [UW]

### L5-15 **[承重]**

The second reconciliation factor: how controls were FED. NIA control monkeys received measured portions based on National Research Council published values for approximate ad libitum intake, whereas Wisconsin controls were fed at levels approximating true ad libitum following 3-6 months of measured baseline intake. NIA's portion-controlled 'controls' were effectively themselves mildly calorie-restricted, shrinking the contrast the study could detect. Colman 2014 makes the same point explicitly.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Mattison JA, et al. Nat Commun. 2017;8:14063, PMC5247583; corroborated by Colman RJ, et al. Nat Commun. 2014;5:3557
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5247583/
- 逐字引:> [NIA] Controls received measured portions based on data published by the National Research Council to provide approximate ad libitum intake. [UW] Food was provided at levels approximating ad libitum to control animals ... [Colman 2014:] control animals in the NIA study were effectively undergoing CR

### L5-16 **[承重]**

The third reconciliation factor: AGE AT ONSET, with a result that cuts against CR. In the NIA study, juvenile/young-onset CR produced no survival benefit and the CR monkeys actually reached 80% mortality BEFORE the controls; old-onset CR showed no difference against its own controls. Wisconsin's adult-onset CR showed a significant survival benefit with control animals dying at almost twice the rate. Adult-onset CR looked beneficial; juvenile-onset CR did not, and trended harmful. Fourth factor: NIA animals were of mixed Indian and Chinese origin, Wisconsin animals all Indian-origin. Ns: NIA 121 monkeys, UW 76 (38/group).

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Mattison JA, et al. 'Caloric restriction improves health and survival of rhesus monkeys.' Nat Commun. 2017;8:14063. PMC5247583
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5247583/
- 逐字引:> [NIA young-onset] CR monkeys reached 80% mortality before the controls. ... [UW adult-onset] statistically significant effect...increasing survival [with] control animals having almost twice the rate of death. ... [NIA monkeys were] sourced from several locations and included monkeys of both Indian and Chinese origin [vs UW] all of Indian origin.

### L5-17 **[承重]**

The TREAT trial found NO weight benefit from 16:8 time-restricted eating. N=141 randomized, 116 analyzed, adults with overweight/obesity, 12 weeks, TRE arm eating ad libitum 12:00-20:00. Primary outcome (weight) between-group difference -0.26 kg (95% CI -1.30 to 0.78; P=0.63). Critically, energy intake was never measured — the authors state they have no self-reported measures of energy or macronutrient intake, so the trial cannot say whether the timing manipulation changed calories at all.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Lowe DA, Wu N, Rohdin-Bibby L, et al. 'Effects of Time-Restricted Eating on Weight Loss and Other Metabolic Parameters in Women and Men With Overweight and Obesity: The TREAT Randomized Clinical Trial.' JAMA Intern Med. 2020;180(11):1491-1499. PMC7522780
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC7522780/
- 逐字引:> Between-group difference: −0.26 kg (95% CI, −1.30 to 0.78; P = .63) ... We do not have self-reported measures of energy or macronutrient intake ... We did not measure changes in protein intake

### L5-18 **[承重]**

TREAT's much-quoted lean-mass alarm: appendicular lean mass fell 0.64 kg in TRE vs 0.17 kg in control (P<0.001), and roughly 65% of the weight lost in the TRE arm was lean mass (1.10 kg of lean vs only 0.51 kg of fat), against a typical expectation of 20-30%. It drew a published critical letter, and JAMA Intern Med also issued a separate correction to Figure 2 of the paper. Grounds for caution on the number: it was one of many secondary DXA outcomes; DXA lean soft tissue includes water and glycogen and is sensitive to hydration shifts that fasting induces; protein intake was unmeasured; and the specifically large lean-mass penalty has not been replicated (TREATY at 12 months found lean mass tracked its null primary outcome).

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Lowe DA, et al. JAMA Intern Med. 2020;180(11):1491-1499; critique: Tinsley GM, Peterson CM, Horne BD. 'Caution Against Overinterpreting Time-Restricted Eating Results.' JAMA Intern Med. 2021;181(6):877-878, PMID 33616609, with author reply by Weiss EJ, Lowe DA, Vittinghoff E
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/33616609/
- 逐字引:> The TRE group lost 0.64 kg appendicular lean mass versus 0.17 kg in controls (P < .001) ... Of this, 1.10 kg (approximately 65% of weight lost) was lean mass; only 0.51 kg of weight loss was fat mass
- 利益相关:The critics have TRE-favourable interests: Tinsley consulted for an intermittent fasting app; Horne leads intermittent-fasting research funded by the Intermountain Research and Medical Foundation. Interests run in both directions on this dispute.

### L5-19 **[承重]**

TREATY — the largest and longest TRE randomized trial (12 months) — found time-restricted eating adds nothing to matched calorie restriction. N=139 adults with obesity (BMI 28-45), China; BOTH arms calorie-restricted to 1500-1800 kcal/day (men) and 1200-1500 kcal/day (women); TRE arm confined to 08:00-16:00. 118/139 (84.9%) completed. Weight: TRE -8.0 kg (95% CI -9.6 to -6.4) vs CR-alone -6.3 kg (95% CI -7.8 to -4.7); between-group difference -1.8 kg (95% CI -4.0 to 0.4; P=0.11). Waist, BMI, body fat, lean mass, blood pressure and metabolic risk factors all tracked the null primary result.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Liu D, Huang Y, Huang C, et al. 'Calorie Restriction with or without Time-Restricted Eating in Weight Loss.' N Engl J Med. 2022;386(16):1495-1504. PMID 35443107
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/35443107/
- 逐字引:> Among patients with obesity, a regimen of time-restricted eating was not more beneficial with regard to reduction in body weight, body fat, or metabolic risk factors than daily calorie restriction.

### L5-20 **[承重]**

The decisive isocaloric test: when calories are held constant by providing matched meals, TRE does nothing. N=41 adults with obesity plus prediabetes or diet-controlled diabetes (mean age 59, 93% women, mean BMI 36), 12 weeks; TRE arm 10-hour window with 80% of calories before 1pm, control arm <=16-hour window with >=50% of calories after 5pm, both isocaloric with identical nutrient content. Weight difference 0.3 kg (95% CI -1.2 to 1.9) — null. Fasting glucose, HOMA-IR, glucose AUC and glycated albumin all null. Limits: small N, 93% women, 12 weeks. Funded by the American Heart Association.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Maruthur NM, Pilla SJ, White K, et al. 'Effect of Isocaloric, Time-Restricted Eating on Body Weight in Adults With Obesity: A Randomized Controlled Trial.' Ann Intern Med. 2024;177(5):549-558. PMID 38639542
- URL:https://www.acpjournals.org/doi/10.7326/M23-3132
- 逐字引:> In the setting of isocaloric eating, TRE did not decrease weight or improve glucose homeostasis relative to a UEP, suggesting that any effects of TRE on weight in prior studies may be due to reductions in caloric intake.
- 利益相关:Funder: American Heart Association (no commercial interest in the result).

### L5-21 

A 2025 Science Translational Medicine crossover trial cleanly separates TRE's mechanism from its benefit: N=31 women with overweight/obesity, 2 weeks early TRE (08:00-16:00) vs 2 weeks late TRE (13:00-21:00), intended isocaloric. Shifting the eating window 5 hours later delayed monocyte-derived circadian phase by about 24 minutes, confirming peripheral clocks entrain to meal timing — but insulin sensitivity did not differ between or within interventions, and there were no improvements in glycemic, lipid or inflammatory markers. TRE does something biological without doing something useful. NOTE: I could not reach the full text (science.org and medRxiv both returned 403); this should be confirmed against full text by a verifier.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Peters B, Schwarz A, et al. 'Intended isocaloric time-restricted eating shifts circadian clocks but does not improve cardiometabolic health in women with overweight.' Sci Transl Med. 2025;17(822). doi:10.1126/scitranslmed.adv6787
- URL:https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/scitranslmed.adv6787
- 逐字引:> Intended isocaloric time-restricted eating shifts circadian clocks but does not improve cardiometabolic health in women with overweight

### L5-22 **[承重]**

The best current meta-analysis (99 randomized trials, 6,582 adults) finds intermittent fasting roughly equivalent to ordinary calorie restriction. Versus ad libitum: alternate-day fasting -3.40 kg (95% CI -4.14 to -2.67, HIGH certainty); whole-day fasting -2.36 kg (-3.02 to -1.70, HIGH); continuous energy restriction -2.11 kg (-2.73 to -1.50, MODERATE); time-restricted eating -1.72 kg (-2.21 to -1.22, MODERATE). Head-to-head, only ADF beat CER, by -1.29 kg (-1.99 to -0.59, MODERATE certainty). Median trial duration was 12 weeks (range 3-52); only 17 of 99 trials ran >=24 weeks. No trial measured a hard clinical endpoint.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Semnani-Azad Z, et al. 'Intermittent fasting strategies and their effects on body weight and other cardiometabolic risk factors: systematic review and network meta-analysis of randomised clinical trials.' BMJ. 2025. PMC12175170
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12175170/
- 逐字引:> The current evidence provides some indication that intermittent fasting diets have similar benefits to continuous energy restriction for weight loss and cardiometabolic risk factors. Longer duration trials are needed to further substantiate these findings.

### L5-23 **[承重]**

The '91% higher cardiovascular mortality from 8-hour eating' claim originated as AHA EPI|Lifestyle Sessions 2024 Abstract P192 (Circulation. 2024;149:AP192) — a conference abstract only, not peer reviewed, promoted worldwide via an AHA newsroom press release. Its fatal design feature: habitual eating window over ~8 years of follow-up was inferred from just TWO 24-hour dietary recalls.

- 来源类型:media-secondhand
- 出处:American Heart Association newsroom release, '8-hour time-restricted eating linked to a 91% higher risk of cardiovascular death', 18 March 2024, reporting Abstract P192 (Zhong VW et al.), AHA EPI|Lifestyle Scientific Sessions 2024, Chicago; abstract indexed as Circulation. 2024;149:AP192
- URL:https://newsroom.heart.org/news/8-hour-time-restricted-eating-linked-to-a-91-higher-risk-of-cardiovascular-death
- 逐字引:> Research abstracts are considered preliminary until published in a peer-reviewed scientific journal.
- 利益相关:Recorded as an example of institutional hype: the AHA issued a global press release for an unreviewed abstract based on two days of diet recall.

### L5-24 

Named experts criticised the 91% abstract on exactly the right grounds. Prof Kevin McConway (Open University): classification rested on 'just two days' over a study averaging 8 years and 'We don't know whether their eating times over those two 24-hour periods was typical'; and reverse causation is live — people already worried about cardiovascular health 'might be more likely to decide to restrict their eating times.' Dr Duane Mellor (Aston Medical School) and Prof Tom Sanders (King's College London) questioned whether diet quality and confounders (smoking, physical activity, alcohol, social class) were adjusted for.

- 来源类型:media-secondhand
- 出处:Science Media Centre, 'Expert reaction to conference abstract about time-restricted eating and cardiovascular death', March 2024
- URL:https://www.sciencemediacentre.org/expert-reaction-to-conference-abstract-about-time-restricted-eating-and-cardiovascular-death/
- 逐字引:> researchers classified people into different dietary patterns on the basis of what and when they reported they ate in just two days, over a study period averaging 8 years ... It could even be that there's some sort of reverse causation. People who were already worried about their cardiovascular health, perhaps for some good reason such as signs of illness or previous unhealthy activity, might be more likely to decide to restrict their eating times.

### L5-25 **[承重]**

The abstract WAS eventually published in full in 2025 — and the numbers are not the ones in circulation. N=19,831 NHANES adults 2003-2018 with two valid 24-h recalls, linked to the National Death Index through Dec 2019, median follow-up 8.1 years, reference eating duration 12-14 h. Eating duration <8 h: cardiovascular mortality HR 2.35 (95% CI 1.39-3.98) — a LARGER relative effect than the publicised 91% — but NOT associated with all-cause or cancer mortality, and the all-cause association 'did not survive many sensitivity analyses.' The authors themselves refuse the causal reading. Adjustment was extensive (Model 5: demographics, SES, food security, smoking, drinking, physical activity, HEI-2015, BMI and BMI-squared, self-reported CVD/cancer/dyslipidemia/hypertension/diabetes, breakfast skipping), so the residual worry is that a <8h window in NHANES marks constrained or chaotic life circumstances rather than deliberate TRE.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Chen M, Xu L, Van Horn L, Manson JE, Tucker KL, Du X, Feng N, Rong S, Zhong VW. 'Association of eating duration less than 8 h with all-cause, cardiovascular, and cancer mortality.' Diabetes & Metabolic Syndrome: Clinical Research & Reviews. 2025. doi:10.1016/j.dsx.2025.103278
- URL:https://doi.org/10.1016/j.dsx.2025.103278
- 逐字引:> During a median follow-up of 8.1 years, compared with eating duration of 12–14 h, eating duration <8 h was robustly associated with higher cardiovascular mortality (HR, 2.35 [95 % CI, 1.39–3.98]), but not with all-cause and cancer mortality. ... However, the association with all-cause mortality did not survive many sensitivity analyses. Conclusions: Although a positive association was observed between eating duration <8 h and cardiovascular mortality, further research is required to understand whether this risk is attributed to the short eating duration itself or residual confounding resulting from its contributing factors.

### L5-26 **[承重]**

The authors of that same 2025 paper state plainly that the entire TRE randomized evidence base is short-term and surrogate-only, and that the missing trials are unlikely ever to be run. They also concede that TRE's cardiometabolic benefits may be a calorie effect rather than a timing effect.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Chen M, et al. Diabetes Metab Syndr. 2025. doi:10.1016/j.dsx.2025.103278
- URL:https://doi.org/10.1016/j.dsx.2025.103278
- 逐字引:> All published randomized controlled trials (RCTs) studied the effects of TRE regimens on changes in risk factors within 1 year. Long-term RCTs investigating the effects of 8-h TRE on hard clinical endpoints are currently unavailable and will be challenging to conduct in the future. ... Eight-hour TRE is the most commonly studied, but its cardiometabolic benefits may primarily stem from caloric restriction rather than the timing of food intake.

### L5-27 

The foundational Longo fasting-mimicking-diet trial reported markers only, and its broader biomarker panel came from a post hoc analysis. N=100 randomized, only 71 completed three FMD cycles (29% attrition); randomized crossover — arm 1 (n=48) normal diet 3 months then crossed to FMD, arm 2 (n=52) FMD immediately; FMD = 5 consecutive days per month x 3 cycles. Three cycles reduced body weight, trunk and total body fat, blood pressure, and IGF-1. Caliber caveat: the abstract reports directions of effect, not effect sizes with confidence intervals.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Wei M, Brandhorst S, Shelehchi M, et al. 'Fasting-mimicking diet and markers/risk factors for aging, diabetes, cancer, and cardiovascular disease.' Sci Transl Med. 2017;9(377):eaai8700. PMID 28202779
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/28202779/
- 逐字引:> A post hoc analysis of subjects from both FMD arms showed that body mass index, blood pressure, fasting glucose, IGF-1, triglycerides, total and low-density lipoprotein cholesterol, and C-reactive protein were more beneficially affected in participants at risk for disease than in subjects who were not at risk. ... Cycles of a 5-day FMD are safe, feasible, and effective in reducing markers/risk factors for aging and age-related diseases.
- 利益相关:Longo is founder of and holds equity in L-Nutra, which sells the FMD as ProLon; USC licenses the IP to L-Nutra and can receive royalties.

### L5-28 **[承重]**

The celebrated '2.5 years younger biological age' from the fasting-mimicking diet is a WITHIN-GROUP pre-post comparison in the FMD arm, not a randomized between-group contrast — even though both parent trials had control arms. It is a secondary and exploratory reanalysis of two already-published RCTs (NCT02158897: n=52 FMD vs n=48 control who 'continued their normal diet for 3 months'; NCT04150159: n=44 FMD vs n=40 Mediterranean diet). Effects: 2.5 years in NCT02158897 (n=52, p=0.0007), 2.7 years in NCT04150159 (n=34), 2.6 years pooled (N=86). The measure is the Klemera-Doubal method built from just 7 ordinary clinical values — albumin, alkaline phosphatase, creatinine, CRP, HbA1c, systolic blood pressure, total cholesterol — several of which fall transiently during and after 5 days of severe energy restriction. A within-arm drop is close to guaranteed and indexes acute physiological response, not aging rate.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Brandhorst S, Levine ME, Wei M, et al. 'Fasting-mimicking diet causes hepatic and blood markers changes indicating reduced biological age and disease risk.' Nat Commun. 2024;15:1309. PMC10879164
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10879164/
- 逐字引:> secondary and exploratory analysis of blood samples from a randomized clinical trial ... median biological age decreased by nearly 2.5 years (Fig. 3A, p = 0.0007) ... [KDM built from] albumin, alkaline phosphatase, serum creatinine, C-reactive protein, Hba1c, systolic blood pressure, and total cholesterol
- 利益相关:The FMD was supplied by L-Nutra Inc.; USC licensed the IP to L-Nutra and can receive royalties; V.D.L. and T.E.M. hold equity in L-Nutra (V.D.L.'s pledged to charity). Full-stack commercial interest, properly disclosed.

### L5-29 **[承重]**

The FMD authors' own limitations undercut the life-expectancy extrapolation that generated the headlines: the models rest on only 86 participants, drawn from advantaged and enthusiastic volunteers, ignore compliance and dropout, and the authors warn that the effects may be erased entirely once participants stop.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Brandhorst S, et al. Nat Commun. 2024;15:1309. PMC10879164
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10879164/
- 逐字引:> the models used to estimate the effects of 3 cycles of FMD on Biological age were based on results from two clinical trials and include only 86 participants. Moreover, trial participants generally shared advantageous social, economic, behavioral, and health characteristics and therefore their results may not generalize to the population as a whole... The simulation also does not take into account compliance, dropout, or the bias that may arise as a result of enthusiastic volunteers... Also, projections obtained by extrapolating the effects after 3–6 months of the FMD to a lifelong intervention should be cautiously interpreted, since they may be erased in study participants returning to their previous lifestyle in the absence of additional FMD cycles.
- 利益相关:See L5-28.

### L5-30 

HYPE ARTEFACT, recorded as such: the widely circulated 'reduce biological age by 11 years' figure for the fasting-mimicking diet is not a measured result. It is a 20-year simulation assuming continued efficacy, extrapolated from a 2.5-year within-group short-term biomarker shift. The article's own body text concedes this while the headline does not. Three tier-jumps in one press cycle: within-group pre-post biomarker change -> 20-year simulation -> headline.

- 来源类型:media-secondhand
- 出处:Newsweek, 'Research Suggests Diet Plan May Reduce Biological Age by 11 Years', 2024
- URL:https://www.newsweek.com/research-suggests-diet-plan-may-reduce-biological-age-11-years-1875245
- 逐字引:> A simulation of the effect of 3 FMD cycles for twenty years assuming continued efficacy of this dietary intervention suggests a potential decrease in biological age of about 11 years.
- 利益相关:Amplifies a claim from a study whose diet was supplied by, and whose senior author holds equity in, L-Nutra.

### L5-31 **[承重]**

SYSTEMATIC SEARCH RESULT: I scanned all 1,472 studies registered on ClinicalTrials.gov matching 'caloric restriction' OR 'calorie restriction' OR 'intermittent fasting' OR 'time-restricted eating' OR 'fasting-mimicking', regex-matching primary outcome measures for mortality, death, all-cause, survival, MACE, or incident cancer/diabetes/CVD/stroke/dementia. Only 9 matched, and every one is in a DISEASE population: BWEL breast cancer weight loss (NCT02750826, n=3177, invasive disease-free survival); FMD in colorectal cancer (NCT05384444, n=602); a not-yet-recruiting CKD/MAFLD trial (NCT07391267, n=90); glioma reirradiation (NCT01754350); LKB1-inactive lung cancer (NCT03709147); pre-hepatectomy low-calorie diet (NCT01645852); pre-surgical OptiSurg (NCT04627688); one mis-registered pediatric BMT study; and one withdrawn head-and-neck cancer TRE study with zero enrollment. No CR, fasting, or TRE trial in a healthy or general population anywhere has mortality or incident age-related disease as a primary endpoint.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:ClinicalTrials.gov API v2 systematic scan of 1,472 registered CR/fasting/TRE studies, executed 2026-07-28; full method and hit table recorded in working notes
- URL:https://clinicaltrials.gov/api/v2/studies
- 逐字引:> TOTAL SCANNED: 1472 / HITS with hard-clinical-endpoint primary outcome: 9 — NCT02750826 Breast Cancer WEight Loss Study (BWEL), 3177, 'Invasive disease-free survival'; NCT05384444 Effect of FMD on Colorectal Cancer Patients, 602, 'Disease-free Survival'; NCT06603155 TRE in metastatic HNSCC, 0, WITHDRAWN

### L5-32 

The only sizeable harm literature on intermittent fasting concerns disordered eating, and it is rarely cited alongside benefit claims. In the Canadian Study of Adolescent Health Behaviors (N=2,762, ages 16-30), IF in the past 12 months was reported by 47.7% of women, 38.4% of men and 52% of transgender/gender-nonconforming participants; IF was significantly associated with overeating, binge eating, vomiting, laxative use and compulsive exercise in women, and with compulsive exercise and higher EDE-Q global scores in men. Caliber caveat: observational and self-report, and reverse causation is highly plausible — people with existing disordered eating may adopt 'intermittent fasting' as a socially acceptable label for restriction. Record as a signal, not a causal claim.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Ganson KT, Cuccolo K, Hallward L, Nagata JM. 'Intermittent fasting: Describing engagement and associations with eating disorder behaviors and psychopathology among Canadian adolescents and young adults.' Eating Behaviors. 2022;47:101681
- URL:https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/abs/pii/S1471015322000873
- 逐字引:> Overall, 47.7% of women, 38.4% of men, and 52% of transgender/gender nonconforming participants engaged in intermittent fasting in the past 12 months.

**交叉口径问题:**
- The 25% vs 11.9% gap. CALERIE is universally described as a '25% caloric restriction' trial — that is its protocol name — but the achieved restriction was 11.7-11.9%. Every effect size attributed to CALERIE is the effect of roughly 12% restriction. Claims of the form '25% CR in humans produced X' mix the prescribed dose with the delivered dose and are false as stated.
- CALERIE's N is quoted three different ways: 218 (Kraus 2019, Villareal 2016), 220 (Waziry 2023), and 238 (ClinicalTrials.gov actual enrollment). 218/220 are randomized-and-analyzed counts under slightly different analysis samples; 238 is enrolled. Any citation must say which denominator it means.
- The '91% higher cardiovascular death' number and the peer-reviewed number are different numbers for the same analysis. The circulating figure (HR~1.91) comes from the unreviewed 2024 conference abstract; the 2025 published paper reports HR 2.35 (95% CI 1.39-3.98) — a larger relative effect — while simultaneously reporting NO association with all-cause or cancer mortality. The alarming half travelled and the null half did not. Anyone citing '91%' is citing a superseded, unreviewed figure.
- The FMD 'biological age' numbers mix calibers three times over. The 2.5-year figure is a WITHIN-GROUP pre-post change in the FMD arm, not a randomized between-group contrast, despite both parent trials having control arms. The 11-year figure is a 20-year simulation assuming continued efficacy. Neither is a controlled effect estimate, yet both circulate as if they were trial results.
- 'CR extended monkey lifespan (Wisconsin 2009)' misquotes that paper. Its all-cause mortality result was explicitly non-significant (p=0.16); the significant p=0.03 applied only to 'age-related deaths' after excluding 16 deaths, 9 of which were in the CR arm. The endpoint choice produced the headline. All-cause significance arrived only in 2014, at HR 1.78 with a CI nearly touching 1.
- The DunedinPACE-to-mortality translation ('2-3% slower pace of aging = 10-15% lower mortality risk = smoking cessation') is imported from DunedinPACE's observational validation cohorts. No mortality was measured in CALERIE. This is a surrogate-to-outcome conversion presented as if it were a trial finding.
- Species and mechanism tiers are silently merged for TRE. The rodent literature does show timing effects independent of calories; the human isocaloric trials (Maruthur 2024, TREATY, Peters 2025) do not. Popular claims cite the rodent mechanism and human popularity together as a single evidence base.
- Selecting DunedinPACE from among five measured clocks is a multiplicity problem that is never priced in. Reporting 'CR slowed the pace of aging' without stating that PhenoAge, GrimAge, Horvath and Hannum were all null — and that PhenoAge and GrimAge point estimates were in the wrong direction at 24 months — inverts the strength of the finding.
- Colman 2014's abstract describes onset as 'since young adulthood' whereas Colman 2009 states animals were 7-14 year old adults at entry. Minor internal inconsistency, but age at onset is precisely the variable the 2017 reconciliation identifies as decisive, so loose language here matters.
- TREAT's '65% of weight lost was lean mass' is computed on a within-arm loss of only 1.70 kg total in a trial whose primary weight outcome was null. A percentage of a small, statistically indistinguishable quantity is being reported as though it were a robust physiological finding.

**证据缺口(搜过但不存在):**
- THE CENTRAL GAP: no CR, fasting, or time-restricted-eating trial in a healthy or general population anywhere in the world has mortality or incident age-related disease as a primary endpoint. I verified this systematically rather than by assertion: scanning all 1,472 studies on ClinicalTrials.gov matching CR/fasting/TRE terms and regex-matching primary outcomes for mortality/death/survival/MACE/incident disease returned only 9 hits, every one in a disease population (oncology, pre-surgical, renal). The whole human longevity case rests on surrogates.
- No human CR or fasting trial has ever measured a hard clinical endpoint at all, not merely as primary. The BMJ 2025 network meta-analysis of 99 trials and 6,582 adults contains no clinical outcomes whatsoever — only weight, glucose, lipids, blood pressure, CRP and liver markers. The Chen 2025 authors state the same for TRE specifically: all published RCTs studied risk-factor change within 1 year, and long-term RCTs on hard endpoints 'are currently unavailable and will be challenging to conduct in the future.'
- CALERIE never reported results against its own registered primary endpoints as a headline finding. Core body temperature was null and RMR was significant at 12 months but not 24, yet no prominent publication or press communication frames CALERIE as a trial that missed its primary outcomes. The negative primary result exists in Ravussin 2015 and then effectively disappears from the discourse.
- No long-term follow-up data exist for CALERIE participants. The CALERIE Legacy study (NCT05651620) has estimated primary completion 2027-01-31 and completion 2028-01-31 — and even it uses Klemera-Doubal biological age and 'healthspan' as primary endpoints rather than mortality or incident disease. The question 'did two years of CR help these people over 15 years?' has no answer yet and will not have a clean one.
- No trial has ever tested the actual 25% restriction that CALERIE was designed to test. Human adherence capped the delivered dose at ~12%, so the dose-response above 12% in humans is entirely unmeasured. Whether benefits scale, plateau, or reverse at higher restriction in humans is unknown.
- There is no long-term human data on the bone cost. Villareal 2016 documents significant BMD loss at spine, hip and femoral neck over 2 years and calls it 'a potential limitation of prolonged CR for extending life span' — but no study has followed CR practitioners long enough to see whether that translates into fracture incidence. A trial reporting a fracture endpoint does not exist.
- No randomized trial has tested whether intermittent fasting causes or worsens disordered eating. The entire harm literature is observational and self-report (e.g. Ganson 2022), leaving reverse causation unresolved — and no TRE or fasting RCT that I found screens for or reports eating-disorder outcomes as a safety endpoint, despite ~40-50% population uptake of IF in the surveyed young-adult cohorts.
- The BMJ 2025 network meta-analysis does not appear to report a direct TRE-versus-continuous-energy-restriction pooled estimate in its reachable text, despite that being the single most decision-relevant comparison in the field. Non-superiority has to be inferred indirectly from each arm's contrast against ad libitum. The direct league-table estimate should be checked in the supplement.
- No replication exists of the specific TREAT lean-mass alarm. A 65%-lean-mass-loss signal from a 12-week remote trial has never been reproduced with a gold-standard body-composition method (D3-creatine dilution or MRI) in a trial with measured protein intake — despite it being the most-cited safety concern about TRE for six years.
- No head-to-head primate study has ever been run under the design the 2017 reconciliation implies is necessary: CR versus a genuinely ad libitum, low-sucrose, healthy control diet, with onset age randomized. The reconciliation diagnosed the confound and no one has since resolved it experimentally — and given ~25-year primate study durations, no one will.
- No independent group has published a reanalysis of the FMD biological-age data using the parent trials' randomized control arms. The between-group contrast that would test the 2.5-year claim properly is computable from data the authors hold, and it has not been reported.
- I found no formal published critique, letter, or correction addressing the Brandhorst 2024 FMD paper's within-group design — searched specifically and found none. The methodological objection to Klemera-Doubal 'age reversal' claims exists only in the general methods literature, never applied to this paper in print. A high-profile commercial claim has gone two years without documented peer challenge.

## L2 — Rapamycin / rapalogs: the human and companion-animal evidence

### L2-01 **[承重]**

MECHANISM (primary). The target of rapamycin was identified genetically in yeast, not mammals: a screen for rapamycin-resistant Saccharomyces cerevisiae mutants identified FKBP12 as rapamycin's direct binding partner and two novel genes, TOR1 and TOR2, as required for rapamycin toxicity. Rapamycin therefore does not inhibit mTOR directly — the rapamycin–FKBP12 complex is the inhibitory species. It inhibits mTORC1 acutely; mTORC2 inhibition requires chronic exposure and is cell-type dependent. This mTORC1-vs-mTORC2 distinction is the entire pharmacological basis of the 'intermittent dosing avoids side effects' hypothesis and of the next-generation rapalog companies. Species: yeast. Tier 1 (not even animal — fungal).

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Heitman J, Movva NR, Hall MN. Targets for cell cycle arrest by the immunosuppressant rapamycin in yeast. Science. 1991;253(5022):905-909.
- URL:https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.1715094
- 逐字引:> Could not reach the 1991 paywalled primary text directly; content confirmed via multiple secondary descriptions including the JCI Insight interview with Heitman and Movva (insight.jci.org/articles/view/99816): the authors 'devised a genetic screen to isolate drug resistant mutants of Saccharomyces cerevisiae' and identified 'FKBP12 as the direct binding partner for rapamycin and two previously undescribed proteins, which they designated TOR1 and TOR2 (for target of rapamycin), as essential for rapamycin toxicity.'
- 利益相关:Movva was at Sandoz (later Novartis), the company that developed rapamycin analogues. Not a conflict affecting the 1991 finding, but it is the origin of the Novartis rapalog franchise that runs through this entire line.

### L2-02 **[承重]**

PEARL PRIMARY ENDPOINT WAS NOT MET, AND NOT NARROWLY. The registered primary outcome of the PEARL trial (NCT04488601) was change in visceral fat by DXA. Population: 114 completers aged 50-85 in relatively good health (5 mg/week n=40, 10 mg/week n=35, placebo n=39), 48 weeks, decentralised double-blind RCT. The effect size was ηp2 = 0.001 — indistinguishable from zero, not a near-miss. This is human, surrogate-endpoint (Tier 2) evidence, and it is negative on its own pre-specified question.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Moel M, Harinath G, Lee V, Nyquist A, Morgan SL, Isman A, Zalzala S. Influence of rapamycin on safety and healthspan metrics after one year: PEARL trial results. Aging (Albany NY). 2025;17(4):908-936. doi:10.18632/aging.206235. Published 2025-04-04.
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12074816/
- 逐字引:> "Visceral adiposity did not change significantly (ηp2 = 0.001, p = 0.942)"
- 利益相关:Sponsor is AgelessRx, a telehealth company that sells off-label rapamycin. Paper's COI statement: "GH, VL, AN, MM, SM, AI, and SZ are employees and shareholders of AgelessRx." Funding: "Funding for the study came primarily from donors via a Lifespan.io crowdfunding effort, with significant donations from Vitalik Buterin, Micah Zoltu, Brad Armstrong, and anonymous gifts. Administrative and trial support, article publishing charges, and other fees were provided by AgelessRx."

### L2-03 **[承重]**

PEARL'S POSITIVE FINDING RESTS ON ROUGHLY SEVEN WOMEN. The trial's headline positive result — lean tissue mass improvement in women on 10 mg/week (48-week md = 6.194, 95% CI 0.8773-11.5105, p = 0.018; 24-week md = 3.60472, 95% CI 0.0913-7.1182, p = 0.043) — comes from a subgroup in which women were 35.1% of the whole cohort and only 20% of the 10 mg arm (n=35), i.e. approximately 7 women. Both confidence intervals nearly touch zero. Self-reported pain in the same subgroup: md = 8.071 (95% CI 3.044-13.098), p<0.001. The paper reports only within-model corrections (Greenhouse-Geisser, Welch, Bonferroni/Games-Howell) and states no family-wise correction across the many secondary outcomes × 2 doses × 2 sexes × 2 timepoints examined, and the sex-stratified analyses were not pre-registered.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Moel M, et al. Aging (Albany NY). 2025;17(4):908-936. doi:10.18632/aging.206235.
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12074816/
- 逐字引:> "Data were analyzed using tests as described in the text, with relevant corrections for sphericity (Greenhouse-Geisser), homogeneity of variance (Welch's test), and multiple comparisons (Bonferroni or Games-Howell correction), unless otherwise noted." And on the cohort: "our cohort demographics showed relatively few women and predominantly health-conscious participants"
- 利益相关:AgelessRx employees/shareholders are the authors; AgelessRx sells rapamycin.

### L2-04 **[承重]**

PEARL DID NOT MEASURE BLOOD RAPAMYCIN LEVELS, AND USED A COMPOUNDED FORMULATION THAT THE SAME GROUP LATER SHOWED IS ~3x LESS BIOAVAILABLE. PEARL used compounded rapamycin from Belmar Pharma Solutions at 5 or 10 mg/week and did not measure participants' blood rapamycin concentrations. A subsequent paper by the same AgelessRx group measured 24-hour post-dose blood levels in real-world users (compounded n=23 at 5/10/15 mg; commercial n=44 at 2/4/6/8 mg; plus an observational cohort n=316) and found compounded rapamycin delivers 0.27 ng/mL per mg vs 0.87 ng/mL per mg for commercial. Consequence: PEARL's top dose of 10 mg/week compounded corresponds to roughly 3 mg/week commercial-equivalent systemic exposure — materially below the median 6 mg/week (mostly commercial) that off-label users take. PEARL's null primary endpoint AND its reassuring safety result both apply to a lower exposure than the drug is actually used at.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Harinath G, Lee V, Nyquist A, et al. The bioavailability and blood levels of low-dose rapamycin for longevity in real-world cohorts of normative aging individuals. GeroScience. 2025;47(4):5681-5694. doi:10.1007/s11357-025-01532-w. (PEARL formulation: Moel M, et al. Aging. 2025;17(4):908-936.)
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12397450/
- 逐字引:> From the PK paper: "compounded rapamycin (mean = 0.27 ng/mL per 1 mg) is 31.03% (3 ×) less bioavailable per milligram than commercial formulations (mean = 0.87 ng/mL per 1 mg)" and "routine measurement of blood rapamycin levels at standardized time points and dose personalization are likely the most reliable strategies for optimizing longevity dosing". From PEARL: "Rapamycin used in this study was a compounded formulation of 5 mg or 10 mg, received from Belmar Pharma Solutions (Golden, CO, USA)." and PEARL's own acknowledgment that "compounded rapamycin did indeed have approximately ⅓ the concentration in blood after 24 hrs relative to commercial".
- 利益相关:Both papers: "GH, VL, AN, MM, BV, CT, SN, SLM, AI, and SZ are employees and shareholders of AgelessRx." AgelessRx dispenses compounded rapamycin commercially.

### L2-05 **[承重]**

PEARL WAS ANALYSED AS COMPLETERS ONLY, AND ITS RESULTS HAVE NEVER BEEN POSTED TO THE REGISTRY. The published analysis covers 114 completers; 11 additional participants discontinued (placebo 6, 10 mg 3, 5 mg 2), giving 125 accounted for, while ClinicalTrials.gov lists actual enrollment of 129 — a 4-participant discrepancy that is unexplained. This is a completers-only, not intention-to-treat, analysis. The registry record shows hasResults = No despite primary completion on 2023-12-30.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:ClinicalTrials.gov, NCT04488601, Participatory Evaluation (of) Aging (With) Rapamycin (for) Longevity Study; sponsor AgelessRx, collaborator UCLA. Cross-referenced with Moel M, et al. Aging. 2025;17(4):908-936.
- URL:https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT04488601
- 逐字引:> Registry primary outcome: "Changes in visceral fat as measured by dual-energy x-ray absorptiometry (DXA) scan"; Actual enrollment: 129; Results Posted: No; Primary completion: December 30, 2023. Paper: "A total of 114 participants completed the study and were included in data analysis. An additional 11 discontinued participation prior to study completion."
- 利益相关:Sponsor AgelessRx sells the drug tested.

### L2-06 **[承重]**

THE 2014 MANNICK STUDY IS THE ORIGIN OF THE FAMOUS '20%' NUMBER, AND ITS CALIBER IS MUCH NARROWER THAN ITS REPUTATION. 218 volunteers aged ≥65 without unstable medical disease, 9 sites in New Zealand and Australia, Dec 2011-Apr 2012; 211 completed, 199 in the ITT population. Arms: everolimus (RAD001) 0.5 mg daily (N=53), 5 mg weekly (N=53), 20 mg weekly (N=53), pooled placebo (N=59). Six weeks of drug, 2-week washout, then 2012 seasonal influenza vaccine; HI titres at baseline and 4 weeks post-vaccination. The '20% improvement' is exactly the pre-specified ≥1.2-fold geometric mean titre ratio versus placebo — a relative increase in antibody titre on a log scale, four weeks after one vaccination. It is not a reduction in influenza illness, hospitalisation, or anything a patient experiences. Tier 2 surrogate, six weeks, everolimus not rapamycin.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Mannick JB, Del Giudice G, Lattanzi M, Valiante NM, Praestgaard J, Huang B, Lonetto MA, Maecker HT, Kovarik J, Carson S, Glass DJ, Klickstein LB. mTOR inhibition improves immune function in the elderly. Sci Transl Med. 2014 Dec 24;6(268):268ra179.
- URL:https://health.uconn.edu/aging/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2016/04/mTOR-inhibition-improves-immune-function-in-the-elderly.pdf
- 逐字引:> "The study was powered to be able to demonstrate that in at least two of three influenza vaccine strains, there was a ≥1.2-fold GMT ratio increase relative to placebo and a posterior probability of at least 80% that the placebo-corrected GMT ratio exceeded 1.0." And: "RAD001 enhanced the response to the influenza vaccine by about 20% at doses that were relatively well tolerated."
- 利益相关:All authors affiliated to Novartis Institutes for BioMedical Research, Novartis Vaccines and Diagnostics, or Novartis Pharmaceuticals; corresponding author email joan.mannick@novartis.com. RAD001/everolimus is a Novartis product. Novartis-designed, Novartis-funded, Novartis-authored.

### L2-07 **[承重]**

THE 2014 STUDY'S THRESHOLD FOR 'SIGNIFICANCE' WAS AN 80% BAYESIAN POSTERIOR PROBABILITY, NOT p<0.05 — AND THE AUTHORS SAID SO. The primary analysis was a Bayesian normal regression model with noninformative priors; the endpoint required only an 80% posterior probability that the placebo-corrected GMT ratio exceeded 1.0. The authors explicitly labelled the study exploratory with a liberal threshold. Separately, the immunophenotyping used a false discovery rate threshold of q<0.20 across 76 tests (the widely-cited PD-1+ CD4 T cell decrease at week 6 was P=0.03, q=0.13). Only 2 of 3 dose arms met the primary endpoint; the 20 mg weekly arm did not — a non-monotonic dose response.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Mannick JB, et al. Sci Transl Med. 2014;6(268):268ra179.
- URL:https://health.uconn.edu/aging/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2016/04/mTOR-inhibition-improves-immune-function-in-the-elderly.pdf
- 逐字引:> "Finally, this was an exploratory proof-of-concept study that used a liberal preplanned threshold for significance (80% posterior probability of an effect over placebo)." And: "In the intent-to-treat population, the low-dose RAD001 (0.5 mg daily or 5 mg weekly) cohorts, but not the higher-dose (20 mg weekly) cohort, met the primary endpoint of the study." And on FDR: "We rejected all null hypotheses at 0.05 significance level and q value less than 20%."
- 利益相关:Novartis employees throughout.

### L2-08 **[承重]**

THE HARDER SURROGATE IN THE 2014 STUDY — SEROCONVERSION — DID NOT REACH SIGNIFICANCE IN THE ITT POPULATION. Seroconversion rates for H3N2 and B strains were numerically increased on drug but not statistically significant in the intent-to-treat analysis. Significance appeared only in the subgroup with low baseline titres (≤1:40), and there only for the B strain in the 0.5 mg daily arm and for the heterologous H3N2 strain (pooled RAD001 39% vs placebo 20%, P=0.007). The trial thus met a soft continuous endpoint and missed the categorical one in its primary population.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Mannick JB, et al. Sci Transl Med. 2014;6(268):268ra179, Tables 2 and 3.
- URL:https://health.uconn.edu/aging/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2016/04/mTOR-inhibition-improves-immune-function-in-the-elderly.pdf
- 逐字引:> "In the intent-to-treat population, seroconversion rates for the H3N2 and B influenza strains were increased in the RAD001-treated cohorts as compared to the placebo cohorts, although the increases did not meet statistical significance (Table 2)."
- 利益相关:Novartis employees throughout.

### L2-09 

ADVERSE EVENTS IN THE 2014 STUDY WERE ROUGHLY DOUBLE PLACEBO, AND MOUTH ULCERS OCCURRED EVEN AT THE LOWEST DOSE. Subjects with adverse events: 41.5% (0.5 mg daily), 37.7% (5 mg weekly), 50.9% (20 mg weekly) versus 20.3% on placebo. Mouth ulceration: 11.3% / 3.8% / 17.0% versus 5.1% placebo (20/218 = 9.2% overall). Increased blood cholesterol 3.8% in all three drug arms versus 0% on placebo. Neutropenia (5.7%) and thrombocytopenia (3.8%) occurred only in the 20 mg weekly arm. Six weeks of exposure, ages ≥65.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Mannick JB, et al. Sci Transl Med. 2014;6(268):268ra179, Table 1.
- URL:https://health.uconn.edu/aging/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2016/04/mTOR-inhibition-improves-immune-function-in-the-elderly.pdf
- 逐字引:> "Nonetheless, significant numbers of subjects in our study developed mouth ulcers even when dosed as low as 0.5 mg RAD001 daily, although most mouth ulcers were transient and of mild severity. Development of mTOR inhibitors with cleaner safety profiles than the currently available rapalogs may provide better therapeutic options for boosting immune responses in the elderly in the future."
- 利益相关:Novartis employees throughout.

### L2-10 **[承重]**

THE DECISIVE RESULT IN THIS FIELD: RTB101 PHASE 3 FAILED IN 1024 PATIENTS, WITH THE POINT ESTIMATE ON THE WRONG SIDE OF 1. Phase 3 (PROTECTOR 1) enrolled 1024 adults aged ≥65, non-smokers without COPD, across NZ/AU/USA, 2018-05-07 to 2019-07-19; RTB101 10 mg once daily (n=513) vs placebo (n=510) for 16 weeks. Primary outcome (changed at FDA request to clinically symptomatic respiratory illness, with or without laboratory confirmation): 134/511 (26%) on RTB101 versus 125/510 (25%) on placebo; odds ratio 1.07 (90% CI 0.80-1.42), p=0.65. Crucially, the mechanism biomarker DID work in the same trial — interferon-induced antiviral genes were significantly upregulated on drug. The pathway engaged and the clinical endpoint did not move. This is the single cleanest demonstration in geroscience that a validated mTOR surrogate does not deliver a clinical outcome.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Mannick JB, Teo G, Bernardo P, et al. Targeting the biology of ageing with mTOR inhibitors to improve immune function in older adults: phase 2b and phase 3 randomised trials. Lancet Healthy Longev. 2021;2(5):e250-e262. doi:10.1016/S2666-7568(21)00062-3. PMID 33977284.
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/33977284/
- 逐字引:> "In the full analysis set, RTB101 did not reduce clinically symptomatic respiratory illness (134 [26%] of 511 RTB101 group versus 125 [25%] of 510 placebo group; odds ratio 1.07 [90% CI 0.80-1.42]; p=0.65)." And: "Both trials showed significantly upregulated interferon-induced antiviral genes in RTB101-treated patients compared with placebo."
- 利益相关:Funding, verbatim: "resTORbio and National Institute on Aging." Authors include resTORbio employees; RTB101 was resTORbio's lead asset, in-licensed from Novartis.

### L2-11 **[承重]**

THE PHASE 2b RESULT THAT LAUNCHED RTB101 IS QUOTED WITH A 90% CONFIDENCE INTERVAL AND CAME FROM A SECOND ANALYSIS AFTER THE FIRST FAILED. Phase 2b enrolled 652 adults aged 65-85 WITH elevated RTI risk (asthma, T2D, COPD, CHF, current smokers, or recent RTI hospitalisation). The FIRST prespecified efficacy analysis, of RTB101 10 mg plus everolimus 0.1 mg (part 2), did not reach statistical significance. The famous positive number came from a SECOND prespecified analysis pooling parts 1 and 2: 34/176 (19%) with ≥1 laboratory-confirmed RTI on RTB101 10 mg once daily versus 50/180 (28%) on pooled placebo; OR 0.601, 90% CI 0.391-0.922, p=0.02. At a conventional 95% CI the interval would cross 1. The 90% CI is almost never disclosed when this result is cited.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Mannick JB, Teo G, Bernardo P, et al. Lancet Healthy Longev. 2021;2(5):e250-e262. PMID 33977284.
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/33977284/
- 逐字引:> "First prespecified efficacy analysis of part 2 showed RTB101 10 mg plus everolimus did not reach statistical significance. Second prespecified analysis combining parts 1 and 2 demonstrated statistically significant reduction in patients with ≥1 laboratory-confirmed RTI in RTB101 10 mg once daily group (34 [19%] of 176) versus pooled placebo (50 [28%] of 180); odds ratio 0.601 (90% CI 0.391-0.922); p=0.02."
- 利益相关:Funded by resTORbio, the company whose lead asset was RTB101, plus NIA.

### L2-12 

AN INDEPENDENT COMMENTARY IDENTIFIED THE TWO DESIGN CHANGES BETWEEN PHASE 2b AND PHASE 3, INCLUDING THAT THE FDA FORCED THE ENDPOINT CHANGE. The population changed from high-RTI-risk (COPD, smokers, diabetics) to lower-risk non-smokers without COPD; and the primary endpoint changed from laboratory-confirmed RTI to clinically symptomatic respiratory illness, because the FDA judged laboratory confirmation not relevant to how patients feel and function. This is a genuinely independent source: the commentary authors declare no industry interests.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Dzankic PJ, Partridge L. Clinical trials of mTOR inhibitors to boost immunity to viral infection in older adults. Lancet Healthy Longev. 2021;2(5):e232-e233. doi:10.1016/S2666-7568(21)00090-8. PMID 33977282.
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC8102037/
- 逐字引:> "The US Food and Drug Administration requested a change in primary endpoint between the phase 2b and phase 3 trials because of concerns that laboratory confirmation of an infection was not relevant to how patients feel and function." And: "Compared with the phase 2b trial, the phase 3 trial was thus done in patients who were at lower overall risk of RTIs and with a primary endpoint that was less clearly linked to underlying immune function."
- 利益相关:Declared: "LP reports a grant from European Research Council, outside the submitted work. PJ-D declares no competing interests." No industry ties — this is one of the few genuinely independent voices on the record.

### L2-13 **[承重]**

WHAT HAPPENED AFTER THE FAILURE: resTORbio CEASED TO BE AN AGING COMPANY WITHIN SIX MONTHS. On 2019-11-15 resTORbio announced PROTECTOR 1 missed its primary endpoint (risk of clinically symptomatic respiratory illness 0.46 on RTB101 vs 0.44 on placebo) and stopped development in the indication; the stock fell approximately 74%. The remaining RTB101 programme in Parkinson's disease was terminated without enrolling its fifth cohort (announced 2020-04-03, attributed to New Zealand's COVID-19 level 4 alert). On 2020-04-28 resTORbio entered a reverse merger with Adicet Bio; the surviving company is a gamma-delta T-cell oncology company with no rapalog in its pipeline. RTB101 was abandoned entirely.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:resTORbio, Inc. press release, GlobeNewswire, 2019-11-15; resTORbio, Inc. Form 8-K, SEC filing, 2020-04-28 (CIK 1720580); resTORbio press release 2020-04-03 (Parkinson's cohort 5 postponement).
- URL:https://www.globenewswire.com/news-release/2019/11/15/1947932/0/en/resTORbio-Announces-That-the-Phase-3-PROTECTOR-1-Trial-of-RTB101-in-Clinically-Symptomatic-Respiratory-Illness-Did-Not-Meet-the-Primary-Endpoint.html
- 逐字引:> "While we are disappointed in these results, there are extensive preclinical data supporting the potential therapeutic benefit of TORC1 inhibition in multiple aging-related diseases" — and the company "has stopped the development of RTB101 in this indication." Reported numbers: 1024 patients randomized 1:1; "the risk of having a CSRI was 0.46 in subjects treated with RTB101 versus 0.44 in those on placebo."
- 利益相关:Company self-disclosure of its own failure — the direction of bias here runs against the company, which makes it credible. Note the framing move in the quote: pivoting immediately from a failed human phase 3 back to 'extensive preclinical data' — a Tier 3 failure answered with Tier 1 evidence.

### L2-14 

AS OF 2026 THE FIELD'S MOST EXPERIENCED RAPALOG DEVELOPER NO LONGER DEVELOPS RAPALOGS, AND HER RAPALOG NEVER REACHED HUMANS. Joan Mannick's path: Genzyme → seven years at Novartis → resTORbio → Life Biosciences → co-founder and CEO of Tornado Therapeutics (a Cambrian Bio 'PipeCo' developing selective mTORC1 inhibitors licensed from Novartis, lead asset TOR-101). In January 2025 Mannick had stated TOR-101 was expected to enter trials in 2025; as of mid-2026 TOR-101's highest global R&D status is still preclinical and no phase 1 initiation has been announced.

### L2-15 **[承重]**

THE MOST-CITED DOG CARDIAC RESULT IS 15 DOGS ON DRUG FOR 10 WEEKS, UNCORRECTED, WITH THE TWO STATISTICAL MODELS DISAGREEING. 24 middle-aged companion dogs (≥6 years, ≥18 kg, mean age 9.7 ± 1.84 y): placebo n=8, rapamycin 0.05 mg/kg n=5 completers, 0.1 mg/kg n=10 completers, dosed three times weekly for 10 weeks. Echocardiography pooled rapamycin vs placebo: fractional shortening F(2,20)=5.05, P=0.036; E/A ratio significant by Wilcoxon (W=93.5, P=0.029) but NOT by the linear model; ejection fraction P=0.058, not significant. No correction for multiple comparisons. Mean corpuscular volume significantly decreased on drug. This is Tier 1 companion-animal evidence, hypothesis-generating only, and the authors said so.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Urfer SR, Kaeberlein TL, Mailheau S, Bergman PJ, Creevy KE, Promislow DEL, Kaeberlein M. A randomized controlled trial to establish effects of short-term rapamycin treatment in 24 middle-aged companion dogs. GeroScience. 2017;39(2):117-127. doi:10.1007/s11357-017-9972-z. PMID 28374166.
