### L2-16 **[承重]**

TRIAD IS THE ONLY TRIAL IN THE ENTIRE RAPAMYCIN LITERATURE WITH A HARD CLINICAL OUTCOME AS ITS PRIMARY ENDPOINT — AND IT WILL NOT REPORT BEFORE ~2029. TRIAD (Test of Rapamycin in Aging Dogs) is a prospective, parallel-group, double-masked, randomised, placebo-controlled multicentre trial. Primary objective: whether rapamycin extends LIFESPAN in healthy, middle-aged, medium-to-large companion dogs. Design: rapamycin or identical placebo by mouth once weekly for 1 year; each dog participates 3 years in-trial plus 2 years post-trial monitoring; recheck visits at 6/12/18/24/30/36 months. Eligibility: dogs ≥7 years, ≥44 lb (20 kg), apparently healthy, no diabetes/heart disease/cancer/infection. Approximately 20 clinical sites. Status as of 2026: still recruiting, 235 of a 580-dog target enrolled (~41%); enrollment aimed to complete end of 2026; trial slated to conclude November 2029.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Coleman LA, Creevy KE, et al. Test of Rapamycin in Aging Dogs (TRIAD): study design and rationale for a prospective, parallel-group, double-masked, randomized, placebo-controlled, multicenter trial of rapamycin in healthy middle-aged dogs from the Dog Aging Project. GeroScience. 2025;47(3):2851-2877. doi:10.1007/s11357-024-01484-7. Enrollment status per dogagingproject.org/triad and Tufts Cummings School TRIAD clinical trial page, 2026.
- URL:https://link.springer.com/article/10.1007/s11357-024-01484-7
- 逐字引:> "This is the first rigorous test of a pharmacologic intervention against biological aging with lifespan and healthspan metrics as endpoints to be performed outside of the laboratory in any species." Enrollment: "So far, 235 dogs have been enrolled in the TRIAD study. The goal is to enroll 580 dogs in total." Tufts site lists trial status as "Recruiting."
- 利益相关:Dog Aging Project investigators (Kaeberlein, Promislow, Creevy) have publicly advocated rapamycin geroprotection for a decade; the project's continued NIH funding depends on TRIAD. Kaeberlein is now CEO of Optispan, a longevity company.

### L2-17 

THE 2024 DOG AGING PROJECT FUNDING CRISIS NEARLY LEFT TRIAD PERMANENTLY UNDERPOWERED. The Dog Aging Project's ~$29M five-year NIA grant was not renewed; funding expired June 2024, threatening up to 90% of the annual budget. Founders said they needed to raise $40-50M. A partial rescue came in December 2024 in the form of an NIH/NIA award to Texas A&M investigators to expand TRIAD — reported as $7 million by Texas A&M and in the AVMA headline, but described in the AVMA body text as $5 million over five years with only the first-year portion received and subsequent years uncertain. These two figures are not reconciled in any source reachable.

- 来源类型:media-secondhand
- 出处:STAT News, 'As NIH grant runs out, Dog Aging Project founders look to raise up to $50 million', 2024-01-24; Texas A&M College of Veterinary Medicine & Biomedical Sciences press release, 'Dog Aging Project Receives $7M To Expand Trial Of Rapamycin', 2024-12-09; AVMA News, '$7M grant rescues dog study investigating rapamycin for canine aging', 2025-02-26.
- URL:https://www.statnews.com/2024/01/24/dog-aging-project-founders-on-losing-nih-funding/
- 逐字引:> Kaeberlein to STAT: "The trial would end only half-powered. So it is very unlikely that we would be able to actually detect an effect statistically, even if the clinical trial worked." AVMA: the original "five-year, $29 million grant from the National Institute on Aging" was not renewed in 2024; the replacement is described as "$5 million over five years" with "first-year portion received; subsequent years' funding noted as uncertain."
- 利益相关:Institutional press releases from the grant recipients have an incentive to state the largest headline figure. The STAT quote is a direct quote from a principal investigator arguing for his own funding.

### L2-18 **[承重]**

THE SURVEY THAT IS USED TO ARGUE OFF-LABEL RAPAMYCIN IS SAFE IS SELF-SELECTED, UNMATCHED, AND SURVIVOR-BIASED — BY THE AUTHORS' OWN ADMISSION. 333 rapamycin users vs 172 never-users (505 completing the full survey), collected March-June 2022, recruited from the Rapamycin Users Facebook page, the Rapamycin News website, prescribing physicians' email lists, and word of mouth. Users were mean age ~61 and 78% male; non-users mean age 52-56 and 63% male — the comparison groups differ by 5-9 years and by sex composition. Regimens: 88-92% once-weekly, median weekly dose 6 mg (range 1-20 mg), median duration 218 days; only 4 participants dosed daily. Only mouth sores was significantly more prevalent in users after FDR adjustment.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Kaeberlein TL, Green AS, Haddad G, et al. Evaluation of off-label rapamycin use to promote healthspan in 333 adults. GeroScience. 2023 May 16;45(5):2757-2768. doi:10.1007/s11357-023-00818-1.
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10187519/
- 逐字引:> "Participants were recruited to the study by direct email from participating physicians, through social media posts, via targeted recruitment within rapamycin user groups such as the Rapamycin Users Facebook page and the Rapamycin News website (https://www.rapamycin.news/), word of mouth, and the study website." Limitations: "This study has several limitations that make the data less reliable than what would be obtained from a double-blind, randomized clinical trial... In particular, we cannot rule out the possibility that the population of rapamycin users is self-selected against people who started taking rapamycin and stopped because of negative experiences."
- 利益相关:Declared: "MK and GH are co-founders of Optispan Geroscience... ASG and BSR are practicing physicians who may prescribe rapamycin off-label for certain patients when appropriate. AI, AN, and SZ are employees and/or shareholders of AgelessRX, a longevity-focused telemedicine platform that sponsors several clinical trials, including interventional and observational efforts for the repurposed use of rapamycin."

### L2-19 **[承重]**

THE SURVEY'S OWN INTERNAL TELL: IT MANUFACTURED SIX IMPLAUSIBLE BENEFITS, WHICH CALIBRATES HOW MUCH CONFOUNDING IS IN THE SAFETY CONCLUSION. The same dataset reported that abdominal cramps, depression, abdominal pain, muscle tightness, anxiety and eye pain were all SIGNIFICANTLY LESS FREQUENT in rapamycin users than non-users. No mechanism makes weekly mTOR inhibition reduce eye pain, anxiety and depression. These are healthy-user and reporting-bias artefacts. A design that generates six spurious benefits at conventional significance can equally conceal harms of similar magnitude — which is precisely what the study is cited to rule out. This is the strongest available demonstration that self-report longevity surveys cannot support safety claims.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Kaeberlein TL, et al. GeroScience. 2023;45(5):2757-2768. doi:10.1007/s11357-023-00818-1.
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10187519/
- 逐字引:> "Several conditions were significantly less frequent in rapamycin users compared to non-users: abdominal cramps, depression, abdominal pain, muscle tightness, anxiety, and eye pain." Conclusion: "Taken together, these observations support further study of rapamycin as a potential intervention to improve quality, and perhaps quantity, of life in otherwise healthy older adults."
- 利益相关:Authors include AgelessRx employees/shareholders and physicians who prescribe rapamycin off-label.

### L2-20 **[承重]**

THE MOST IMPORTANT NEW HUMAN RAPAMYCIN RESULT (2026) IS NEGATIVE, AND IT TESTED THE INTERMITTENT-DOSING HYPOTHESIS DIRECTLY. RAPA-EX-01: 40 sedentary adults aged 65-85 (mean 72.2 y, 47.5% female) randomised 1:1 to sirolimus 6 mg once weekly (commercial tablets) or matched placebo for 13 weeks, alongside a standardised home-based exercise programme 3×/week. The dose was deliberately timed to day 6 of the training week, ~24 h after the last session, to test the 'cycling' hypothesis that weekly dosing spares muscle adaptation. Primary outcome, change in 30-second chair-stand repetitions: adjusted mean difference (sirolimus − placebo) = −2.13 repetitions, 95% CI −4.61 to 0.34, p = 0.089 — not significant, direction favouring PLACEBO. Sensitivity analyses were significant AGAINST the drug: complete-case −2.46 reps, p=0.045; per-protocol p=0.007. All secondaries trended toward placebo (6-min walk −4.87 m, p=0.706; grip strength −1.19 kg, p=0.344).

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Stanfield B, Kaeberlein M, Leroux B, et al. Exercise and Weekly Sirolimus (Rapamycin) in Older Adults: RAPA-EX-01 Randomised, Double-Blind, Placebo-Controlled Trial. J Cachexia Sarcopenia Muscle. 2026. doi:10.1002/jcsm.70274. Protocol: Trials. 2024;25:642. doi:10.1186/s13063-024-08490-2.
- URL:https://onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/10.1002/jcsm.70274
- 逐字引:> From the published abstract: "The primary intention-to-treat analysis showed an adjusted mean difference (sirolimus–placebo) of −2.13 repetitions (95% CI −4.61 to 0.34; p = 0.089)." Conclusion: once-weekly 6-mg sirolimus "didn't enhance, and may have modestly attenuated, the short-term functional gains of a home exercise program in older adults." (Full text is paywalled at Wiley; abstract text and the sensitivity-analysis p-values of 0.045 complete-case and 0.007 per-protocol were obtained from the published abstract and from Peter Attia's detailed write-up of the paper.)
- 利益相关:Funded by Dr Brad Stanfield Ltd and Vitasang Ltd. Stanfield is a health-communication YouTuber who had publicly promoted the rapamycin-plus-exercise hypothesis and self-funded the trial; he declared no Pfizer affiliation and no financial incentive in the outcome. Notably the funder had a reputational stake in a POSITIVE result and published a negative one — which makes this null MORE credible than the field's vendor-funded positives, not less. Kaeberlein is senior collaborator.

### L2-21 **[承重]**

RAPA-EX-01 ALSO PRODUCED A METABOLIC SAFETY SIGNAL AT THE EXACT REGIMEN THAT IS SUPPOSED TO BE CLEAN. There was a statistically significant increase in HbA1c in the sirolimus arm at 6 mg once weekly over 13 weeks. Total adverse-event burden was 99 events on sirolimus versus 63 on placebo (though 17/20, or 85%, of participants in EACH arm reported at least one AE). One case of pneumonia occurred in the sirolimus arm. This matters because intermittent dosing is specifically claimed to avoid the metabolic effects seen at daily transplant dosing. It converges with Kraig et al 2018 (25 adults aged 70-95, 1 mg/day for 8 weeks, mean level 7.2 ng/mL), which reported increased triglycerides and increased HbA1c, decreased plasma albumin, and a near-significant (p=0.06) VLDL increase.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Stanfield B, Kaeberlein M, Leroux B, et al. J Cachexia Sarcopenia Muscle. 2026. doi:10.1002/jcsm.70274. And: Kraig E, Linehan LA, Liang H, et al. A randomized control trial to establish the feasibility and safety of rapamycin treatment in an older human cohort: Immunological, physical performance, and cognitive effects. Exp Gerontol. 2018;105:53-69. doi:10.1016/j.exger.2017.12.026.
- URL:https://peterattiamd.com/rapamycin-plus-exercise-trial/
- 逐字引:> RAPA-EX-01, per Attia's paper analysis: a "statistically significant increase in HbA1c in rapamycin group" and "One case of pneumonia in rapamycin arm"; total AE burden 99 (sirolimus) vs 63 (placebo). Kraig 2018, as characterised in the independent Hands et al 2025 review: "a significant decrease in plasma albumin, increased triglycerides and Hemoglobin A1C (HbA1C) and a near-significant (p=0.06) increase for very-low-density lipoprotein (VLDL) within rapamycin-treated subjects." I could not reach the Wiley or ScienceDirect full texts directly; both figures come from detailed secondary readings of the primary papers, not from the primary text itself.
- 利益相关:RAPA-EX-01 self-funded by Stanfield's company. Attia is a rapamycin-sympathetic physician who prescribes it and takes it, so his reporting of an adverse signal runs against his own prior.

### L2-22 **[承重]**

THE ONLY INDEPENDENT SYSTEMATIC REVIEW OF HUMAN RAPAMYCIN AGEING EVIDENCE FOUND 19 STUDIES TOTAL, AND ITS SAFETY REASSURANCE IS CONDITIONAL ON BEING HEALTHY. A search of five databases yielded 18,400 unique articles and 19 included studies. Rapamycin/rapalogs improved physiological parameters in the immune, cardiovascular and integumentary systems; NO significant effects were found on the endocrine, muscular or neurological systems; and effects on the respiratory, digestive, renal and reproductive systems were not assessed at all. In healthy individuals no serious adverse events were attributed to the drugs, BUT in individuals with ageing-related diseases there were increased numbers of infections and increases in total cholesterol, LDL cholesterol and triglycerides. Every endpoint in all 19 studies is a physiological parameter or surrogate — Tier 2.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Lee DJW, Hodzic Kuerec A, Maier AB. Targeting ageing with rapamycin and its derivatives in humans: a systematic review. Lancet Healthy Longev. 2024;5(2):e152-e162. doi:10.1016/S2666-7568(23)00258-1. PMID 38310895.
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/38310895/
- 逐字引:> "A search across five databases yielded 18 400 unique articles, resulting in 19 included studies. Rapamycin and its derivatives improved physiological parameters associated with ageing in the immune, cardiovascular, and integumentary systems of healthy individuals or individuals with ageing-related diseases. Overall, no significant effects on the endocrine, muscular, or neurological systems were found. The effects of rapamycin or its derivatives on the respiratory, digestive, renal, and reproductive systems were not assessed. No serious adverse events attributed to rapamycin and its derivatives were reported in healthy individuals; however, there were increased numbers of infections and increases in total cholesterol, LDL cholesterol, and triglycerides in individuals with ageing-related diseases. Future studies should assess the remaining unexamined systems and test the effects of long-term exposure to rapamycin and its derivatives."
- 利益相关:Academic authors (Healthy Longevity Translational Research Programme, National University of Singapore); no vendor involvement identified. This is one of the few independent syntheses available.

### L2-23 **[承重]**

AN INDEPENDENT, UNFUNDED 2025 REVIEW CONCLUDES THE HUMAN CASE IS UNPROVEN AND NAMES THE SPECIFIC HOLES. The authors identified 'less than a dozen known trials' of low-dose rapamycin or analogues in healthy participants, all assessing biomarkers (immune function, protein synthesis, haematologic parameters). They state there is no established dose-response curve for healthspan extension, insufficient human outcome data on cardiovascular effects, no completed neurocognitive trials, and no cancer-incidence outcome data in healthy non-immunocompromised cohorts. On biological-age clocks they note the PhenoAge analysis in this literature is a modelling exercise on published group-level data, and that clock-based claims are controversial.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Hands JM, Lustgarten MS, Frame LA, Rosen B. What is the clinical evidence to support off-label rapamycin therapy in healthy adults? Aging (Albany NY). 2025 Aug 7;17(8):2079-2088. doi:10.18632/aging.206300. PMID 40778880.
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12422820/
- 逐字引:> "Despite the preclinical evidence supporting the use of sirolimus to enhance mean and maximal lifespan, the data in humans has yet to establish that rapamycin, or its analogues, is a proven seno-therapeutic that can delay aging in healthy older adults." Also: "The clinical evidence evaluating low-dose rapamycin...in healthy participants is scant"; the evidence is "compelling, but not convincing at present"; "Human data have yet to demonstrate that rapamycin can extend mean or maximal lifespan"; "No established dose response curve for rapamycin with regard to health span extension"; on clocks, "These data are suggestive at best and are limited by lack of subject level observations" and "The subject of clocks in human aging is controversial."
- 利益相关:Declared: "The authors declare that they have no conflicts of interest." And: "No funding was solicited in the course of this paper." Notably published in Aging (Albany NY) — the same journal that published PEARL.

### L2-24 

THE VENDOR THAT RAN PEARL DESCRIBES ITS OWN NULL PRIMARY ENDPOINT WITHOUT SAYING IT WAS THE PRIMARY ENDPOINT. AgelessRx's public explainer of the PEARL results never states that the trial's registered primary outcome was missed. It reports the visceral fat null as a directional trend, leads with the safety conclusion, and separately calls rapamycin 'the gold standard for pharmacological interventions' — a characterisation supportable only from the ITP mouse literature, presented on a page that sells rapamycin to humans. Documented here as an exemplar of Tier 1 → Tier 3 promotion by a commercially interested party, not as evidence of anything about the drug.

- 来源类型:vendor-selfreport
- 出处:AgelessRx, 'Results of the PEARL Trial: An Expert Analysis', agelessrx.com (accessed 2026-07-28).
- URL:https://agelessrx.com/results-of-the-pearl-trial/
- 逐字引:> "Though there were no statistically significant differences to visceral fat by the end of the trial, those who took Rapamycin were more likely to lose visceral fat than those in the placebo group." And: "The PEARL trial successfully showed that Rapamycin is a safe and well-tolerated drug when taken at low doses." And: "Overall, the results from the PEARL trial were just as reaffirming as they were surprising."
- 利益相关:AgelessRx sponsored PEARL and sells compounded rapamycin off-label (entry pricing ~$65/month per its own 2026 marketing). This is the trial sponsor describing its own trial to prospective customers.

### L2-25 

THE FIRST TRIAL DESIGNED TO ANSWER THE DOSING QUESTION LAUNCHED IN MARCH 2026 AND WILL NOT REPORT FOR YEARS. UT Health San Antonio launched an NIA-funded randomised placebo-controlled trial of approximately 84 older adults comparing DAILY rapamycin versus INTERMITTENT rapamycin versus placebo, 6 months of treatment plus 6 months of follow-up, with immune and metabolic markers as the outcomes. PIs: Ellen Kraig PhD, Dean Kellogg Jr MD PhD, Brett Ginsburg PhD. No NCT number, no specific doses and no primary endpoint were disclosed in the launch announcement. Note that even this trial — the first head-to-head test of the intermittent-dosing hypothesis — uses surrogate biomarker endpoints, not clinical outcomes.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:UT Health San Antonio, 'Large rapamycin clinical trial launches at UT Health San Antonio', news.uthscsa.edu, published 2026-03-16.
- URL:https://news.uthscsa.edu/large-rapamycin-clinical-trial-launches-at-ut-health-san-antonio/
- 逐字引:> Ellen Kraig, PhD: "Rapamycin is widely discussed in popular culture as a longevity drug. But there's a difference between something that is biologically plausible and something that has been rigorously tested in people." Design as described: "approximately 84 older adults who will receive either daily rapamycin, intermittent dosing or a placebo"; "testing different dosing schedules to determine how much rapamycin is needed to reach biological targets without negative side effects."
- 利益相关:NIA-funded academic trial; no vendor sponsor identified. This is the first non-vendor, non-advocate randomised rapamycin ageing trial in the human literature.

### L2-26 **[承重]**

THE FDA LABEL FOR THE DRUG BEING TAKEN OFF-LABEL CARRIES A BOXED WARNING FOR IMMUNOSUPPRESSION, INFECTION AND MALIGNANCY. Sirolimus (Rapamune) is approved only for prophylaxis of organ rejection in renal transplant, at DAILY dosing. The boxed warning covers immunosuppression with increased susceptibility to infection and possible development of lymphoma and other malignancies. The label additionally flags increased risk of abnormal wound healing (specifically for BMI >30), hyperlipidaemia requiring monitoring and NCEP-guideline intervention, hyperglycaemia and diabetes mellitus as adverse reactions, and increased skin-cancer risk. Every element of the longevity use case — the dose, the schedule, the population, the indication — sits outside this label.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:US Food and Drug Administration, RAPAMUNE (sirolimus) Oral Solution and Tablets, prescribing information, accessdata.fda.gov drugsatfda_docs.
- URL:https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/label/2013/021083s053,021110s067lbl.pdf
- 逐字引:> Boxed warning content as summarised from the label: increased susceptibility to infection and the possible development of lymphoma and other malignancies. On wound healing: "Patients with a body mass index (BMI) greater than 30 kg/m2 may be at increased risk of abnormal wound healing based on data from the medical literature." On lipids: "Any patient who is administered Rapamune should be monitored for hyperlipidemia, and if detected, interventions such as diet, exercise, and lipid-lowering agents should be initiated as outlined by the National Cholesterol Education Program guidelines." (I retrieved these via search-indexed extracts of the accessdata label PDFs; the 2017 revision PDF returned 404 on direct fetch. Verify the exact boxed-warning wording against the current label before publication.)
- 利益相关:None — regulatory document.

**交叉口径问题:**
- THE '20%' NUMBER IS A SHAPE-SHIFTER. It originates strictly as the ≥1.2-fold influenza geometric-mean-titre ratio vs placebo in Mannick 2014 — everolimus, not rapamycin; 6 weeks; ages ≥65; a Bayesian 80%-posterior threshold, not p<0.05; an antibody surrogate 4 weeks after one vaccination. It is routinely re-attached to rapamycin generally, to 'immune rejuvenation', and — in AI-generated and aggregator summaries encountered during this research — directly to the PEARL trial ('both low-dose regimens improved vaccine responses by 20 percent'). PEARL did not measure vaccine response at all. Any occurrence of '20%' in this field should be traced back to Mannick 2014 before use.
- 'RAPAMYCIN IMPROVES BONE MASS IN MEN' (PEARL) IS NOT IN THE PUBLISHED PAPER. This claim appears in press coverage of PEARL (e.g. nmn.com, and in search-engine summaries). The published Aging 2025 paper reports no significant bone outcomes and no significant improvement in men in either lean tissue or bone mineral content. This looks like preprint-to-publication drift or a directional trend reported as a result. Demand a p-value from anyone repeating it.
- THE RTB101 PHASE 2b RESULT IS UNIVERSALLY QUOTED WITHOUT ITS 90% CONFIDENCE INTERVAL. OR 0.601, 90% CI 0.391-0.922, p=0.02. A 90% CI is a materially lower bar than the conventional 95%; at 95% the interval would cross 1. Worse, this came from the SECOND prespecified analysis, pooling two separately-designed trial parts, after the FIRST prespecified analysis (the RTB101+everolimus arm) failed. Presented in most secondary literature as a clean positive phase 2.
- PEARL'S DOSE IS NOT THE COMMUNITY'S DOSE, AND NOBODY SAYS SO. PEARL used compounded rapamycin at 5/10 mg/week; the sponsor's own subsequent PK paper found compounded delivers 31% of commercial bioavailability per mg. PEARL's 10 mg/week is therefore ~3 mg/week commercial-equivalent, against a real-world median of 6 mg/week mostly commercial. Both PEARL's null primary endpoint AND its reassuring safety profile apply to a substantially lower exposure than most off-label users take. This cuts in both directions and is almost never stated in either.
- DOG AGING PROJECT RESCUE FUNDING: $7 MILLION OR $5 MILLION? Texas A&M's press release (2024-12-09) and the AVMA headline (2025-02-26) both say $7 million; the AVMA body text says $5 million over five years from NIA, with only the first-year portion received and subsequent years uncertain. Unreconciled across every source reachable. Do not quote a figure without flagging the discrepancy.
- TRIAD ENROLLMENT FRACTIONS DO NOT LINE UP ACROSS TIME. STAT (Jan 2024) reported the trial at 'approximately 50% of its enrollment target'; against the stated 580-dog target, enrollment was 170 in Dec 2024 and 235 in 2026 (~41%). The Jan-2024 50% figure must refer to a smaller earlier target and must not be quoted against 580.
- THE MOUSE-TO-HUMAN PROMOTION IS DONE IN A SINGLE PHRASE. AgelessRx calls rapamycin 'the gold standard for pharmacological interventions' on a page selling it to humans. That characterisation is defensible only of the ITP mouse literature. Watch for this exact move — a Tier 1 superlative deployed inside a Tier 3 sales context with no species qualifier.
- PEARL WAS ANALYSED AS COMPLETERS-ONLY (114) WHILE REGISTERING 129 AND ACCOUNTING FOR 125. Discontinuations were unevenly distributed (6 placebo vs 5 drug) and the registry-vs-paper gap of 4 participants is unexplained. Completers-only is not how the trial was registered to be read, and the trial's results were never posted to ClinicalTrials.gov.
- A COMPANY THAT FAILS A HUMAN PHASE 3 IMMEDIATELY RETREATS TO MOUSE DATA. resTORbio's own failure press release pivots in one sentence from a null 1024-patient trial to 'extensive preclinical data supporting the potential therapeutic benefit of TORC1 inhibition'. This is the Tier 3 → Tier 1 retreat, the mirror image of the Tier 1 → Tier 3 promotion, and it is the standard rhetorical move when human evidence goes the wrong way.

**证据缺口(搜过但不存在):**
- NO HUMAN RANDOMISED TRIAL OF RAPAMYCIN HAS EVER USED A CLINICAL OUTCOME (MORTALITY, INCIDENT DISEASE, DISABILITY) AS ITS PRIMARY ENDPOINT, AND NONE IS CURRENTLY REGISTERED. Every human trial in this line uses a surrogate: antibody titre (Mannick 2014), self-reported or laboratory-confirmed infection (Mannick 2018/2021), visceral fat on DXA (PEARL), 30-second chair-stands (RAPA-EX-01), FDG-PET cerebral glucose uptake (ERAP), immune/metabolic markers (UT Health San Antonio 2026). Not one hard outcome. The only lifespan-endpoint trial in the entire field is in dogs.
- THERE IS NO HUMAN EVIDENCE AT ALL FOR THE CENTRAL PHARMACOLOGICAL CLAIM — that intermittent dosing spares mTORC2 and therefore avoids immunosuppression, impaired wound healing and dyslipidaemia. No human trial has ever measured wound healing on intermittent rapamycin. No trial has measured infection incidence as a powered safety endpoint at weekly dosing over ≥1 year. No cancer-incidence data in healthy users at any duration. The claim rests entirely on mTORC1/mTORC2 pharmacology plus rodent data — and the two human datapoints that do exist (Kraig 2018 at 1 mg/day, RAPA-EX-01 at 6 mg/week) BOTH show HbA1c and lipid drift, i.e. they point mildly against the clean version of the hypothesis.
- NO INDEPENDENT (NON-VENDOR, NON-ADVOCATE) RANDOMISED TRIAL OF RAPAMYCIN FOR HEALTHY HUMAN AGEING HAS BEEN COMPLETED. PEARL = AgelessRx, which sells the drug. The entire RTB101 programme = resTORbio/Novartis. The 2023 safety survey = AgelessRx + Optispan founders. RAPA-EX-01 = a health influencer's own company (though notably it published against its funder's interest). The NIA-funded UT Health San Antonio trial launched March 2026 will be the first, and reports years from now.
- NOBODY KNOWS HOW MANY PEOPLE TAKE OFF-LABEL RAPAMYCIN. Searched for and could not find: any prescription-database analysis, claims-data study, pharmacy-dispensing analysis, or registry. The only quantified figure obtainable is a vendor self-report (AgelessRx: 'more than 5,000 people' in the US). Every statement of the form 'thousands of biohackers take rapamycin' is uncounted.
- NO LONG-TERM (>1 YEAR) PLACEBO-CONTROLLED HUMAN SAFETY DATA OF ANY KIND EXISTS. The longest is PEARL at 48 weeks with n≈38 per arm and, per the sponsor's own later PK work, roughly one-third of the intended systemic exposure. That design cannot exclude an adverse event with an incidence below several percent, let alone a malignancy signal.
- PEARL'S RESULTS WERE NEVER POSTED TO CLINICALTRIALS.GOV. Registry record NCT04488601 shows hasResults = No, despite primary completion on 2023-12-30 and journal publication in April 2025.
- NO HEAD-TO-HEAD OF COMPOUNDED VS COMMERCIAL RAPAMYCIN ON ANY CLINICAL ENDPOINT, despite a documented 3x bioavailability gap and the fact that longevity-clinic prescriptions are commonly compounded. Patients on 'the same dose' from different sources are on materially different exposures.
- NO TRIAD RESULTS EXIST AND NONE ARE DUE BEFORE ~NOVEMBER 2029. Enrollment was 235 of 580 as of 2026, dosing was to begin spring 2026. Any 2026-vintage claim that rapamycin extends lifespan in dogs is unsupported.
- NO PUBLISHED RAPAMYCIN TRIAL WITH AN EPIGENETIC-CLOCK PRIMARY ENDPOINT HAS REPORTED. The only clock-adjacent claim in the peer-reviewed literature is a PhenoAge modelling exercise applied to published GROUP-LEVEL data from the Kraig trial, which the reviewing authors themselves call 'suggestive at best and... limited by lack of subject level observations'. I could not verify a reported 2026 Aging Cell paper (Kell, Jones, Gharahdaghi et al.) on low-dose rapamycin reducing p21 in aged human immune cells, nor its bioRxiv precursor (2025.08.15.670559) — both returned 403 and my search budget was exhausted. DO NOT cite either without independent verification; if real, it is another Tier 2 biomarker result.
- NO RETRACTION OR ERRATUM ON ANY MAJOR RAPAMYCIN LONGEVITY TRIAL COULD BE LOCATED. This is worth stating positively: the corrective literature in this field takes the form of failed replication (PROTECTOR 1 after the phase 2b) and null trials (RAPA-EX-01), not fraud or retraction. The pathologies here are endpoint softness, unlabelled 90% confidence intervals, uncontrolled multiplicity, self-selected survey samples, completers-only analyses, and vendor-run trials — all of which are entirely legal and entirely visible in the published record if you read the methods.
- THE FDA RAPAMUNE LABEL'S CURRENT BOXED-WARNING WORDING SHOULD BE RE-VERIFIED. Direct fetch of the 2017 revision PDF returned 404 and I obtained the content from search-indexed extracts of accessdata.fda.gov label PDFs plus the 2013 revision. The substance (immunosuppression, infection, lymphoma/malignancy; wound healing at BMI>30; hyperlipidaemia; hyperglycaemia) is stable across revisions, but pull the current label before quoting exact wording.
- TORNADO THERAPEUTICS' TOR-101 — the flagship 'safer next-generation rapalog' — HAS NOT ENTERED HUMANS. Mannick stated in January 2025 that trials were expected in 2025; as of mid-2026 no phase 1 initiation has been announced and drug-status tracking lists it as preclinical. Meanwhile Mannick herself moved to Altos Labs (August 2025), a company with no rapalog programme. Searched for a TOR-101 IND, NCT registration, or phase 1 announcement; found none.
