## L4 — NAD+ precursors (nicotinamide riboside, nicotinamide mononucleotide): the human trial evidence

### L4-01 **[承重]**

The founding premise of the field — that NAD+ declines with age in humans — rests on a very small primary human literature. The most-cited human tissue datapoint is Massudi 2012: human pelvic skin samples (surgical/newborn), n=49, ages 0–77, cross-sectional, showing a negative correlation between NAD+ and age (males p=0.001, r=−0.706; females p=0.01, r=−0.537). Caliber: one tissue (skin), one cross-section, n=49 — not a longitudinal measurement, not a systemic measurement.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Massudi H, Grant R, Braidy N, Guest J, Farnsworth B, Guillemin GJ (2012). Age-Associated Changes In Oxidative Stress and NAD+ Metabolism In Human Tissue. PLoS ONE 7(7):e42357
- URL:https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371/journal.pone.0042357
- 利益相关:None identified.

### L4-02 **[承重]**

The entire in-vivo human BRAIN evidence for age-related NAD+ decline is a single 31P-MRS study of the occipital lobe with n=17 total subjects, split into three age bins (7 aged 21–26; 4 aged 33–36; 6 aged 59–68). It reports an age-dependent rise in NADH and falls in NAD+, total NAD and the NAD+/NADH redox potential.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Zhu X-H, Lu M, Lee B-Y, Ugurbil K, Chen W (2015). In vivo NAD assay reveals the intracellular NAD contents and redox state in healthy human brain and their age dependences. PNAS 112(9):2876–2881
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/25730862/
- 逐字引:> Furthermore, an age-dependent increase of intracellular NADH and age-dependent reductions in NAD(+), total NAD contents, and NAD(+)/NADH redox potential of the healthy human brain were revealed in this study.
- 利益相关:None identified.

### L4-03 **[承重]**

In May 2026 a 32-author consortium (Amsterdam UMC, Helsinki, Leiden, Valencia) measured NAD+ by validated UHPLC-HRMS across seven independent human cohorts and found whole-blood NAD+ does NOT decline with age and does not change with lifestyle interventions — while confirming it does rise with NR supplementation. Their stated conclusion is that blood NAD+ is not a valid ageing biomarker. This directly undercuts both the mechanistic premise as usually stated and the surrogate endpoint most NR/NMN trials used.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Trętowicz MM, Scantlebery AML, Schomakers BV, Eroglu KD, … Houtkooper RH (2026). Human whole-blood NAD+ levels do not vary with age or lifestyle interventions. Nature Metabolism, published 14 May 2026. PMID 42135539
- URL:https://www.nature.com/articles/s42255-026-01537-5
- 逐字引:> whole-blood NAD+ levels remain remarkably stable with age and across lifestyle interventions, but change in response to nicotinamide riboside supplementation, as expected … our results challenge the utility of blood NAD+ levels as a biomarker of ageing or lifestyle factors.
- 利益相关:Not established from the record I could reach; the accompanying Nature Metabolism editorial 'Whole-blood NAD+ levels do not reflect healthy ageing' (doi 10.1038/s42255-026-01540-w) endorses the finding.

### L4-04 **[承重]**

The most recent independent expert review of the whole field (Nature Metabolism, Oct 2025) states plainly that the age-related human NAD+ decline has been consistently observed only in a limited number of studies, and that human clinical trials of NAD+ precursors have shown limited efficacy despite strong preclinical support.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Vinten KT, Trętowicz MM, Coskun E, van Weeghel M, Cantó C, Zapata-Pérez R, Janssens GE, Houtkooper RH (2025). NAD+ precursor supplementation in human ageing: clinical evidence and challenges. Nature Metabolism 7(10):1974–1990
- URL:https://www.nature.com/articles/s42255-025-01387-7
- 逐字引:> However, evidence for an age-related decline in NAD+ levels in humans has been consistently observed only in a limited number of studies. Similarly, although preclinical studies support the idea that supplementation with NAD+ precursors is a promising therapeutic strategy to promote healthy ageing, human clinical trials have shown limited efficacy. … Viewed in perspective, the published body of data on NAD+ dynamics in human tissues remains sparse, and the extrapolation of rodent-based data is not straightforward.
- 利益相关:Co-author Carles Cantó is a scientist at the Nestlé Institute of Health Sciences, which subsequently ran the 2026 head-to-head NAD+ precursor trial.

### L4-05 **[承重]**

The NIA Interventions Testing Program — the most rigorous multi-site blinded mouse lifespan protocol in existence — tested nicotinamide riboside chloride at 1,000 ppm in diet, started at 8 months of age, in genetically heterogeneous UM-HET3 mice (156 males, 136 females split across three independent sites) and found NO lifespan effect: males median −3% (p=0.252), 90th percentile −3% (p=0.99); females median 0% (p=0.612), 90th percentile +2% (p=0.99). This is the direct refutation of the single 'NR extends mouse lifespan ~5%' result (Zhang/Auwerx, Science 2016, treatment begun at ~24 months) that the consumer field rests on.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Harrison DE, Strong R, Reifsnyder P, et al. (2021). 17-a-estradiol late in life extends lifespan in aging UM-HET3 male mice; nicotinamide riboside and three other drugs do not affect lifespan in either sex. Aging Cell 20(5):e13328. PMID 33788371
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC8135004/
- 逐字引:> nicotinamide riboside and three other drugs do not affect lifespan in either sex
- 利益相关:NIA-funded program; no commercial sponsor.

### L4-06 **[承重]**

The first human NR trial (Trammell 2016) was a 12-subject (6M/6F, ages 30–55) single-dose crossover of 100/300/1000 mg with 7-day washouts. The controlled result at 1,000 mg was PBMC NAD+ rising from ~12 to ~18 µM. The widely-repeated '2.7-fold rise in blood NAD+' figure comes from a pilot in ONE individual, and the paper says so explicitly.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Trammell SAJ, Schmidt MS, Weidemann BJ, et al., Brenner C (2016). Nicotinamide riboside is uniquely and orally bioavailable in mice and humans. Nature Communications 7:12948
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5062546/
- 逐字引:> human blood NAD+ can rise as much as 2.7-fold with a single oral dose of NR in a pilot study of one individual
- 利益相关:Funded in part by ChromaDex: "supported by grants from the Roy J. Carver Trust, ChromaDex and National Institutes of Health." COI statement: "F.J., R.W.D. and C.B. own stock in ChromaDex." Charles Brenner is the NR patent inventor and ChromaDex chief scientific adviser.

### L4-07 **[承重]**

The largest NR RCT ever conducted (n=140 randomized healthy overweight adults, 40–60y, BMI 25–30; placebo vs 100/300/1000 mg/day for 8 weeks) established a clean dose–response in whole-blood NAD+ (+22±9%, +51±7%, +142±14% at day 14) and found nothing clinically meaningful across blood pressure, heart rate, weight, lipids, CBC, liver/kidney function, homocysteine, resting energy expenditure, BCAAs and CRP. It is a biomarker-and-safety study, funded and co-authored by the manufacturer.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Conze D, Brenner C, Kruger CL (2019). Safety and Metabolism of Long-term Administration of NIAGEN (Nicotinamide Riboside Chloride) in a Randomized, Double-Blind, Placebo-controlled Clinical Trial of Healthy Overweight Adults. Scientific Reports 9:9772
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6611812/
- 逐字引:> ChromaDex funded the study. … Claire Kruger and Dietrich Conze are employees of ChromaDex. Charles Brenner is the inventor of intellectual property licensed by ChromaDex and serves as their chief scientific adviser.
- 利益相关:ChromaDex funded the study; two of three authors are ChromaDex employees; the third is ChromaDex's chief scientific adviser and NR patent inventor.

### L4-08 **[承重]**

The most important negative finding in the entire pharmacokinetic layer: in the only trial that measured muscle NAD+ directly with biopsies in aged humans (12 aged men, 1 g NR/day for 21 days, randomized double-blind placebo-controlled crossover), muscle NAD+ ITSELF did not rise significantly (NR 210 vs placebo 197 pmol/mg, p=0.22). What rose was the surrounding metabolome — NAAD and nicotinamide CLEARANCE products. The 'NR raises muscle NAD+' claim is not supported by its own source paper.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Elhassan YS, Kluckova K, Fletcher RS, et al., Lavery GG (2019). Nicotinamide Riboside Augments the Aged Human Skeletal Muscle NAD+ Metabolome and Induces Transcriptomic and Anti-inflammatory Signatures. Cell Reports 28(7):1717–1728
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6702140/
- 逐字引:> without an increase in NAD+ (NR 210 pmol/mg versus 197 pmol/mg; p = 0.22)
- 利益相关:NR supplied by the manufacturer: "NR was supplied as 250 mg capsules by the manufacturer (Niagen, ChromaDex, Irvine, CA)." COI: "C.B. is the inventor of patents licensed by ChromaDex, owns stock in ChromaDex, and serves as adviser to ChromaDex and Cytokinetics."

### L4-09 **[承重]**

The same muscle-biopsy trial found no functional benefit: no difference in complex I- or complex II-mediated oxidative phosphorylation, and no difference in peak hand-grip strength (NR 32.5 kg vs placebo 34.7 kg; p=0.96). Muscle RNA-seq showed DOWN-regulation of energy metabolism and mitochondrial pathways — the opposite direction to the mouse narrative. The one positive signal was reduced circulating inflammatory cytokines.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Elhassan YS et al. (2019), Cell Reports 28(7):1717–1728
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6702140/
- 逐字引:> No differences were detected between the NR and placebo groups in skeletal muscle complex I- and complex II-mediated oxidative phosphorylation … we did not observe any differences in the participants' peak hand-grip strengths (NR 32.5 kg versus placebo 34.7 kg; p = 0.96)
- 利益相关:See L4-08.

### L4-10 **[承重]**

The most rigorous human insulin-sensitivity test of NR was null. 40 obese, sedentary, insulin-resistant men (BMI>30, ages 40–70) received 2,000 mg/day NR or placebo for 12 weeks in a parallel-group double-blind RCT with hyperinsulinemic-euglycemic clamp as the primary endpoint. Nothing improved — not insulin sensitivity, endogenous glucose production, glucose disposal or oxidation, and not resting energy expenditure, lipolysis, lipid oxidation or body composition.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Dollerup OL, Christensen B, Svart M, et al., Jessen N, Treebak JT (2018). A randomized placebo-controlled clinical trial of nicotinamide riboside in obese men: safety, insulin-sensitivity, and lipid-mobilizing effects. American Journal of Clinical Nutrition 108(2):343–353. PMID 29992272
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/29992272/
- 逐字引:> Insulin sensitivity, endogenous glucose production, and glucose disposal and oxidation were not improved by NR supplementation. … 12 wk of NR supplementation in doses of 2000 mg/d appears safe, but does not improve insulin sensitivity and whole-body glucose metabolism in obese, insulin-resistant men.
- 利益相关:The Copenhagen group operates under a 2014 Material Transfer Agreement in which ChromaDex supplies NIAGEN NR for research (PI Jonas Thue Treebak).

### L4-11 **[承重]**

A second independent muscle-biopsy trial (13 healthy overweight/obese men and women, 1,000 mg/day NR for 6 weeks, randomized double-blind placebo-controlled crossover, with clamps, MRS and ex vivo mitochondrial function) DID raise skeletal muscle NAD+ metabolites and altered acetylcarnitine, but produced no change in muscle mitochondrial respiration, hepatic lipid content, cardiac function measures, inflammatory markers, blood glucose, or insulin sensitivity.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Remie CME, Roumans KHM, Moonen MPB, et al., Schrauwen P (2020). Nicotinamide riboside supplementation alters body composition and skeletal muscle acetylcarnitine concentrations in healthy obese humans. American Journal of Clinical Nutrition 112(2):413–426. PMID 32320006
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/32320006/
- 利益相关:Not verified from the primary text in this pass.

### L4-12 **[承重]**

The famous 'NR lowers blood pressure by 9 mmHg' figure is a post-hoc exploratory subgroup from a 24-completer crossover trial, and the authors explicitly forbade inference from it. The pre-specified whole-group results (n=30 randomized, 24 completed, ages 55–79, 500 mg twice daily, 2×6-week crossover) were SBP −3.9 mmHg and DBP −2.0 mmHg, neither significant after multiplicity correction; carotid-femoral PWV showed only a non-significant trend. Blood NAD+ (PBMC) rose ~60%.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Martens CR, Denman BA, Mazzo MR, et al., Seals DR (2018). Chronic nicotinamide riboside supplementation is well-tolerated and elevates NAD+ in healthy middle-aged and older adults. Nature Communications 9:1286
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5876407/
- 逐字引:> these comparisons were not statistically significant after correction for multiple comparisons … Because this post-hoc subgroup analysis was exploratory, no statistical inferences can be made.
- 利益相关:"Study pills, NR standards for metabolite analyses and partial funding support were provided by ChromaDex, Inc." — yet the same paper states "The authors declare no competing interests."

### L4-13 **[承重]**

The flagship NMN clinical result — 250 mg/day NMN for 10 weeks raising muscle insulin sensitivity in postmenopausal women with prediabetes — comes from a trial with 13 women on NMN and 12 on placebo. Insulin-stimulated glucose disposal and muscle AKT/mTOR phosphorylation rose in the NMN arm only; body weight and body composition did not change. Five years on it has not been independently replicated.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Yoshino M, Yoshino J, Kayser BD, et al., Klein S (2021). Nicotinamide mononucleotide increases muscle insulin sensitivity in prediabetic women. Science 372(6547):1224–1229. PMID 33888596
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/33888596/
- 逐字引:> Insulin-stimulated glucose disposal, assessed by using the hyperinsulinemic-euglycemic clamp, and skeletal muscle insulin signaling [phosphorylation of protein kinase AKT and mechanistic target of rapamycin (mTOR)] increased after NMN supplementation.
- 利益相关:NIH/NIDDK-supported academic trial at Washington University — one of the few NAD+ precursor trials without manufacturer funding, which is precisely why it drew the sharpest commercial rebuttal (see L4-14).

### L4-14 **[承重]**

That flagship NMN trial was formally challenged in Science on randomisation grounds: hepatic lipid content was 6.3 ± 1.2% in the NMN arm versus 14.8 ± 2.0% in placebo (P=0.003), and since liver fat itself depresses muscle insulin sensitivity the critic argued the result would not reproduce in an effectively randomised trial. The critic is Charles Brenner — the NR patent inventor, ChromaDex stockholder and ChromaDex chief scientific adviser, i.e. the commercial rival of NMN. The authors replied that baseline muscle insulin sensitivity was identical between arms and that muscle insulin signaling rose only with NMN.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Brenner C (2021). Comment on 'Nicotinamide mononucleotide increases muscle insulin sensitivity in prediabetic women'. Science 373(6553). PMID 34326206. Reply: Klein S, Yoshino M (2021), Science. PMID 34326209
- URL:https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.abj1696
- 利益相关:Brenner is the inventor of NR intellectual property licensed by ChromaDex, owns ChromaDex stock, and is ChromaDex's chief scientific adviser. NR and NMN are directly competing consumer products.

### L4-15 **[承重]**

The 2025 meta-analysis of NR and NMN on skeletal muscle — 10 RCTs, participants of mean age 60.9–83, NMN 250–2000 mg/d for 3–24 weeks (6 RCTs) and NR 500–1000 mg/d for 3–12 weeks (4 RCTs) — found no effect on skeletal muscle index (n=3 studies; MD −0.42, 95% CI −0.99 to 0.14), no effect on handgrip strength, and no effect on gait speed.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Prokopidis K, et al. (2025). The Effect of Nicotinamide Mononucleotide and Riboside on Skeletal Muscle Mass and Function: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis. Journal of Cachexia, Sarcopenia and Muscle. PMID 40275690
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12022230/
- 逐字引:> Current evidence does not support NMN and NR supplementation for preserving muscle mass and function in adults with mean age of over 60 years.
- 利益相关:None identified.

### L4-16 **[承重]**

The 2024/2025 meta-analysis of NMN on metabolic endpoints pooled 8 RCTs, n=342 middle-aged/older adults (49% female, mostly non-diabetic), NMN 250–2000 mg/day for 14 days to 12 weeks, trials published 2021–2023. No significant benefit on fasting glucose, fasting insulin, HbA1c, HOMA-IR or lipid profile. A parallel meta-analysis in Critical Reviews in Food Science and Nutrition (2024) reported the same split: NMN significantly raises blood NAD, while most clinically relevant outcomes did not differ from control.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Effects of Nicotinamide Mononucleotide on Glucose and Lipid Metabolism in Adults: A Systematic Review and Meta-analysis of Randomised Controlled Trials. Current Diabetes Reports (2025), published online Nov 2024. PMID 39531138
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC11557618/
- 逐字引:> Short-term supplementation of NMN of 250-2000 mg/d did not show significantly positive impacts on glucose control and lipid profile.
- 利益相关:None identified.

### L4-17 **[承重]**

The most complete critical review of the NR human literature assessed all 25 published human NR research articles and concluded the effects are few and clinically marginal, and that the literature systematically overstates them. Notably its senior author's own lab received NIAGEN from ChromaDex under a 2014 material transfer agreement — i.e. the harshest published verdict comes from inside the sponsor's own research network.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Damgaard MV, Treebak JT (2023). What is really known about the effects of nicotinamide riboside supplementation in humans. Science Advances 9(29):eadi4862. PMID 37478182
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/37478182/
- 逐字引:> Collectively, oral nicotinamide riboside supplementation has displayed few clinically relevant effects, and there is an unfortunate tendency in the literature to exaggerate the importance and robustness of reported effects. Even so, nicotinamide riboside may play a role in the reduction of inflammatory states and has shown some potential in the treatment of diverse severe diseases.
- 利益相关:Senior author Jonas Thue Treebak's group at the University of Copenhagen has received NIAGEN nicotinamide riboside from ChromaDex under a material transfer agreement signed 15 April 2014.

### L4-18 **[承重]**

The single largest and longest RCT of any NAD+ precursor with a genuine clinical progression endpoint — NOPARK, 410 patients with early Parkinson's disease, NR 500 mg twice daily vs placebo for 52 weeks across 12 Norwegian sites, primary outcome total MDS-UPDRS change from baseline to 52 weeks — completed on 17 June 2025. As of 28 July 2026, more than 13 months later, no results have been posted to the registry, no paper has appeared, and no sponsor topline release exists. Results were publicly promised 'by end of 2025' and then 'early 2026'.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:NCT03568968, A Randomized Controlled Trial of Nicotinamide Riboside Supplementation in Early Parkinson's Disease (NOPARK). Lead sponsor Haukeland University Hospital with 10 Norwegian hospital collaborators. Actual enrollment 410; start 6 May 2020; completion 17 June 2025; status Completed (verified Sept 2025); results not posted.
- URL:https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT03568968
- 逐字引:> NOPARK is a double-blinded randomized controlled phase II trial, with the aim to assess the efficacy of nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide (NAD)-replenishment therapy in the form of oral nicotinamide riboside (NR) in delaying the progression of early Parkinson's disease (PD).
- 利益相关:Niagen Bioscience (formerly ChromaDex) secured worldwide exclusive commercial rights from Haukeland University Hospital to develop and commercialize nicotinamide riboside (Niagen) as a Parkinson's disease therapy — a licence taken before any public disclosure of the trial result.

### L4-19 **[承重]**

NADPARK is the one human study that demonstrates an oral NAD+ precursor raising NAD in a target ORGAN rather than blood: 30 newly diagnosed, treatment-naive Parkinson's patients, 1,000 mg NR/day for 30 days, double-blind randomized phase I, with cerebral NAD measured by 31P-MRS. The increase was significant but explicitly variable between individuals; downstream FDG-PET metabolic changes and 'mild clinical improvement' were exploratory in a trial not powered for clinical outcome.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Brakedal B, Dölle C, Riemer F, et al., Tzoulis C (2022). The NADPARK study: A randomized phase I trial of nicotinamide riboside supplementation in Parkinson's disease. Cell Metabolism 34(3):396–407. PMID 35235774
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/35235774/
- 利益相关:Two authors filed a patent application relating to the use of NR in Parkinson's disease; remaining authors declared no competing interests.

### L4-20 

NR-SAFE is a genuine safety result and should be reported as such: 20 Parkinson's patients randomized 1:1 to NR 1,500 mg twice daily (3,000 mg/day) or placebo for 4 weeks produced no moderate or severe adverse events and no significant excess of mild adverse events, with blood NAD+ rising up to 5-fold and no methyl-donor depletion (a transient early rise in serum homocysteine aside). The secondary observation of MDS-UPDRS improvement is from n=20 over 4 weeks and cannot support an efficacy claim.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Brakedal B, et al., Tzoulis C (2023). NR-SAFE: a randomized, double-blind safety trial of high dose nicotinamide riboside in Parkinson's disease. Nature Communications, November 2023. NCT05344404
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10684646/
- 利益相关:Same Bergen/Haukeland group as NADPARK and NOPARK; NR patent application by group members.

### L4-21 **[承重]**

The Ataxia Telangiectasia evidence — the field's most consistent clinical signal — is not randomized. The 2021 study was open-label, single-arm, proof-of-concept: 24 A-T patients on NR for 4 consecutive months, mean total SARA falling 2.4 points and ICARS 10.1 points, with scores WORSENING again after NR withdrawal, and mean serum IgG rising 0.52 g/L in immunodeficient patients. The 2024 follow-up was also single-arm open-label: 10 patients (7 children, 3 adults, ages 3–28) on 10–37 mg/kg/day NR for 2 years, compared against historical controls. No placebo-controlled randomized trial of NR in A-T has been published.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Veenhuis SJG, et al. (2021). Nicotinamide Riboside Improves Ataxia Scores and Immunoglobulin Levels in Ataxia Telangiectasia. Movement Disorders 36(12):2951–2957 (PMID 34515380). Presterud R, Deng WH, Wennerström AB, et al. (2024). Long-Term Nicotinamide Riboside Use Improves Coordination and Eye Movements in Ataxia Telangiectasia. Movement Disorders 39(2):360–369
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/34515380/
- 利益相关:ChromaDex issued a press release (15 Nov 2023) marketing the A-T work as a 'Phase II Clinical Study … Improving Motor Coordination and Eye Movement', omitting that the designs were open-label and uncontrolled.

### L4-22 **[承重]**

The strongest functional-endpoint NR result in a common disease was declared positive only against a pre-specified ONE-SIDED p<0.10 threshold. In the NICE trial (n=90 with peripheral artery disease, 6 months, NR 1,000 mg ± resveratrol 125 mg vs placebo, primary outcome 6-minute walk distance), the registry-posted intention-to-treat least-squares mean changes were: NR alone +7.00 ± 8.69 m (n=28), NR+resveratrol −6.93 ± 7.84 m (n=33), placebo −10.58 ± 8.45 m (n=29). NR vs placebo difference +17.57 m, one-sided 90% CI lower bound 1.77, p=0.0775 — not significant at a conventional two-sided α=0.05, and below the ~20 m generally taken as the minimal clinically important difference for 6MWD in PAD. The 31 m figure often quoted is the ≥75%-adherence per-protocol subgroup (n=60).

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:NCT03743636, Nicotinamide Riboside With and Without Resveratrol to Improve Functioning in Peripheral Artery Disease (NICE). Results first posted 14 January 2025. Published as McDermott MM et al. (2024), Nature Communications, PMID 38871717; Publisher Correction PMID 39134546
- URL:https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT03743636
- 利益相关:Lead sponsor Northwestern University; collaborator American Heart Association. One of the few sizeable NR trials without manufacturer funding. A Publisher Correction was issued (Aug 2024); its content was not retrievable from the PubMed record.

### L4-23 **[承重]**

A 24-week double-blind placebo-controlled RCT of NR 2,000 mg/day in long-COVID (n=58 completing baseline, 2:1 randomization, 20 weeks NR vs 10 weeks placebo then 10 weeks NR) raised NAD+ 2.6–3.1-fold within 5–10 weeks and produced NO significant between-group difference on any pre-specified clinical outcome: cognition (ECog, RBANS, TMT-B; p=0.47–0.74), fatigue severity (p=0.59), sleep quality (p=0.69), anxiety (p=0.84), depression (p=0.20). The paper's positive-sounding findings come only from post-hoc within-person analyses pooling all NR exposure.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Effects of nicotinamide riboside on NAD+ levels, cognition, and symptom recovery in long-COVID: a randomized controlled trial (2025/2026), PMC12675013
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12675013/
- 逐字引:> In individuals with long-COVID, NR was safe and effective in increasing NAD+ levels within 5 weeks. However, NAD+ augmentation did not produce statistically significant between-group differences (NR vs. PBO) in objective cognitive outcomes or in self-reported measures of fatigue severity, sleep quality, or mood.
- 利益相关:Niagen Bioscience provided funding and donated the NR and placebo via ChromaDex; two authors hold equity in Niagen Bioscience.

### L4-24 **[承重]**

The 2023 twin study routinely cited as proof that NR builds mitochondria in humans is nearly uncontrolled and reports a metabolically adverse outcome. 20 BMI-discordant monozygotic twin pairs took NR escalated 250→1,000 mg/day for 5 months; only 4 concordant pairs (8 individuals) carried the placebo comparison — in the 16 discordant pairs BOTH cotwins received NR. NR improved NAD+ metabolism, muscle mitochondrial number, myoblast differentiation, gut microbiota and DNA methylation, but participants gained ~3 kg body weight with a significant rise in whole-body fat percentage, elevated fasting glucose and insulin, and impaired insulin sensitivity.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Lundgren/Pietiläinen group (2023). Nicotinamide riboside improves muscle mitochondrial biogenesis, satellite cell differentiation, and gut microbiota in a twin study. Science Advances 9:eadd5163. PMC9839336
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC9839336/
- 逐字引:> Of the concordant twin pairs, one cotwin was randomized to placebo and the other one to NR. … NR did not ameliorate adiposity or metabolic health … body weight (~3 kg) and the whole-body fat percentage increased significantly … NR's effect on body composition and metabolic health did not markedly differ from the effects of placebo.
- 利益相关:ChromaDex publicised this trial in a January 2023 press release ('ChromaDex Shares Promising Findings from First-Of-Its Kind Clinical Study…'), foregrounding mitochondrial biogenesis and omitting the weight/fat/insulin findings.

### L4-25 **[承重]**

A 2026 Nature Metabolism head-to-head trial (65 healthy participants, randomized, OPEN-LABEL, placebo-controlled, 1,000 mg/day of NR, NMN or nicotinamide for 14 days) found NR and NMN raise circulating NAD+ comparably with no significant difference between them, while nicotinamide raises it only acutely and also raises homocysteine. Mechanistically it proposes that NR and NMN are converted by GUT MICROBIOTA to nicotinic acid, and that the systemic NAD+ rise runs through the Preiss-Handler pathway via that microbially-produced niacin — not via intact NR or NMN reaching tissues. If correct, the entire NR-vs-NMN commercial distinction is largely moot and cheap nicotinic acid becomes the relevant comparator.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Christen S, et al. (2026). The differential impact of three different NAD+ boosters on circulatory NAD and microbial metabolism in humans. Nature Metabolism 8:62–73, published 15 January 2026. PMID 41540253
- URL:https://www.nature.com/articles/s42255-025-01421-8
- 逐字引:> In a randomized, open-label, placebo-controlled study in 65 healthy participants, 14 days of supplementation with NR and NMN, but not Nam, comparably increases circulatory NAD+ concentrations in healthy adults.
- 利益相关:Designed, conducted and funded by the Nestlé Institute of Health Sciences, which sells nutrition products.

### L4-26 **[承重]**

The cancer signal is real but strictly tier-1 (mouse) and bidirectional, and the lead author says so. A bioluminescent NR-uptake probe showed triple-negative breast cancer takes up NR avidly, and NR supplementation increased cancer prevalence and dramatically increased brain metastasis rate in a TNBC mouse model. The same literature contains an NAD+ precursor SUPPRESSING hepatocellular cancer progression in mice, and NMN PROMOTING pancreatic ductal adenocarcinoma via the senescence-associated secretory phenotype. No human trial of NR or NMN has been powered for cancer incidence and none is registered. Note the study is frequently misdated to 2024 — it is Biosensors and Bioelectronics 220:114826, online November 2022, print 15 January 2023 — and the group is University of Missouri plus EPFL, not Missouri alone.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Ma X, et al., Goun EA (2023). A bioluminescent-based probe for in vivo non-invasive monitoring of nicotinamide riboside uptake reveals a link between metastasis and NAD+ metabolism. Biosensors and Bioelectronics 220:114826. PMID 36371959
- URL:https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/36371959/
- 逐字引:> These findings have not been studied in humans
- 利益相关:Funded by the European Research Council (ERC-2019-COG 866338), Swiss National Science Foundation (51NF40_185898) and NCCR Chemical Biology — no supplement-industry funding. The quoted caveat is from the University of Missouri press release accompanying the paper (ScienceDaily, 11 Nov 2022).

### L4-27 **[承重]**

Two of the most-quoted NMN functional results are sponsor-run and internally inconsistent. Igarashi 2022 (42 healthy men aged 65+, 250 mg/day NMN vs placebo, 12 weeks, Mitsubishi-funded) reported improved gait speed and improved LEFT-hand grip strength but NOT right-hand grip strength, with no change in skeletal muscle mass. Katayoshi 2023 (36 healthy middle-aged adults, 250 mg/day NMN, 12 weeks) MISSED its arterial-stiffness primary endpoint (pulse wave velocity, p=0.097) and the widely-circulated positive result is a post-hoc subgroup of participants with above-average BMI or blood glucose.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Igarashi M, et al. (2022). npj Aging 8:5, PMID 35927255. Katayoshi T, et al. (2023). Nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide metabolism and arterial stiffness after long-term nicotinamide mononucleotide supplementation: a randomized, double-blind, placebo-controlled trial. Scientific Reports 13
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC9935856/
- 逐字引:> Pulse wave velocity values indicating arterial stiffness tended to decrease in the NMN intake group. However, no significant difference was found between the two groups. … This work was supported by a research grant from the DHC Corporation.
- 利益相关:Katayoshi: funded by DHC Corporation, an NMN seller, and ALL authors are employed by DHC Corporation Laboratories — while the paper declares 'The authors declare no competing interests.' Igarashi: Mitsubishi-funded.

### L4-28 **[承重]**

Funder concentration is structural, not incidental. Every large or landmark NR human trial I could verify carries ChromaDex/Niagen Bioscience money, product, or an author with ChromaDex equity or advisory ties: Trammell 2016 (ChromaDex grant, authors own stock), Martens 2018 (pills, standards and partial funding from ChromaDex), Elhassan 2019 (NR supplied by ChromaDex, Brenner stock+adviser), Conze 2019 (ChromaDex funded, two authors employees, third is chief scientific adviser), Dollerup 2018 (Copenhagen ChromaDex MTA), the long-COVID RCT (Niagen Bioscience funded and donated product, two authors hold equity), and NOPARK (Niagen Bioscience holds the exclusive commercial licence). The clearest exception, NICE/PAD, is Northwestern + American Heart Association funded — and it is also the only one that hit a functional endpoint, at one-sided p<0.10.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Composite of funding and competing-interest statements in Trammell 2016 Nat Commun 7:12948; Martens 2018 Nat Commun 9:1286; Elhassan 2019 Cell Rep 28:1717; Conze 2019 Sci Rep 9:9772; ChromaDex–University of Copenhagen material transfer agreement press release 15 April 2014; long-COVID NR RCT PMC12675013; NCT03568968 licensing to Niagen Bioscience
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6611812/
- 逐字引:> ChromaDex funded the study.
- 利益相关:This claim is itself about conflicts of interest. Note two papers (Martens 2018, Katayoshi 2023) declare 'no competing interests' while disclosing manufacturer funding or universal manufacturer employment elsewhere in the same paper.

**交叉口径问题:**
- 'NAD+ falls ~50% by age 50' has no clean human primary source. The circulating figure fuses three incompatible calibers: a skin-biopsy correlation (Massudi 2012, n=49, cross-sectional), an occipital-lobe 31P-MRS study (Zhu 2015, n=17, three age bins), and a muscle metabolomics comparison that is actually confounded by physical activity (Janssens 2022 — exercise-trained older adults had near-young NAD+). Whole-blood NAD+, the compartment consumer tests measure, is now shown NOT to decline with age across seven cohorts (Trętowicz 2026). Any single percentage attached to 'NAD+ decline' is a cross-caliber artifact.
- 'NR raises NAD+ 2.7-fold' is an n=1 single-dose pilot from Trammell 2016 stated as such in the paper. The controlled figures are: PBMC NAD+ ~12→18 µM at 1,000 mg single dose (n=12), and +142 ± 14% at 1,000 mg/day for 14 days (n=140, Conze 2019). The n=1 number is what propagated into marketing.
- 'NR raises muscle NAD+' inverts its own source. Elhassan 2019 measured muscle NAD+ and reported no significant increase (210 vs 197 pmol/mg, p=0.22); the title's 'augments the NAD+ metabolome' refers to NAAD and nicotinamide clearance products. Rising clearance products is at least as consistent with accelerated disposal as with repletion.
- 'NR lowers blood pressure 9 mmHg' is a post-hoc exploratory subgroup of a 24-completer crossover (Martens 2018) about which the authors wrote 'no statistical inferences can be made'. The pre-specified whole-group effect was −3.9 mmHg and non-significant after multiplicity correction. The 9 mmHg number is nonetheless the one that entered circulation.
- 'NMN improves insulin sensitivity 25%' comes from n=13 vs n=12 in arms that were significantly imbalanced on the very variable most likely to drive the endpoint: hepatic lipid content 6.3 ± 1.2% (NMN) vs 14.8 ± 2.0% (placebo), P=0.003.
- 'NR improves walking in PAD' rests on a trial judged positive against a pre-specified ONE-SIDED p<0.10. The ITT effect is +17.6 m on 6-minute walk — below the ~20 m usually cited as minimally clinically important in PAD — with two-sided-equivalent p=0.0775. The 31 m figure widely quoted is the ≥75%-adherence per-protocol subgroup.
- 'NR extends lifespan' is one mouse study (~5%, dosing started at ~24 months, Zhang 2016) against a null in the NIA Interventions Testing Program (1,000 ppm from 8 months, 156M/136F, three sites: −3%/0%, p=0.252/0.612). The positive is quoted constantly; the better-powered null almost never.
- 'NMN improves grip strength in older men' (Igarashi 2022): LEFT hand improved, RIGHT hand did not, in a two-arm n=42 trial. Reporting one hand as the result is a multiplicity artifact.
- Two papers declare 'The authors declare no competing interests' in the same document that discloses manufacturer entanglement: Martens 2018 (ChromaDex supplied study pills, standards and partial funding) and Katayoshi 2023 (DHC Corporation grant; all authors employed by DHC Corporation Laboratories). Any COI audit that trusts the declaration line alone will miss both.
- Phase mislabelling: NOPARK is registered on clinicaltrials.gov as a 'phase II' trial but is described as phase III by the sponsor's institution, its commercial licensee, and press coverage. Cite the registry, not the press.
- Date error in wide circulation: the NR / brain-metastasis mouse study is repeatedly dated 2024 and attributed to Missouri alone. It is Biosensors and Bioelectronics 220:114826, online November 2022 / print 15 January 2023, from a Missouri–EPFL collaboration.
- The 2026 Nestlé head-to-head result — that gut microbiota convert NR and NMN into nicotinic acid, which is what actually raises systemic NAD+ via the Preiss-Handler pathway — would, if it holds, mean the NR-vs-NMN 'which precursor is superior' literature has been arguing about the wrong molecule, and that the correct control arm in every past trial should have been cheap niacin.

**证据缺口(搜过但不存在):**
- No completed and published RCT of NR or NMN with a hard clinical outcome (mortality, major adverse cardiac events, incident disease, disability) exists in any population. Searched clinicaltrials.gov phase 3 records for both compounds and the published literature; there are none.
- NOPARK — the field's flagship, 410 patients, NR 1,000 mg/day vs placebo for 52 weeks, primary endpoint total MDS-UPDRS — completed 17 June 2025 and as of 28 July 2026 has NO posted registry results, NO publication, NO preprint, and NO sponsor topline release. Niagen Bioscience's Q1 2026 financial release (6 May 2026) does not mention the outcome; the sponsor's own study page was last updated 12 June 2025 saying only that the trial 'will be concluded in June 2025'. Meanwhile the commercial licence to develop NR for Parkinson's was already granted. Thirteen months of silence on the only well-powered clinical-endpoint trial in the field is itself the finding.
- No RCT of NR or NMN longer than roughly six months with an intact control group has been published. The longest controlled exposures found are NOPARK (52 weeks, unreported), the long-COVID trial (20 weeks of NR), and the twin study (5 months, but with only 8 individuals in the placebo comparison). The two-year A-T study is single-arm.
- No human trial has demonstrated tissue NAD+ repletion COUPLED to a functional improvement in that tissue. Elhassan measured muscle NAD+ and it did not rise; Remie raised muscle metabolites and nothing functional moved; NADPARK raised cerebral NAD but was phase I, n=30, 30 days, and not powered for clinical outcome.
- No human study of NAD+ precursors and cancer incidence exists, and none is registered. The mouse signal runs in both directions (NR promoting TNBC brain metastasis; NMN promoting pancreatic adenocarcinoma via the SASP; an NAD+ precursor suppressing hepatocellular cancer) and has never been tested in people. The honest status is 'biologically plausible, unresolved, untested' — neither 'NR causes cancer' nor 'proven safe long-term' is supportable.
- I could not find a single large, independent NR trial free of ChromaDex/Niagen funding, product donation, or an author with ChromaDex equity or advisory ties. The nearest exception is the NICE peripheral-artery-disease trial (Northwestern University + American Heart Association).
- No NMN phase 3 trial with a clinical endpoint exists. Querying clinicaltrials.gov for NMN restricted to PHASE3 returned exactly one record: NCT06739720 (CP003 for CFS/ME or post-COVID fatigue, n=130, University of Hong Kong, listed as not yet recruiting).
- No independent replication of Yoshino 2021 has been published in the five years since. The most-cited NMN clinical result in existence rests on 13 versus 12 participants, and the meta-analyses that include it find no pooled glycaemic effect.
- No RCT of NR or NMN has pre-registered a functional endpoint as primary and hit it at a conventional two-sided α=0.05. The closest, NICE, required a pre-specified one-sided p<0.10.
- No head-to-head trial of NR or NMN against plain nicotinic acid on any clinical endpoint exists — which the 2026 Nestlé mechanism (microbial conversion of NR/NMN to nicotinic acid) makes the single most obvious missing comparator in the field.
- No randomised placebo-controlled trial of NR in Ataxia Telangiectasia has been published, despite A-T being where the mechanistic case is strongest and where the clinical signal is most consistent. The evidence is one n=24 open-label single-arm study with a withdrawal rebound, and one n=10 two-year single-arm study benchmarked against historical controls.
- I could not retrieve the full text of Damgaard & Treebak 2023 (403 on science.org) and therefore could not extract their per-trial funding tabulation, which would give an exact count of how many of the 25 human NR trials carried ChromaDex funding or product. That table is the cleanest available answer to the independence question and someone with journal access should pull it.

## L7 — The regulatory and commercial layer: who sells what, under what rules, with what claims

### L7-01 **[承重]**

On 29 September 2025 FDA issued two citizen-petition response letters (docket ref FDA-2023-P-0872-2754) reversing its 2022 position and concluding that NMN (nicotinamide mononucleotide) is NOT excluded from the definition of a dietary supplement under FD&C Act §201(ff)(3)(B). FDA granted-in-part/denied-in-part the NPA+ANH petition (received 6 Mar 2023) and the CRN petition. Basis: evidence that NMN was marketed as a dietary supplement in the US as early as 2017, before the drug IND authorization date; and the doctrinal change that the prior marketing need not have been *lawful*. ANSWER TO 'is NMN legally sellable in the US today': YES, as of 2026, subject to New Dietary Ingredient Notification requirements. CAVEAT: I could not reach the FDA letters themselves (regulations.gov and fda.gov both returned 403/404 from this environment); all quoted FDA language is second-hand from counsel and trade bodies and needs primary verification.

- 来源类型:media-secondhand
- 出处:Venable LLP, 'FDA Declares Nicotinamide Mononucleotide Is a Dietary Supplement', October 2025 insights publication; corroborated by AHPA blog 1 Oct 2025 and NutraIngredients 30 Sep 2025
- URL:https://www.venable.com/insights/publications/2025/10/fda-declares-nicotinamide-mononucleotide-is
- 逐字引:> FDA clarified it does not require "proof that the ingredient was lawfully marketed or otherwise compliant with all laws," only that marketing occurred in the U.S. before the drug authorization date. [Venable's rendering of FDA's letter; the FDA letter itself was NOT reachable — 403 on regulations.gov document FDA-2023-P-0872-2754 and on downloads.regulations.gov attachment PDF.]
- 利益相关:Venable is a law firm serving the supplement industry; AHPA/NPA/CRN are supplement trade associations whose members profit from the reversal.

### L7-02 **[承重]**

The reversal was operationalised on 2 December 2025, when FDA sent letters to SyncoZymes and Inner Mongolia Kingdomway Pharmaceutical Ltd setting aside its late-2022 determinations against their NMN New Dietary Ingredient notifications, restoring NDI status. NPA announced this on 10 December 2025. NDI notification remains required and is notifier-specific (tied to a defined production process and conditions of use) — the reversal is not a blanket ingredient authorisation, and FDA did not permit self-affirmed GRAS as a substitute.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:Natural Products Association press release, 'FDA Reinstates NMN As Dietary Supplement After NPA Lawsuit', 10 December 2025; NutraIngredients, 9 December 2025
- URL:https://www.npanational.org/news/fda-reinstates-nmn-as-dietary-supplement-after-npa-lawsuit/
- 逐字引:> Daniel Fabricant (NPA CEO): "FDA's recent letters represent a massive U-turn, and we're proud that we were able to help shape the outcome."
- 利益相关:NPA is the petitioner and plaintiff; its members sell NMN. Self-reported victory announcement.

### L7-03 **[承重]**

The regulatory chain that removed NMN from US shelves in 2022 ran through parties with direct commercial stakes on both sides. The IND that triggered drug preclusion belonged to Metro International Biotech LLC (drug candidate MIB-626, a proprietary crystalline β-NMN); David Sinclair co-founded the company and headed its Scientific Advisory Board. ChromaDex — which sells the competing NAD precursor nicotinamide riboside as Tru Niagen — publicised FDA's conclusion in a press release on 11 November 2022 and then filed with FDA on 9 January 2024 supporting the exclusion.

- 来源类型:media-secondhand
- 出处:SupplySide SJ (Josh Long), 'ChromaDex sides with FDA, pharma in NMN supplements debate'; ChromaDex Businesswire release 11 Nov 2022
- URL:https://www.supplysidesj.com/healthy-aging/chromadex-agrees-with-fda-on-nmn-decision
- 逐字引:> ChromaDex called it "the correct" determination that NMN does not meet the definition of a dietary supplement, said "the pursuit of NMN as a drug candidate was overtly evident based on publicly available information", called industry claims of ignorance "patently unfounded", and described its own NR as a "superior" alternative to NMN.
- 利益相关:ChromaDex sells NR (Tru Niagen), NMN's direct competitor, and benefited commercially from NMN's removal. Sinclair holds equity/SAB roles at Metro International Biotech while being the field's most visible public NMN advocate.

### L7-04 

FDA's reversal left the underlying drug-preclusion framework intact. CRN's five stated objections to the 29 Sep 2025 decision: (1) FDA still uses confidential IND effective dates as the trigger, so industry cannot know when the clock started; (2) 'substantial clinical investigations' remains undefined, so even early-phase or limited studies can preclude an ingredient; (3) FDA continues to reject foreign prior-marketing evidence, counting only US marketing; (4) FDA declined to issue interpretive guidance or initiate rulemaking, citing limited resources; (5) the structural problem is untouched.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:Council for Responsible Nutrition, 'CRN Dismayed by FDA's Response to its Citizen Petition on Drug Preclusion', 2025
- URL:https://www.crnusa.org/newsroom/crn-dismayed-fdas-response-its-citizen-petition-drug-preclusion
- 逐字引:> Steve Mister, CRN President & CEO: "FDA missed a pivotal opportunity to bring clarity and balance to the line between pharmaceuticals and dietary supplements." Megan Olsen, CRN General Counsel: "The same unstable framework remains in place for the next ingredient, inviting repeat uncertainty."
- 利益相关:CRN is a supplement-industry trade association and was a petitioner; its members' commercial interest is in a narrow drug-preclusion clause.

### L7-05 

Litigation timeline: NPA filed suit against FDA in the US District Court for the District of Columbia on 28 August 2024 seeking a declaration that NMN is not excluded from the dietary supplement definition. Judge Paul L. Friedman stayed proceedings in October 2024 pending FDA's answer to the citizen petition. After the 29 Sep 2025 response, NPA filed a notice of voluntary dismissal without prejudice. Commercially, Amazon delisted NMN dietary supplements at the start of 2023 and NMN products were back on Amazon by October 2025 — the platform, not the agency, was the effective enforcement mechanism during the interregnum.

- 来源类型:media-secondhand
- 出处:SupplySide SJ and NutraIngredients coverage, 2024–2025; NutraIngredients 'NMN back on Amazon but FDA's citizen petition response seen as a mixed bag for supplements', 8 October 2025
- URL:https://www.nutraingredients.com/Article/2025/10/08/nmn-back-on-amazon-but-fdas-citizen-petition-response-seen-as-a-mixed-bag-for-supplements/
- 逐字引:> Scott Bass (Sidley Austin) called FDA's use of "substantial" in evaluating drug investigations "100% wrong" while praising the letter as "a major shift in their approach to DSHEA issues." Rend Al-Mondhiry: FDA's interpretation "seems to broaden application of the drug preclusion clause" — "a mixed bag in terms of what FDA's response means moving forward."
- 利益相关:Both quoted lawyers represent supplement-industry clients. Case number not obtained.

### L7-06 **[承重]**

NAD+/NAD-precursor supplement market size estimates disagree by roughly 5x for the same year, and every figure is produced by a firm selling the report. Future Market Insights: USD 876.2 million in 2025, growing to USD 1,581.1 million by 2035, CAGR 6.1%, scope = NAD+ boosting finished supplements (NMN, NR, nicotinamide, tryptophan, blends). Global Market Insights: USD 488.17 million in 2021 to USD 1,451.19 million by 2030, CAGR 13%, scope = NAD by grade (food/pharma) across applications including anti-aging; report price 'Starting at: $2,450'. market.us: ~USD 3.9 billion in 2025, CAGR 14.2% (2026–2035). Other circulating 2025 figures: USD 795.9 million and USD 1.42 billion. CAGR spread across publishers: 6.1% to 18.2%. Methodologies are unauditable — GMI describes 'a bottom-up approach combining primary interviews (contributing ~80% of insights)'; FMI describes 'scenario-based forecasting (optimistic/likely/conservative)'. Neither publishes an underlying sales panel.

### L7-07 

Longevity-clinic market estimates show the same dispersion. The Business Research Company: USD 5.35 billion (2025) → 6.02 billion (2026) → 9.55 billion (2030), CAGR 12.2%; report price USD 4,490 single-user; scope defined as 'a specialized healthcare facility dedicated to extending healthy lifespan by emphasizing preventive, personalized, and proactive medical care'. A competing report gives USD 4.20 billion (2025) → USD 18.91 billion (2034) at 18.20% CAGR — a 2x difference in terminal value driven entirely by assumed growth rate. Clinic counts are equally soft: a market report asserts 'more than 700 dedicated longevity clinics operate globally' (April 2026), while the only actual field survey found covered 82 clinics.

### L7-08 **[承重]**

The only field survey of longevity clinics located covered 82 clinics across the US, Australia, Brazil, and multiple countries in Europe and Asia, conducted by Longevity.Technology and published as its 'longevity clinics survey 2025'. Over one third of surveyed clinics offered stem-cell treatments, for which there is no evidence of lifespan benefit. Most surveyed clinics prescribe approved medicines off-label for aging.

- 来源类型:media-secondhand

- 利益相关:The survey was conducted and published by Longevity.Technology, a longevity-industry trade publisher that sells market reports and conference access — i.e. the only census of the industry is produced by a participant in it.

### L7-09 **[承重]**

Published academic critique of longevity clinics is thin: the strongest item found is a single-author editorial, not a systematic study. Marco Demaria, 'Longevity clinics: between promise and peril', Aging (Albany NY), 13 October 2025. It gives no clinic counts or percentages. No formal position statement from the AMA, American Geriatrics Society, Gerontological Society of America, AFAR or ACP on longevity clinics was located.

- 来源类型:peer-reviewed
- 出处:Demaria M. Longevity clinics: between promise and peril. Aging (Albany NY). 13 October 2025. PMC12606959
- URL:https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12606959/

Stem-cell infusions or experimental biologics are sometimes offered without robust safety data." … "Several tools used, such as biological age tests based on epigenetics or telomeres, are presented to clients as definitive scores, but their precision and clinical utility are still under debate." … "Many clinics position themselves as wellness providers rather than medical facilities, so that they escape rigorous oversight." … "CONFLICTS OF INTEREST: The author declares no conflicts of interest related to this study."
- 利益相关:None declared by the author. Note the venue, Aging (Albany NY), publishes extensively in the geroscience commercial ecosystem.

### L7-10 **[承重]**

Under DSHEA (FD&C Act §403(r)(6); 21 CFR 101.93), a dietary supplement may make structure/function claims without premarket FDA review provided it notifies FDA and carries the statutory disclaimer; it may not make disease claims (diagnose, treat, cure, mitigate, prevent), which convert the product into an unapproved new drug. Because aging is not a recognised disease indication, 'supports healthy aging' sits in permitted structure/function space while 'reverses Alzheimer's' does not. Consequence documented below: FDA enforcement in this sector lands on disease claims and on manufacturing/labeling — not on longevity claims as such. The disclaimer as it actually appears in the wild, fetched from Blueprint's own storefront 2026-07-28.

- 来源类型:vendor-selfreport
- 出处:blueprint.bryanjohnson.com homepage, fetched 28 July 2026 (disclaimer text); statutory framework per FD&C Act §403(r)(6) and 21 CFR 101.93
- URL:https://blueprint.bryanjohnson.com/
- 逐字引:> "These statements have not been evaluated by the Food and Drug Administration. This product is not intended to diagnose, treat, cure, or prevent any disease."
- 利益相关:Vendor page. NOTE: I could not fetch eCFR for 21 CFR 101.93 (redirected to unblock.federalregister.gov) — the statutory text is unverified in-session and should be checked against eCFR before publication.

### L7-11 **[承重]**

Concrete FDA warning/advisory letters citing anti-aging products — all triggered by DISEASE claims, not by longevity claims: (1) Aardsma Research & Publishing, online advisory letter, July 2025, product 'Dr. Aardsma's Anti-Aging Vitamins'; FDA cited website statements recommending the vitamins to treat or prevent prostate cancer, non-Hodgkin's lymphoma, thrombocytosis, macular degeneration and hypertension. (2) Bonagens, warning letter MARCS-CMS 609905, 17 November 2020, products including 'Anti-Aging Supports'; cited claims that products would 'alleviate the effects of menopausal syndrome', 'prevent and aid in rehabilitation from strokes', 'alleviate cataract problems'. (3) Sovereign Laboratories LLC, warning letter 566227, 5 February 2019, cited web content 'Anti-Aging Benefits of Bovine Colostrum' plus disease claims. (4) Time Challenger Labs International Inc., warning letter 706900, 18 September 2025 — resveratrol product found misbranded under supplement labeling requirements (labeling, not an aging claim).

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:US FDA warning/advisory letters: Aardsma Research & Publishing (July 2025); Bonagens 609905 (17 Nov 2020); Sovereign Laboratories LLC 566227 (5 Feb 2019); Time Challenger Labs International Inc. 706900 (18 Sep 2025)
- URL:https://www.fda.gov/inspections-compliance-enforcement-and-criminal-investigations/aardsma-research-publishing-online-advisory-letter
- 逐字引:> From the Aardsma letter as surfaced from fda.gov: the website recommended the use of these vitamins to treat or prevent "Prostate cancer, nonHodgkin's lymphoma, Thrombocytosis, Macular Degeneration, and Hypertension". From the Bonagens letter: claims that products would "alleviate the effects of menopausal syndrome," "prevent and aid in rehabilitation from strokes," and "alleviate cataract problems". VERIFICATION CAVEAT: every direct WebFetch of a www.fda.gov URL returned 403/404 from this environment; these quotes come from fda.gov page content surfaced via domain-restricted search, not from a page I rendered.

### L7-12 **[承重]**

The sharpest safety event in the commercial NAD+ layer is injectable, not oral. GenoGenix LLC (Boca Raton, FL) NAD+ for Injection, 100 mg/mL and 200 mg/mL in 10 mL multi-dose amber vials, Lot GG121624-023: voluntary recall initiated 30 July 2025, classified Class I by FDA on 21 October 2025. An unopened vial from that lot assayed at 3,360 EU/mL bacterial endotoxin. Three patients who received product from the same vial developed low blood pressure, uncontrollable shaking/shivering and body aches and were directed to the emergency room. A second GenoGenix recall was classified Class II (sterility). FDA received a Form 483 for GenoGenix dated 18 July 2025 and issued warning letter 718739 on 20 January 2026. Context: FDA has warned that some compounders use food-grade NAD+ intended for oral supplements to make sterile IV injections.

- 来源类型:media-secondhand
- 出处:FDA recall classification reporting via HMP Global Learning Network, 'FDA Issues Class I Recall for NAD+ Injection Due to Elevated Endotoxin Levels'; FDA warning letter GenoGenix LLC 718739 (20 Jan 2026); FDA Form 483 GenoGenix LLC (18 Jul 2025)
- URL:https://www.hmpgloballearningnetwork.com/site/pln/news/fda-issues-class-i-recall-nad-injection-due-elevated-endotoxin-levels
- 逐字引:> Recall initiated 30 July 2025; classified Class I on 21 October 2025. Unopened vial from Lot# GG121624-023 measured 3,360 EU/mL bacterial endotoxin. Three patients who received NAD+ Lot# GG121624-023 from the same vial developed low blood pressure, uncontrollable shaking, shivers and body aches and were directed to the emergency room. [FDA source pages not directly fetchable — verify.]

### L7-13 **[承重]**

The most consequential 2026 claims ruling in this sector: on 21 May 2026 a National Advertising Review Board (NARB) panel, case #7487-346, recommended that Niagen Bioscience Inc. (formerly ChromaDex) discontinue or modify multiple Tru Niagen (nicotinamide riboside) claims — the 'clinically proven to significantly increase NAD+ levels' establishment claim, heart/brain/immune health claims, anti-aging claims and testimonials depicting real-world improvement, and muscle-health/energy claims promising functional recovery or vitality. The underlying NAD decision (case #7487, 16 March 2026) was brought by competitor Reus Research LLC. The panel's holding is the general statement of the tier-1→tier-3 promotion problem: substantiation is judged by the message consumers receive, not by the advertiser's regulatory classification, and biomarker/mechanistic evidence does not substantiate perceptible functional benefits.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:BBB National Programs, National Advertising Review Board, Niagen Bioscience Inc., Case #7487-346, decision 21 May 2026; underlying NAD Case #7487, decision 16 March 2026 (challenger: Reus Research LLC)
- URL:https://bbbprograms.org/media/newsroom/decisions/niagen-bioscience
- 逐字引:> The panel determined that the clinical evidence "did not support the claim asserting that specified increases in NAD+ levels following use of Tru Niagen had been clinically proven for the general population." Claims "went beyond cell-level mechanistic effects to promise perceptible or functional health benefits that were not properly supported." The governing principle: "the required substantiation depends on the message conveyed to consumers, not the advertiser's regulatory classification." Niagen advertiser statement: "deeply disappointed with the NARB Panel's decision but will nevertheless comply."
- 利益相关:The challenge was brought by a direct competitor (Reus Research LLC), not a consumer body; NAD itself ruled against Reus's own 'Cata Kor' NAD claims on 13 Oct 2025. NARB is industry self-regulation with no statutory enforcement power. Niagen warned the decision 'has a chilling effect on innovation and may actively discourage future investments in human clinical trials.'

### L7-14 

Government consumer-protection enforcement specifically against ANTI-AGING claims is old and rare. The two clearest cases: (1) FTC v. Telomerase Activation Sciences Inc. and Noel Thomas Patton (TA Sciences), complaint February 2018, final consent order approved 19 April 2018 (matter 142-3103, docket C-4644), covering TA-65MD capsules/powder and TA-65 for Skin; FTC alleged the company lacked competent and reliable scientific evidence for claims that the products prevent and repair DNA damage, restore aging immune systems, increase bone density, reverse effects of aging skin and eyes, and prevent or reduce cancer risk; the order also barred presenting paid advertising as independent programming and failing to disclose material connections with endorsers. (2) FTC settlements announced 5 February 2020 with Quantum Wellness Botanical Institute LLC, CEO Fred Auzene and marketer Maria Gutierrez Veloso over the 'ReJuvenation' pill — judgments totalling roughly $3.4 million with $660,000 paid for consumer redress; ads claimed the pill reversed aging and treated heart-attack damage, heart disease, blindness, brain damage from stroke, Alzheimer's, Parkinson's and deafness.

- 来源类型:official-primary
- 出处:FTC, 'FTC Approves Final Consent Order in Telomerase Activation Sciences Deceptive Advertising Case', 19 April 2018 (matter 142-3103, C-4644); FTC settlements re Quantum Wellness Botanical Institute LLC / ReJuvenation, announced 5 February 2020
- URL:https://www.ftc.gov/news-events/news/press-releases/2018/04/ftc-approves-final-consent-order-telomerase-activation-sciences-deceptive-advertising-case
- 逐字引:> FTC order prohibits TA Sciences from making any representation about the health benefits, performance, efficacy, safety, or side effects of any covered product, including TA-65MD and TA-65 Skin, "unless it is not misleading and is supported by competent and reliable scientific evidence." [ftc.gov returned 403 on direct fetch; text via FTC page content surfaced in search — verify.]

A private class action alleges Niagen Bioscience Inc. and ChromaDex Inc. marketed Tru Niagen NR supplements as providing anti-aging, cellular repair and organ health benefits by raising NAD+, and 'overstated and misrepresented clinical findings' to charge a price premium for what the complaint calls 'snake oil' supplements. Court, case number and filing date were not verified in this session — treat as unconfirmed pending retrieval of the complaint.
